Instacart Help Guide

Instacart’s official customer help, gathered into one screen-reader-friendly document

2026-07-29

Instacart Help Guide

This guide gathers Instacart’s official help for customers: how the service works, placing and tracking orders, delivery, pricing and fees, payments and gift cards, EBT SNAP, your profile and account settings, Instacart Plus, and Instacart Business. It was taken from Instacart’s own help centre on 28 July 2026.

Instacart runs on the web and as a phone app. There is no Windows program and no Alexa skill, so the usual four-interface scope narrows to those two.

The sections below are Instacart’s own categories, in the order the help centre lists them, and each article sits where Instacart files it. Help for shoppers — the people who do the shopping — is a separate estate for a different audience and is not included here. Contact forms and the virtual assistant are left out too: they hold nothing to read.

Instacart serves some pages at more than one address, so where the same article appeared twice the fullest copy was kept.

Headings run three deep: this title, then a category, then an article, with each article’s own subheadings nested beneath. Every article is listed in the table of contents below.

Instacart Plus

Connecting Instacart+ and retailer accounts

If you can link a retailer account to your Instacart+ account, the option appears in your account settings—

  1. Log into your account on the retailer’s website.
  2. Click Account settings.
  3. Under Instacart+, click Connect your existing Instacart+ account.
  4. If you already have an Instacart account, enter your login credentials.
  5. Under Authorization Required, click Allow.
  6. If you want to link a different Instacart+ account instead, click Switch Accounts.
  7. The site redirects you to your retailer account page with a success message once it links.

Instacart+ and Peacock promotion

Instacart+ members in the United States can receive a Peacock Premium subscription at no additional cost. The Peacock Premium subscription is Peacock’s ad-supported tier.

Qualifying for the offer

You qualify if you:

  • Have a current Instacart+ membership that includes the Peacock benefit.
    • You don’t qualify if you’re an additional user receiving Instacart+ benefits through a family account.
    • If you have Instacart+ through an Instacart Business account, you must activate this offer in your Instacart Business account.
  • Redeem through the Instacart app or Instacart.com.
  • Aren’t a current Peacock subscriber.

Instacart+ trial members

If you’re not already an Instacart+ member, you may be eligible for a 14-day or 30-day trial. Eligible trial members can receive the Peacock Premium subscription during their Instacart+ trial period. Instacart+ trial members may be subject to a daily redemption limit and must have a valid payment method on file prior to activating the Peacock benefit.

If you sign up for a monthly or annual membership after the trial period, you’ll continue receiving Peacock Premium.

Activate the offer

To activate your Peacock Premium subscription in your Instacart account:

  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner (website) or the Account icon in the upper right corner (app).

  2. Select Your Instacart+ Membership on the left (website) or Settings > Your Instacart+ membership (app).

  3. Under Unlock special offers, select Activate under the Peacock offer.

  4. Select Activate Peacock now.

  5. Peacock’s website will open. You can sign in or create an account to activate your offer.

You don’t need to use the same email address associated with your Instacart account when signing up for Peacock.

For the full terms, refer to the Peacock benefit terms and conditions.

Instacart+ benefits

For a flat annual cost or a low monthly fee, you can enjoy $0 delivery fees on eligible orders of $10 or more. Service and other fees, taxes, and tips may apply. Excludes Costco, select locations, prescription medication orders, pick up orders, and restaurants powered by UberEats.

Additional Instacart+ benefits

Instacart also offers additional benefits exclusive for Instacart+ members, including:

  • Peacock (U.S. only): Eligible Instacart+ members receive a Peacock Premium subscription at no additional cost.
  • New York Times Cooking (annual Instacart+ members only): Receive a one year free subscription to New York Times Cooking.
  • Instacart+ partnership promotions: Instacart partners with other companies to provide exclusive Instacart+ promotions and other benefits.
  • Limited time offers include:
    • Eligible Instacart+ customers could receive $10 off your first restaurant order of $25 or more through the Instacart app if you are an active member when you receive the promotion.
    • Spend $40 or more on a non-restaurant order via Instacart and get a coupon for $10 off, on a qualifying restaurant order of $25 or more placed via the Instacart app.

For full terms and conditions for each additional Instacart+ benefit, refer to the following pages:

  • Peacock benefit terms and conditions
  • Instacart+ and New York Times Cooking subscription benefit terms
Additional Instacart+ benefits for Instacart Business Accounts

Businesses can share Instacart+ benefits with up to four additional members through Shared Instacart+ (benefits shared may vary). This includes a 2% Credit Back on eligible orders over $250.

Refer to the following pages for the full terms and conditions:

  • Instacart Business Shared Instacart+ Terms and Conditions
  • Instacart+ promotional terms and conditions; terms for credit back offers

This membership does not give you faster delivery or earlier time slots than other customers. Likewise, membership with Instacart+ does not guarantee that specific delivery slots will be available for a given delivery. For full terms and conditions, please visit the Instacart+ Terms and Conditions. Instacart may in its sole discretion end additional benefits, including the $10 order minimum offer on $0 delivery fees, immediately if a regulation, law, or other issue impacts the ability of Instacart to offer the benefit.**

Instacart+ partnership promotions

Instacart partners with other companies to provide exclusive Instacart+ promotions and other benefits.

To find the latest partner offers for Instacart+ members—

  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner (website) or the Account icon in the upper right corner (app).
  2. Select Your Instacart+ Membership (website) or Settings > Your Instacart membership (app).
  3. View the Unlock special offers section.
  4. Select the offer.

Offer terms are available in the Terms of Promos, Credits, and Gift Cards section of our Help Center. You can also check out the following Help Center articles to learn more about our current offers—

  • EBT SNAP and Instacart+ promotion
  • Instacart+ and Costco partnership
  • Instacart+ and Mastercard promotion
  • Instacart+ and PayPal promotion

Manage your Instacart+ membership

Instacart+ members can update their auto-renew settings, payment method, plan type, and set membership reminders on our website or app.

Manage your membership

For existing Instacart+ members—

  1. On the website, select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner (website) or the Account icon in the upper right corner (app).
  2. Select Your Instacart+ membership.
  3. Under Manage your membership, select Renew membership and choose Annual plan or Monthly plan.

For trial members—

  1. On the website, select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner (website) or the Account icon in the upper right corner (app).
  2. Select Your Instacart+ membership.
  3. Under Manage your membership, select Renew membership and choose Annual plan or Monthly plan.
Membership charges

At the end of your trial period, you’ll be charged a membership fee if your membership auto-enrolls into a paid membership plan. You can view your membership details and cancel your trial before it ends in your account settings. Charges may be applied up to 6 hours prior to renewal to a paid plan but are refundable per the Instacart+ Terms.

If you want to cancel your membership after you were charged membership fees—

  1. On the website, select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner (website) or the Account icon in the upper right corner (app).
  2. Select Your Instacart+ membership.
  3. Under Manage your membership, select Cancel membership.
Update your payment method
  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner (website) or the Account icon in the upper right corner (app).
  2. Select Your Instacart+ membership.
  3. Under Preferred payment method, select Change.
  4. Choose a saved payment method or add a new one.
  5. Enter the payment information and select Confirm payment method.

You can also use PayPal or Klarna to sign up for an Instacart+ membership or change an existing membership.

For help with PayPal, Klarna, or adding a payment method, go to Updating your payment methods.

Cancel your membership
  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner (website) or the Account icon in the upper right corner (app).
  2. Select Your Instacart+ membership.
  3. Under Manage your membership, select Cancel membership.
    • If you change your mind, you can also set up a reminder sent for 7 days before the membership renews.
    • On the Instacart+ page, you can see when your membership expires.
  4. Select Continue to cancel.
  5. Select Confirm cancellation.
  6. You’ll then see a confirmation message that you canceled your membership. Select Done.

You can return to this page and select Renew membership at any time to become an Instacart+ member again.

You’ll get an email confirming your cancellation. If you can’t find this email, check your spam folder.

For annual members, you may cancel your Instacart+ membership within the first 5 calendar days of your paid Instacart+ membership and get a refund of the Instacart+ membership fee you paid, but only if you haven’t placed any orders using your Instacart+ membership.

If you cancel at any other time, you won’t get a refund, but you can continue enjoying the $0 delivery fees through the end of your billing period.

Pickup credit back

From time to time, Instacart may offer credit back on eligible pickup orders from participating retailers to select customers. If this limited time benefit is available, it will appear when you place an eligible pickup order.

The credit is automatically added to your Instacart account, and you can use it on any future pickup or delivery order.

Credits awarded to your account will expire twelve (12) months after the date you placed the order on which the credits were awarded.

Restrictions

Some restrictions apply when earning this credit—

  • Earn credit back on pickup orders only.
  • The credit back is only earned on your subtotal, and doesn’t include tips, taxes, and fees.
  • You can’t earn credit on alcohol or prescription items.
  • You can’t earn credit back on the value of any item in excess of $150 or above.

There are also some restrictions when spending this earned credit—

  • You can’t use credit to purchase alcohol or prescription items.

Instacart may update or modify these restrictions in the future.

Using your credits

Credits automatically apply to your next pickup or delivery order at checkout.

You can confirm your credit applied at checkout on the Place order page.

  1. Scroll down to Apply credits & promotions.
  2. Here you’ll see a checked green box with the credit amount listed that applies to your order.
View earned credit on order receipt

To see the estimated credit back you could receive after placing your pickup order—

  1. At checkout, look under the Instacart+ Savings section.
  2. Look for the Estimated credit back line item.
    • Here you’ll see how much credit back you could receive from your order.

Note: If you didn’t receive as much credit back as originally expected, your order may have contained items listed within the restrictions above.

Once a shopper has completed your order, the final credit amount may change due to—

  • Item out of stock.
  • Replacement item price is different from requested item price.
  • Issue reported on an item after picking up.

To see the actual credit back you received after your pickup order is complete—

  1. Log in to your Instacart account.
  2. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left-hand corner.
  3. Select Your orders.
  4. Select View order detail next to the order receipt you want to view.
  5. Select View receipt.
  6. Scroll to the bottom of the receipt and look for Credit back from order with a yellow bolt icon next to it.
    • Here you’ll see how much credit back you received for your order.
View credits applied on order

You can see Instacart+ credits applied to an order at checkout and on the order receipt under subtotal—

  1. At checkout, look under the Payment Method section.
  2. You’ll see an Apply Instacart credits checkbox.
    • This box is automatically checked for you but you can uncheck if you don’t want to save the credits for another order.
  3. The line item under the subtotal shows the amount of Instacart credits that applied to your order.
View credits in Instacart account

You can see Instacart credits and their expiration dates in your Instacart account—

  1. Log in to your Instacart account.
  2. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left-hand corner.
  3. Select Credits, promos, and gift cards.
  4. Under Instacart+ and gift card credits you’ll see how much credit you have available.
  5. Select View credit history to see credit balance adjustments.
Terms and conditions

For the full terms and conditions, visit our Instacart+ promotional terms and conditions; terms for credit back offers Help Center article.

Sign up for Instacart+

You don’t need a membership to shop with Instacart. However, Instacart+ is a membership option for customers who want to save more and get other exclusive benefits.

You can sign up for an Instacart+ membership in the app or on the website.

  1. Tap the Account icon (in-app) or the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left-hand corner (web) > Your Instacart+ Membership.
  2. Choose your preferred plan (annual/monthly).
  3. Select Choose Annual/Monthly Plan.
Instacart+ Pricing

To receive extra savings on each order, you can pay either monthly or annually for an Instacart+ membership. You can also sign up for a free trial if you’re new to Instacart+ or qualify for a free membership through one of our partner offers.

Plan pricing information is available when you choose your plan.

  • Annual memberships are billed each year on the same date.
  • Monthly memberships are charged on the same day every month.

Note: You can’t use Instacart gift cards to pay for Instacart+ unless you’re using an Instacart+ membership gift card. For more information, check out our Purchase a gift card Help Center article.

This membership does not give you faster delivery or earlier time slots than other customers. Likewise, membership with Instacart+ does not guarantee that specific delivery slots will be available for a given delivery. Service fees may apply. For full terms and conditions, please visit the Instacart+ Terms and Conditions.

Payments and Gift Cards

Add your loyalty card to your mobile wallet

Use this article to save your store loyalty card to your phone’s wallet app. This lets you access your loyalty card barcode at checkout without opening the Instacart app. This feature works for both new cards and updates to existing cards.

Before you start

Make sure you have:

  • Your loyalty card already linked to your Instacart account
  • The latest version of the Instacart app installed
  • Apple Wallet (iOS) or Google Pay (Android) set up on your device

In the app—

  1. Tap the Account icon.
  2. Under Savings, tap Add Loyalty Cards.
  3. Tap the loyalty card you want to add to your wallet.
  4. Scroll down below the barcode and tap Add to Wallet.
  5. Follow the prompts to save the card to Apple Wallet or Google Pay.

Your loyalty card barcode will now appear in your phone’s wallet app, letting you show it at checkout without opening the Instacart app. Your points and rewards will still track normally when you scan the barcode from your wallet.

Important: The barcode in your wallet is the same as the one in your Instacart app. Both work the same way at checkout.

Troubleshooting

I tapped “Add to Wallet” but nothing happened

  • Check that Apple Wallet or Google Pay is set up on your device.
  • Make sure your device’s operating system is up to date.
  • Close and reopen the Instacart app, then try again.

The barcode in my wallet looks different

  • This is normal. The barcode format may look different in your wallet app, but it contains the same loyalty card information and will scan correctly at checkout.

I already added my loyalty card to my wallet before—what happens if I use “Add to Wallet” again?

  • Your existing wallet pass will be updated automatically. You won’t get a duplicate card in your wallet. The system recognizes that you already have this card saved and will refresh it with the latest information. You can safely tap Add to Wallet anytime to keep your pass current.

Buy now pay later

Buy now pay later lets you divide your total purchase amount into smaller payments. Instacart offers two options: Klarna and PayPal Pay Later. This feature isn’t available on Groceries Powered by Instacart.

Klarna

Klarna lets you split your order into 4 interest-free payments, collected every 2 weeks from the debit or credit card on file until the purchase is paid in full.

How Klarna payments work

  • There are no fees when you pay on time.
  • Your first Klarna payment is based on the authorization total divided by 4 and is due at checkout.
  • The remaining 3 payments are adjusted based on the final total of your order minus the first payment.
  • For refunded items, Klarna updates the remaining balance to reflect the new refunded amount.

Klarna limitations

You can add, replace, or refund items after placing your order. Additions that exceed the original authorized amount have separate authorizations and must be more than $10 to qualify for Pay in 4.

  • While Klarna is available for alcohol purchases, it can’t be used at alcohol-only retailers.
  • Klarna can’t be used to purchase prescriptions.
  • Klarna may not be available for purchases at certain retailers.
  • Klarna may not be available for all payment methods.
  • You can’t move charges to a different payment method after placing your order.
  • Klarna orders can’t be used with EBT SNAP.

Check out with Klarna

  1. Select Klarna as a payment method at checkout.
  2. Select Pay With Klarna.
  3. You’ll be directed to Klarna’s site to confirm your information, add a payment method, and verify your payment plan.

Klarna payments default to your original selection, Pay now, Pay in 4, or Pay Later X. To change your default, remove Klarna from your Saved payment methods and re-add it with your new selection.

Payments and balance inquiries

Klarna places a temporary authorization hold on your bank account after you complete a purchase. It can take up to 24 hours for a hold to lift after a failed or canceled purchase.

Depending on your payment method and bank, it may take more time to reflect in your account.

For questions about payments, balance inquiries, or rejected purchases: go to the Klarna app, visit klarna.com/us/customer-service, or call (844) 552-7621.

PayPal Pay Later

PayPal Pay Later lets you divide your total purchase amount into smaller payments using your PayPal account. There are two different plans you can choose from, depending on how much your order costs.

Available plans

  • Pay in 4 — Split your cost into 4 payments with no interest. This works for orders from $10 to $2,000.
  • Pay Monthly — A longer plan for bigger orders. This works for orders from $199 to $10,000.

Check out with PayPal Pay Later

  1. Pick PayPal Pay Later when you are ready to pay. A window will not pop up yet.
  2. Add your tip and check your order details. Then tap Place Order.
  3. A PayPal window will open showing the total cost, including the tip. Pick your plan and approve it.
  4. Your order is confirmed. If the final price is different from what you approved, PayPal will email you.

ℹ️ You must approve PayPal Pay Later for every single order, even if you saved it as a payment method.

PayPal Pay Later limitations

  • You cannot use this for orders with alcohol or prescriptions.
  • Orders must be at least $10 to use Pay in 4.
  • After you place your order, you can only add items if the new total stays under your original PayPal approval limit. If adding an item makes the total go over that limit, you cannot add it.
  • If you want to increase your tip after placing an order, you cannot do it if the new tip makes the total go over your approval limit.
  • You can use this with Instacart Credits, EBT SNAP, or Chase Pay With Points. You cannot combine it with other payment methods.

Payments and balance inquiries

If you need help with your PayPal login, you can contact PayPal support online at https://www.paypal.com/us/smarthelp/contact-us, call (888) 221-1161, or use the PayPal app.

Fresh Funds

Fresh Funds is an Instacart program that allows providers to fund category-specific stipends, like fresh and frozen produce and household goods. In some cases, Fresh Funds covers everything on Instacart except alcohol. Providers determine what items and fees are eligible. The providers distribute Fresh Funds to recipients who can redeem via a digital code and shop from eligible categories on Instacart.

Nonprofits, insurance companies, hospital systems, employers, etc. can use Instacart Fresh Funds to bring existing food prescription and nutrition incentive programs online or launch easily scalable health programs.

Fresh Funds requirements

You must meet the following requirements for your Fresh Funds to apply to your order—

  • Your cart must contain at least 1 Fresh Funds item from approved categories to apply Fresh Fund credits. Items eligible for Fresh Funds have a Fresh Funds tag under the item.
  • You must add a backup credit/debit card in the Pay With section at checkout for the Fresh Funds checkbox to activate. The backup credit/debit card pays for any uncovered charges. Backup payment methods can’t be EBT SNAP or other benefit cards.
  • You can use multiple Fresh Funds of the same value and from the same sponsor together (ex. 2 $50 codes) to maximize benefits, but can’t combine different values (ex. a $30 and $20 code). When you have two codes of different value, only the first code added to the account will apply, the second code may be used on a new order when the first is fully spent. You can see the final order total in the Pay With section at checkout.
  • Minimum order requirements and fees vary by sponsor. Some programs may include promotions that auto apply to cover service or delivery fees.
  • Some Fresh Funds programs include Instacart+ memberships. These memberships don’t auto-renew.
Using Fresh Funds

Fresh Funds apply to the item and fee categories selected by the sponsor. Most sponsors allow Fresh Funds to cover standard service and delivery fees.

Add a Fresh Funds code from sponsor—

  1. Open the communication (ex. email) from your benefit provider.
  2. Click the link or use the QR code provided by your sponsor. Your unique digital code auto-populates in the form field.
    • You can’t activate Fresh Funds codes in the credits and promos section of your Instacart account.
  3. Once activated, you can log into your Instacart account or create a free account—it takes only a few minutes.

Backup payment method—

Ineligible items and fees will be charged to your backup payment method. You must add a credit or debit card at checkout to use your Fresh Funds, even if the Fresh Funds fully cover your total purchase amount. Your credit or debit card will be used as a backup payment method in the event post-checkout changes cause your total to exceed your available Fresh Funds.

The credit/debit card covers—

  • The authorization hold. Go to Authorization holds, recurring payments, and unknown charges to learn more.
  • Ineligible items, post-order adjustments (including item additions and tips), fees, and any amount that exceeds your Fresh Funds balance charges to your backup payment method.
  • Substitutions that exceed the value of the original item will also be charged to the backup payment method.

Apply Fresh Funds to an order—

Make sure you have a non-EBT credit/debit card added to your account. You can use EBT SNAP with Fresh Funds, but you must add a non-EBT credit/debit card to the account to successfully use Fresh Funds credits. EBT always deducts first (you can modify the amount), followed by Fresh Funds credits. Go to Updating your payment methods for more information on adding, removing, or updating your payment method.

  1. Select the Fresh Funds credits box in the Pay With section.
  2. The total Fresh Funds amount applied to the order appears at the bottom of the checkout screen.

Check your balance—

On the website

  1. Go to Instacart.com
  2. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the top left-hand corner
  3. Click Credits
  4. Click View balances to see your balance totals and breakdowns

In the app

  1. Tap Account at the bottom of your screen
  2. Tap Credits
  3. Select View balances to see your balance totals and breakdowns. If you don’t have a credit balance, View balances won’t appear.

Fresh Funds expire. Please review your organization’s Fresh Funds terms for more information.

Eligible items

Each benefits provider determines Fresh Funds eligibility. Your Fresh Funds cover the preselected food categories, like fresh and frozen produce, plus any fees and/or tips selected by your benefit provider.

Orders with non-Fresh Fund-eligible items and orders that exceed the designated Fresh Funds amount can be purchased with an alternate form of payment. You can add other payment methods, like a gift card, credit card, or debit card, at checkout.

Leaving a Fresh Funds program

Once you leave a Fresh Funds program, you’re separated from the program, but you can choose to rejoin at a later date. You lose access to Fresh Funds credits and other program benefits, including any unused credits in your account. Instacart stops sharing order information with your Fresh Funds partner after processing your request.

Trying to turn off promotional emails? Here’s how.

How to leave Fresh Funds

  • You must complete any active order(s) before requesting to leave a Fresh Funds program.
  • When you’re ready to leave the Fresh Funds program, contact Instacart Customer Experience by selecting the Get help button below. They’ll need the name of your sponsor, your Fresh Funds code, and your email address to process the request.
  • Requests typically process within 30 business days.
Authorization holds

Instacart places a temporary authorization hold on your credit or debit card to account for potential changes to the final total. Authorization hold amounts vary, but are typically calculated as the total value of any ineligible amounts or amounts exceeding your Fresh Funds balance, plus about 4% of the order total. The hold accounts for potential changes in the final total due to added items, items replaced at a different price, and the actual weight of items. You can see the amount of the authorization hold below your order totals at checkout, prior to placing your order.

The hold is released after your order is delivered or canceled. It can then take up to 7 days for the pending charge to drop, depending on your bank.

We will only charge you for the total of the items delivered to you, plus any fees, taxes, and tips. For more information on authorization holds, click here.

Partnering with Fresh Funds

Visit Instacart Health if your organization is interested in partnering with us.

–

Need help with your code?

If your code is invalid, that means the code was already redeemed or deactivated. Please reach out to your benefit provider with your Instacart account email, Fresh Funds code, and a description of the problem for help. Screenshots are also helpful.

  • 1% cash back on all other purchases
  • Unlimited earnings

Cash back on Instacart exclusions—

Additional benefits—

  • Purchase Protection

  • Baggage Delay Insurance
  • Lost Luggage Reimbursement
  • Roadside Assistance

  • Travel and Emergency Assistance Services

This credit card and offer are available to you if you do not currently have the Instacart Mastercard. You must have an Instacart account in order to apply for the card. If you don’t have one already, you’ll be prompted to create an account during the application.

Review the complete list of Instacart Mastercard benefits by visiting TheInstacartMastercard.com. Terms apply.

Sign-on bonus

If you received a sign-on bonus offer, your Instacart credit will appear in your account within 10 days of card approval. You can check your credit balance in the Instacart app under Track credits and promos, or on Instacart.com under Credits, promos, and gift cards.

For questions about your sign-on bonus offer or card approval status, contact Chase at 1-800-232-3114.

Reward redemption options

New Instacart Mastercard cardmembers can choose how they want to redeem cash back rewards they’ve earned on everyday purchases. Cardmembers can choose from the following options—

  • Cash back
    • Direct deposit or a statement credit
  • Travel
    • Choose from flights, hotels, car rentals, and cruises
  • Instacart purchases
    • Pay with Points lets you use points you earn with your Instacart Mastercard to pay for all or part of your purchase in the Instacart app or at Instacart.com (excludes restaurant orders on Instacart powered by Uber Eats)
      • We’ll apply points up to your order total or you can choose a different amount up to $1,500 (150,000 points) minus available Instacart credits.
      • For more information on redeeming points, paying with points, and what points can’t pay for, check out our Instacart Mastercard Pay with Points Help Center article.

For questions about specific benefits, your Chase points balance, or your application status, reach out directly to Chase at 1-800-232-3114.

Instacart Mastercard® Pay with Points

Pay with Points lets you use points earned with your Instacart Mastercard to pay for all or part of your purchase in the Instacart app or at Instacart.com, excluding restaurant orders. Pay with Points isn’t available on retailer-branded or partner sites. Pay with Points automatically enrolls when you add Instacart Mastercard to your Instacart account.

Understanding your points
  • To access Pay with Points, you must select Instacart Mastercard as your payment method at checkout.
  • Each point is worth $0.01 (one cent), meaning 100 points equals $1 in redemption value.
  • There’s no minimum points balance to redeem. For example, if you have 1 point, you can redeem for $0.01 in the Instacart app or at Instacart.com.
  • If you don’t use points to pay for your entire order, you must use your Instacart Mastercard and/or Instacart credits to cover the remaining balance.
  • Points never expire as long as you’re an Instacart Mastercard holder.
Redeeming points

You can check your points balance at checkout. Make sure you selected Instacart Mastercard as the payment method.

You can also redeem points for cash back on chase.com or travel purchased through the Chase Travel Center. Log in at chase.com/instacart for more details.

How to Pay with Points
  1. Shop in the Instacart app or at Instacart.com, add items to your cart, and go to checkout.
  2. Choose your Instacart Mastercard as your payment method, then select the option to apply points to your total.
  3. Your Instacart Mastercard points will be deducted from your order total and displayed in checkout totals.

Please note, if you don’t use Instacart Mastercard points to pay for your entire order, the remaining balance must be paid using your Instacart Mastercard or Instacart credits.

Points can’t pay for—

  • Alcohol
  • Prescriptions
  • Instacart+ memberships
  • Instacart gift cards
  • Orders outside of the U.S.
  • Modifications made after checkout (they charge to your Instacart Mastercard)
  • Restaurant orders on Instacart powered by Uber Eats

Please review your Rewards Program Agreement to learn more about your points.

Pay with Points and EBT SNAP

You can use Pay with Points and EBT SNAP on the same order. Your EBT SNAP balance deducts before your points balance. You must pay any remaining balance on your order after using EBT SNAP and points with your Instacart Mastercard.

Problems redeeming points

If you don’t see the option to redeem points on Instacart after selecting your Instacart Mastercard as the payment method, try the following—

  1. Update your app to the latest version, or log in at Instacart.com.
  2. Select Instacart Mastercard as the payment method at checkout.

If you don’t see the option to redeem points on Instacart, please try the following steps:

  1. Remove your Instacart Mastercard as a payment method from your Instacart account
  2. Delete the Instacart app, then redownload the latest version available on App Store or Google Play Store
  3. Add your Instacart Mastercard as a payment method to your Instacart account and go to checkout to try again.
Returns and cancellations

If your order is cancelled or an item is refunded after you used Pay with Points, your points won’t be restored as Chase points. Any points used will be returned to you as an Instacart credit, redeemable in the Instacart app or at Instacart.com.

Pay with a gift card

This article is about using your Instacart gift card as a payment method during checkout. For more information on applying a gift card to your Instacart account, go to Redeem a gift card.

Instacart cash gift cards cover the costs related to an order, such as items including alcohol and prescriptions, tips, taxes, service fees, and delivery fees. They can be paired with other Instacart credits or promo codes you may have on your account.

Currently, placing an order with a gift card from multiple retailers at one time is not possible. If you would like to use your gift card balance at another retailer, you will need to place a new order.

If you purchased or received a gift card before November 18, 2020, you can still redeem it through your Instacart account. However, this gift card will not cover the cost of service and delivery fees, tips, taxes, prescriptions or alcohol items.

Note: You can’t purchase an Instacart+ membership gift card with an Instacart cash gift card. Check out our Purchase a gift card help center article to learn more about Instacart+ membership gift cards.

Add gift card at checkout

To add a gift card—

  1. Select Benefits and gift cards
  2. Select Add
  3. Enter your gift card code and tap Redeem

To apply your gift card at checkout—

  1. In the Pay with section, select Edit.
  2. Under Benefits and gift cards, select your gift card.
  3. Select apply.

The checkout screen shows the amount we deducted from your gift card and any remaining balance we charged to your payment method.

Note: Gift cards don’t apply automatically at checkout. You must select your gift card as a payment method.

Add credit or debit card at checkout

You must add a credit or debit card at checkout to use your gift card, even if the gift card fully covers your total purchase amount.

To add a credit or debit card at checkout

  1. Click or tap Choose a payment method
  2. Select Add payment method
  3. Enter your debit or credit card information and billing address
  4. Select Save

Note: Instacart charges any items added during shopping or after checkout to your gift card unless the amount is more than the authorization hold. If the order total after adding items exceeds the authorization hold, then the credit or debit card on file is charged even if you have enough funds on the gift card to cover the extra items. Likewise, if the order total after adding items exceeds the amount on your gift card, we charge the difference to the credit or debit card you entered at checkout.

Temporary authorization hold amounts

When processing orders, we place a temporary authorization hold on your gift card for a slightly higher amount than your estimated order total. This small difference accounts for potential changes in the final total due to added items, items replaced at a different price, and actual weight of items.

If the authorization hold exceeds the amount on your gift card, we charge the difference to the credit or debit card you entered at checkout.

Your gift card and/or credit or debit card will only be charged for the total of the items delivered to you.

Problem with a gift card

****Note:** At this time, Instacart can’t accept retailer specific** gift cards.

Didn’t receive gift card

If someone sent you an Instacart gift card, but you didn’t receive an email—

  • Check your email’s Junk or Spam folders
  • Confirm your email address with the sender
  • Check any other email address you may have

Redemption issues

If you see an error message when you try to redeem your gift card—

  • If the error message says that the code you entered is invalid, check that the code entered matches the code from the gift card email.

    • Make sure there are no extra spaces before or after the code
    • Copy and paste the code to make sure it is entered correctly

  • Check any other Instacart accounts you may have.

  • View the Credits, promos and gift cards section in your account to see if the gift card is redeemed. Learn more about redeeming a gift card.

If you still have trouble redeeming your card, take screenshots of the issue you’re experiencing and reach out to Instacart Customer Experience.

Note: Gift cards can only be purchased, sent, and redeemed within the same country that you live in.

Lost or stolen card

At this time, gift cards can’t be replaced if lost, stolen, or used without your permission.

Purchase a gift card

With gift cards, you can share Instacart with anyone who has access to Instacart services in their area. Your recipient can create an Instacart account for free and redeem the gift card into their account. You can also purchase Instacart gift cards through third-party sellers in-store or online.

To purchase a gift card—

  1. Go to the service area page and enter your gift recipient’s zip code to make sure we deliver to their area.
  2. Visit our Instacart gift card page and select Send gift card.
  3. In the Gift card value section, select an amount for the gift card OR Custom to choose your own amount.
  4. Enter your recipient’s name, email address, and an optional message.
  5. Choose a gift card design.
  6. Enter your name and the date you would like to send the gift card.
  7. Select Check out OR Add another card if you want to purchase more than one gift card.
    • You can purchase up to 10 gift cards in one order.

Note: At this time gift cards can only be purchased, sent, and redeemed within the same country that you live in.

To complete your gift card purchase—

  1. Confirm your recipient’s information, send date, and gift card amount.
  2. Enter your email and billing address.
  3. Enter your credit card information.
  4. Read and confirm that you agree to Instacart’s Gift card terms and conditions (US).
  5. Select Place order.

We’ll send an order confirmation to the email address you provided at checkout. Your recipient will receive the gift card by email with instructions on how to redeem it.

Instacart+ Membership gift card

Instacart+ membership provides your recipient with $0 delivery fees on eligible orders (service fees apply). An Instacart+ Membership gift card can only be used for redeeming an Instacart+ membership. It can’t be used at checkout for items, tips, taxes, or to cover all fees.

If you would like to send an Instacart+ Membership gift card, you can select from the following options—

  • 3 months - $29
  • 6 months - $59
  • 1 year - $99

Note: If the recipient of an Instacart+ Membership gift card doesn’t already have an Instacart+ membership, the membership will not auto-renew once the gift card membership ends.

If they had an Instacart+ membership before receiving the gift card and have the auto-renew feature on, their plan will auto-renew as normal. The gift card amount will be added to their existing membership term.

Bulk gift card orders

Bulk orders for Instacart gift cards and Instacart+ Membership gift cards can also be made to purchase more than 10 gift cards at a time. Check out our corporate gift cards portal to easily send and manage larger gift card orders.

Note: There is a $2,000 purchase minimum for bulk gift card orders (includes both USD and CAD gift cards).

Redeem a gift card

This article is about applying your Instacart gift card to your account. For more information on using your gift card to pay for an order or Instacart+ membership, go to Pay with a gift card.

If you purchased or received a gift card before November 18, 2020, redeem it on an account that matches the email address where you received the gift card. If you don’t have an Instacart account, create one using the same email address to which that gift card was sent. Then follow the steps below to redeem. This gift card doesn’t cover the cost of service and delivery fees, tips, taxes, prescriptions or alcohol items.

If you purchased or received a gift card on or after November 18, 2020, through our new gift card landing page or through a third-party seller, follow the steps below to redeem. This gift card covers any costs associated with an Instacart order, such as items, including alcohol and prescriptions, tips, taxes, service fees, and delivery fees.

Note: At this time you can only purchase, send, and redeem gift cards within the same country in which you live. You also can’t transfer gift cards to another Instacart account.

To redeem a gift card with the Instacart app—

  1. Sign up for Instacart or log into your existing account.
  2. Tap the Account icon in the upper right corner.
  3. Tap Credits, promos & gift cards.
  4. Tap Add.
  5. Enter the code and tap Redeem.

To redeem a gift card through the Instacart website—

  1. At the top left, click the 3 horizontal lines.
  2. Click Credits, promos & gift cards.
  3. Click Add.
  4. Enter the code and click Add funds.

Gift cards don’t apply automatically at checkout. You must select your gift card as a payment method. Use of the gift card is subject to its terms and conditions.

View your gift card balance

To view your gift card balance with the Instacart app—

  1. Tap the Account icon in the upper left corner.
  2. Tap Credits, promos & gift cards.
  3. Your gift card balance appears at the top. Tap View balances to see a full breakdown.

To view your gift card balance on the Instacart website—

  1. At the top left, click the 3 horizontal lines.
  2. Click Credits, promos & gift cards.
  3. Your gift card balance appears at the top. Tap View balances to see a full breakdown.

Shopping with FSA and HSA

Using your FSA/HSA card on Instacart
How to add your card

If you don’t see the option to add your FSA/HSA card on the Instacart app, please update to the latest version—at least 7.201.0 for iOS and 7.81.1 for Android. FSA/HSA cards aren’t an available payment method on Groceries Powered by Instacart.

**
Add a card on the Instacart website—**

  1. At the top left, click the 3 horizontal lines.
  2. Click Your account settings.
  3. Click Payment methods.
  4. Click FSA/HSA under Add payment method.
  5. Enter card information and billing address. Then click Save.

Add a card on the Instacart app—

  1. Tap the Account icon.
  2. Tap Settings.
  3. Select Debit/Credit, PayPal, and more under Payment methods.
  4. Tap Add next to FSA/HSA.
  5. Enter card information and billing address. Then tap Save.

Add a card at checkout—

  1. Select FSA/HSA under Add payment method.
  2. Enter your FSA/HSA card number, expiration date, CVV, and click Add payment method.

Remove a FSA/HSA card—

  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the top left corner.
  2. Select Your account settings.
  3. Select Payment methods.
    • From the app, select the pencil icon in the top right corner, tap the red “minus” icon next to the card you want to remove, and Delete.
    • From the web, select Delete next to the payment method you want to remove.

You can’t place an order using both an FSA/HSA card and an OTC Network card/EBT SNAP.

Still need help with payment methods? Go to Updating your payment methods.

How to check out

If you already added your FSA/HSA card—

  1. Select the cart icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Select the green Go to checkout button.
  3. Confirm your order details.
  4. Under Pay with, make sure your FSA/HSA benefit and backup payment card are selected.
  5. If not, select the payment method under Pay with.
  6. Toggle the Apply FSA/HSA benefit under Saved benefits to apply it to your order. Select Edit amount to designate the maximum amount you want applied to FSA/HSA.
  7. Select Confirm payment method.
  8. On the Your order page, confirm all information is correct.
  9. Select Place order.

If you still need to add your FSA/HSA card—

  1. Select the cart icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Select the green Go to checkout button.
  3. Ensure your delivery address, delivery instructions, and delivery time are up to date.
  4. Select the payment method under Pay with.
  5. Select FSA/HSA under Add payment method.
  6. Enter your FSA/HSA card number and any other required information and select Save.
  7. Make sure the FSA/HSA benefit is toggled on under Saved benefits to apply it to your order. Select Edit amount to designate the maximum amount you want applied to FSA/HSA.
  8. Select Confirm payment method.
  9. On the Place order page, confirm all information is correct.
  10. Select Place order.
Eligible items

Items eligible for this benefit show an FSA/HSA tag. Exact item eligibility may vary by plan. Please contact your plan administrator to confirm that the items and fees in your order are eligible under your plan.

Benefit periods

Your FSA/HSA card charges on the delivery date for the cost of eligible items, taxes and fees. Orders placed in advance may fall into a new benefit period.

For example, if you have annual benefits and place an order on December 30 for delivery on January 2, eligible items and fees deduct from your January benefits period.

Additions and substitutions

Items added or substituted after checkout charge to your FSA/HSA card if you authorized sufficient funds, and items qualify as eligible. Ineligible items or fees and additional costs charge to your backup payment methods.

Contact your provider to see the specific items and/or fees covered under your FSA/FSA plan.

FSA/HSA reimbursements

If you don’t use FSA/HSA as a payment at checkout, you may still get reimbursed for eligible items purchased through Instacart.

Look for the badge that says FSA/HSA Eligible when shopping on the Instacart platform to see which items may qualify for FSA or HSA reimbursement. You must submit a receipt of the eligible products you purchase to your benefits provider for reimbursement.

Please note that your Instacart receipt shows the delivery date, not the date you place the order. This may impact your ability to get reimbursement, as you generally forfeit unused FSA funds if not used during the 1-year period. HSA funds typically do not expire. Contact your plan administrator or employer for more information or with item eligibility questions.

You can find a list of healthcare products eligible for FSA and HSA reimbursement at Publication 969 on the IRS website or by contacting your FSA or HSA plan provider. Instacart provides eligibility information for your convenience. Instacart does not guarantee the accuracy of the information. Please note that gift orders, tips, and certain other fees may not qualify for reimbursement.

Shopping with your OTC Network card

Eligible health plan customers can use their OTC Network benefit to buy eligible items on Instacart. OTC Network benefits aren’t applicable to Groceries Powered by Instacart.

Using your OTC Network card on Instacart
How to add your card

There are 2 ways to add your OTC Network card as a payment type.

Add a card from the eligibility email—

  1. Open the email from your provider.
  2. Select the Get Started button. If you don’t have an Instacart account, you need to create one first.
  3. Select Add card from the pop-up window.
  4. Enter your OTC Network card number, expiration date, CVV, then select Submit.

Add a card at checkout—

  1. Select OTC Network under Add payment method.
  2. Enter your OTC Network card number, expiration date, CVV (if applicable), and click Add payment method.

Remove an OTC Network card

On the app—

  1. Tap the Account icon.
  2. Tap Settings.
  3. Select Debit/Credit, PayPal, and more under Payment methods.
  4. Select the pencil icon in the top right corner.
  5. Select the red minus icon next to the payment method you want to remove.
  6. Select Delete.

On the website—

  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the top left corner.
  2. Select Your account settings.
  3. Select Payment methods.
  4. Select Delete next to the payment method you want to remove.
How to check out

You must have a non-EBT backup card on file for authorization holds and non-OTC Network eligible items, which may include delivery and service fees, taxes, tips.

If you already added your OTC Network card—

  1. Select the cart icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Select the green Go to checkout button.
  3. Confirm your order details.
  4. Under Pay with, make sure you selected your OTC Network benefit and backup payment card. If not, select the payment method under Pay with.
  5. Make sure your OTC Network benefit toggled to green under Saved benefits to apply it to your order.
  6. Select Confirm payment method.
  7. On the Place Order page, confirm all information is correct. Select Place Order.

If you still need to add your OTC Network card—

  1. Select the cart icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Select the green Go to checkout button.
  3. Ensure your delivery address, delivery instructions, and delivery time are up to date.
  4. Select the payment method under Pay with.
  5. Select OTC or healthy food card under Add payment method.
  6. Enter your OTC Network card number and any other required information and select Save.
  7. Toggle your OTC Network benefit under Saved benefits to apply it to your order.
  8. Select Confirm payment method.
  9. On the Place Order page, confirm all information is correct.
  10. Select Place Order.

If you use your OTC Network card with another alternative payment method (ex. EBT or Fresh Funds), your refund goes back to the original payment method used.

We can’t issue refunds as credits or to another form of payment for these items.

Unable to checkout with your OTC Network card?

If your OTC Network card doesn’t work at checkout, your plan may not include Instacart in its eligible network. Please log in to your OTC Network member portal, OTC Network mobile app, or contact the number on the back of your OTC Network card to see if Instacart qualifies as a plan benefit.

Eligible items

Items eligible for this benefit feature an OTC Network tag. Fees may or may not be eligible, depending on your plan.

Benefit periods

Your OTC card charges on the delivery date for the cost of eligible items and fees, as determined by your plan. Orders placed in advance may fall into a new benefit period. For example, if you have monthly benefits and place an order on January 30 for delivery on February 2, eligible items and fees deduct from your February benefits period.

Additions and substitutions

Items added or substituted after checkout charge to your OTC card if you authorized sufficient funds, and items qualify as eligible under your plan. Ineligible items or fees, or additional costs charge to your backup payment methods.

Using OTC Network with EBT SNAP or Fresh Funds

You can use both an EBT SNAP and an OTC Network payment method, or Fresh Funds and an OTC Network payment method on the same order. OTC funds apply after your EBT SNAP card and/or Fresh Funds apply to eligible items.

Learn more about using your EBT SNAP card and Fresh Funds.

Contact your provider to see the specific items and/or fees covered under your OTC plan.

Accessibility features

Follow these steps to enable accessibility features.

In the app—

  1. Tap the Account icon.
  2. Tap Settings.
  3. Select Accessibility under App settings.
  4. Toggle the button to enable High contrast display preference.

On the website—

  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the top left corner.
  2. Select Your account settings.
  3. Scroll down to Accessibility.
  4. Toggle the button to Enable high contrast colors and/or Enable extended display time for alerts.

Learn more about Instacart Accessibility.

Profile and Account Settings

Account deletion and deactivation

Having an Instacart account is absolutely free! There’s no charge for keeping an account open. We add new stores, features, and exclusive deals as we grow.

Are you looking to cancel your upcoming monthly or yearly Instacart+ membership renewal? Follow the steps here.

Need to turn off promotional emails? Here’s how.

Account deletion

Please note that deleting your account is permanent and you can’t use it once deleted. You’ll need to create a new account if you want to use Instacart again.

Before requesting to delete your account, please:

  • Wait until any active orders have been delivered/picked up.
  • Cancel your Instacart+ membership, if you have one.

If you’re sure you want to delete your account, follow the directions below for the website or app.

In the app—

  1. Tap the Account icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Tap Settings.
  3. Tap Personal information.
  4. Tap Delete account.

On the website—

  1. Submit an account deletion request here
  2. Log into your Instacart Account

Look for an email from OneTrust with further instructions. Check your spam folder if it’s not in your inbox.

Once you get the email, select Confirm in the email. Please do so within 7 days or you’ll have to submit another request to delete your account.

For more information about data deletion and your privacy rights, see our Privacy Policy.

Account deactivation

When you deactivate your account, you stop receiving notifications and promotional emails. You can still place orders.

Account deactivation occurs 1 of 2 ways—

  1. You contact Customer Experience to ask for account deactivation.
  2. Instacart deactivates it due to potential fraud or other risks.

What to expect if Instacart deactivates your account—

  • Look for an email from our Financial Risk and Account Security team. Reply to that email with any requested information.
  • You can appeal the deactivation once you submit the requested information.
  • For approved appeals: your account reactivates and, if applicable, refund requests can process.
  • For denied appeals: your account stays deactivated and refund requests are denied.

If you use an Instacart account made through Kroger’s website or app, Kroger will delete your account. Call Kroger customer service at 1-800-576-437 with any additional questions.

Account information

It’s easy to update your profile information on Instacart.

Updating profile information

To change your name, email address, or phone number, follow these directions.

In-app
  1. Tap the Account icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Tap Settings.
  3. Tap Personal information.
  4. Tap the information you’d like to change.

Update name—

  1. Enter your first and last name.
  2. Tap Save.

Update email address—

  1. Enter a new email address and tap Continue.
  2. To make this change, we need to verify your identity. Select if you want to receive this code via email or phone.
  3. Enter the verification code.
  4. Log in using your new email address and select Continue.

Update phone number—

  1. Enter a new phone number.
  2. Tap Continue.
  3. Enter the verification code.
On the website
  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Click Account settings.

Update name—

  1. Click Change next to Name under Personal information.
  2. Enter your first and last name.
  3. Click Save.

Update email address—

  1. Click Change next to Email address under Account information .
  2. Enter a new email address and tap Continue.
  3. To make this change, we need to verify your identity. Select if you want to receive this code via email or phone.
  4. Enter the verification code.
  5. Log in using your new email address and select Continue.

Update phone number—

  1. Click Change number next to Phone number under Personal information.
  2. Enter a new phone number.
  3. Click Verify number.
  4. Enter the verification code.

If you use Facebook or Google to log into your account, you have to update your information in those accounts.

Updating delivery address

You can add, update, or remove delivery addresses on the Instacart homepage or in your account settings.

On the homepage—

Select the address at the top of the screen.

  • To add an address, enter the address in the Add a new address bar and tap Save Address.
  • To edit an address, select Edit beside it, make changes, and tap Save Address.
  • To remove an address, select Edit beside it and tap Delete address.

In your account settings—

  1. Select Account in-app or the 3 horizontal lines** **on the website.
  2. Select Settings in-app or Your account settings on the website.
  3. Select Address(es).

To add an address—

  1. Type in the address.
  2. Select the correct address from populated addresses.
  3. Make sure the pin on the map matches the correct location.
  4. If the pin doesn’t match, tap Adjust pin to move the pin to the correct location.
  5. Save map location.
  6. Select Save address.

To edit an address—

  1. Select Edit next to the address.
  2. Make any changes.
  3. Select Save Address.

To remove an address—

  1. Select Edit next to the address.
  2. Select Delete address.

You can also include delivery instructions for specific addresses in this section.

Please note that address changes apply to orders placed after you make the updates. If you have an order in progress and the delivery address is incorrect, please contact support. We can update it for an order in progress unless—

  • The order has alcohol or high-value items
  • It’s a certified delivery
  • A shopper is assigned
  • The new address is in a different zone

In these cases, you have to cancel your current order and place a new one with the correct address.

Account information sharing preferences

When you place an order on Instacart, we may ask if you’d like to opt-in to share your information with the retailer.

Personal information that retailers may see if you opt-in includes—

  • Name
  • Phone number
  • Email address
  • Delivery address
  • Order details
How retailers may use your account information

Retailers may use this information to send you marketing messages. They may also use this information to offer better discounts and keep your favorite items in stock.

Retailers may also disclose to Instacart whether you are a current member of the retailer’s loyalty program and whether you have deleted or canceled your loyalty account.

The information that is shared with the retailer when you opt-in is subject to that retailer’s privacy policy.

You can always select to stop sharing your information with the retailer by visiting your Account Settings.

You can read our Privacy Policy here.

Information sharing rights

In certain US states and Canadian provinces, you may be able to opt-out or opt-in to sharing your personal information with third parties.

Customers can opt out or see if they qualify by visiting Your Privacy Choices in the Instacart app or website.

Add a store loyalty or membership card to your account

Some retailers on our platform offer membership or loyalty card integration, so you can continue to receive select benefits while shopping on Instacart. You can see participating retailers in your area and add cards in your Instacart account.

In the app—

  1. Tap the Account icon.
  2. Under Savings, tap Add Loyalty cards.
  3. Tap Add card next to the store whose card you want to add.
  4. Enter your loyalty/membership information.
  5. Tap Confirm or Link membership.

On the website—

  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Click Your account settings.
  3. Click Loyalty cards.
  4. Click Add card under the store whose card you want to add.
  5. Enter your loyalty/membership information.
  6. Tap Confirm or Link membership.

At this time, we can’t confirm if your information applied. You can see any loyalty pricing and discounts under Total Savings at checkout.

Membership club and loyalty card pricing
