Terms for credit back offers

If you complete an eligible offer as indicated in your account, you will automatically be awarded credits, sometimes referred to as Cart Cash, that can be applied to eligible future orders. Credits awarded to your account will expire thirty (30) days after the date you placed the order on which the credits were awarded unless otherwise indicated in the offer. Check the “Credits, promos, and gift cards” section in your Account for expiration dates of specific awards. There are no fees associated with your ability to redeem credits.

Credits will be earned by you only when:

  1. The order meets certain criteria specified on the retailer storefront page, in the order cart or on the order checkout screen (e.g. the order is being placed for store pickup rather than delivery); and
  2. The order is placed using a valid and eligible payment method on the Instacart platform or on a participating retailer site Storefront.

The amount of credits to be awarded on an order will be displayed in the order cart and/or the order checkout screen and will be offered as a fixed amount or calculated as a specified percentage of the eligible order subtotal after the application of any coupons, credits or promotions and will not be awarded with respect to any amount paid for taxes, fees, and/or tips. Credits may not be awarded with respect to the purchase of alcohol products, prescriptions or with respect to the value of any item in excess of $150. Instacart reserves the right to restrict the ability to earn credits with respect to additional items or item categories and based on payment method. Credits might not apply to an order if you are applying another credit, coupon or promotion.

Any statement regarding the specified percentage of credits to be awarded with respect to an eligible order applies only to the subtotal associated with eligible items included in that order purchased using an eligible payment method after the application of any coupons, credits and/or promotions.

Credits will be awarded only to the account associated with an eligible order after successful pickup or delivery of an eligible order. The amount of credits awarded may be adjusted based on any refunds, credits or promotions applied to the order after successful pickup or delivery. In the event credits are redeemed and thereafter any transaction or order for which you previously obtained and/or used credits, in whole or in part, is either refunded, credited or otherwise rescinded, Instacart may, at its discretion, withhold the subsequent awarding of credits. No credits will be awarded to the extent that such award would increase the total Instacart credit balance to exceed $1,500 or cause more than $2,000 of Instacart credits (including Instacart+ credits and other types of Instacart credits) to be associated with your account in a single day.

Credits can only be applied to an eligible future order placed using the Instacart platform or on a participating retailer site Storefront. Credits may not be applied to alcohol. Instacart may fully or partially limit the ability to apply credits with certain retailers and/or to certain orders containing alcohol or prescription products. How Instacart Works

About restaurant orders powered by Uber Eats

Restaurant orders on Instacart are powered by Uber Eats who provides fulfillment, payment processing and customer support services for your restaurant orders.

To make the Instacart restaurant experience available to you, Instacart shares certain pieces of your information with Uber. Instacart restaurant orders are linked to your Instacart account and are available to you for viewing from within the Instacart App for a certain period of time.

If you update or submit a new payment method while placing your restaurant order, this updated information only applies to your Instacart Restaurants order and will not be available for your grocery orders.

The Instacart restaurant experience is powered by Uber Eats and operates independently from any Uber Eats user account that you may have. Placing an order through the Instacart restaurant experience will not result in the creation of an Uber Eats account accessible through Uber Eats. If you have an existing Uber Eats account, your Instacart restaurant orders won’t appear in your Uber Eats order histories, and your promotions, offers, settings, Uber One benefits, and information stored in your Uber Eats account won’t apply or be made available to you in your Instacart Account. Uber Cash obtained outside of this experience isn’t available as a payment method.

Offers and promotions available through the Instacart restaurant experience may not be available on Uber Eats. Please see specific offer and promotion terms for information on whether it applies on restaurant orders.

To read more about how we process your information, please see Instacart’s Privacy Policy. For more information on restaurant orders, go to our Restaurants help center article.

Ask questions about food in Search

Shopping just got easier and faster when ordering groceries through Instacart. We’re piloting a new feature where you can ask food questions in Search.

We’re using artificial intelligence (AI) to help understand and respond to certain food questions with shoppable suggestions while also providing some food-related inspiration along the way.

Try searches like—

  • “Best meats for a BBQ”
  • “What herbs do I use on potatoes?”
  • “Fruits for ice cream toppings”
  • “Fish similar to salmon”

As AI technology is still in early stages, we’re focused only on answering food-related questions. We’ll watch how our products respond and expand how we use this technology as we learn more. At Instacart, we’re committed to incorporating AI features into our products responsibly, with a goal of enhancing the Instacart shopping experience and delivering more food inspiration to customers.

Always review the suggested products to make sure they meet your specific needs before placing an order.

Keep in mind—

  • The responses and suggestions are only for reference purposes.
  • Any responses or product information given isn’t intended as a substitute for medical advice and shouldn’t be relied upon to diagnose, cure, treat, or prevent any disease, medical or health conditions, or for making health-related decisions.
  • Always consult a doctor or qualified healthcare provider for medical advice.
  • Dietary or nutritional information is provided for reference only and you should consult with a nutritionist or other professional to ensure the products are suitable for your individual circumstances.
  • Responses are based on product information that may not be current or complete. Please review packaging to confirm any information relating to product safety, nutrition, or ingredients (including allergen information).
  • Drink responsibly and ensure that alcohol is only served to people of legal drinking age.

This feature isn’t available to everyone at this time.

Cart Assistant

Cart Assistant is an AI-powered feature that helps you plan meals, discover recipes, and build your shopping list through a simple conversation.

You can ask Cart Assistant things like—

  • “Show me high protein snacks.”
  • “Give me a quick pasta recipe.”
  • “Create a grocery list for taco night.”

Based on your request, Cart Assistant can suggest recipes, recommend products, and help you add items to your cart.

At Instacart, we’re committed to incorporating AI features into our products responsibly to make it easier to discover ideas and shop for the foods you love.

Cart Assistant isn’t available to everyone at this time.

Where to find Cart Assistant

You can find Cart Assistant in the Instacart app or on the website.

In-app—

  1. Select a store to shop.
  2. In the search bar at the top of the store’s landing page, select the green diamond icon.
  3. From there, you can start a conversation and ask questions about meals, recipes, or groceries while you shop.

On the website—

  1. Select a store to shop.
  2. On the left-hand sidebar of the store’s landing page, select Cart Assistant.
  3. From there, you can start a conversation and ask questions about meals, recipes, or groceries while you shop.
How Cart Assistant works

Cart Assistant uses generative AI to help power parts of your shopping experience. When you chat with Cart Assistant, you can—

  • Plan meals: Get meal ideas for the week based on your tastes, dietary preferences, or cooking time.
  • Discover recipes: Find recipes and see suggested ingredients that can be added to your cart.
  • Create shopping lists: Turn meal ideas or recipes into a list of items you can shop for.
  • Explore products: Browse suggested products that match your requests.

You’ll be informed when you’re interacting with an AI-powered feature.

Keep in mind

Cart Assistant is designed to help you explore ideas and simplify shopping, but there are a few things to keep in mind.

AI responses may not always be accurate

AI technology is still evolving and responses may sometimes be incomplete, incorrect, or outdated. Responses may not always reflect current Instacart policies or views.

If something is important to your purchase or decision, please verify the information independently. Suggestions and information provided by Cart Assistant are for reference only. Always review product details and confirm items meet your needs before placing an order.

Product information may change

Product details can vary by store and location, including price, availability, ingredients, nutrition information, and promotions.

Packaging and product pages during checkout are the most accurate sources for product information. Prices shown in recommendations may be estimates and may not include taxes, fees, or promotions.

Recommendations can vary

Cart Assistant recommendations may change based on things like—

  • Store inventory
  • Delivery timing
  • Order size
  • Your location
  • Other real-time marketplace conditions

Because of this, suggested items may not always be available when you shop.

Health, nutrition, and food safety information

Information about ingredients, nutrition, allergens, or dietary suitability, and food safety is provided for reference only.

Always review the product packaging for the most accurate and complete information. If you have food allergies, dietary restrictions, or health concerns, consult a qualified professional. Cart Assistant responses should not be used as medical advice. Never share personally sensitive or protected health information.

Helping us improve

Your interactions with Cart Assistant may be used to help improve AI features over time. We review feedback and system performance so we can continue making the experience more helpful and reliable.

Customer safety support

You help us create a safe, respectful, and honest environment. Because of that, we want you to have access to the support and resources you need, when you need them the most.

Community guidelines for customers

Learn how we create a safe, respectful, and honest environment.

Health and safety measures

Reporting an emergency

If you have an emergency and need immediate assistance, please dial 911.

After you contact emergency services and are safe, please feel free to use our customer in-app reporting feature to notify our Trust & Safety team about your recent experience.

To report a safety incident—

  1. Select Get help on the relevant order.
  2. Select Report safety issue under Other issues.
  3. Select the issue(s) you experienced and provide any additional information.
  4. Select Submit.
Substance abuse support

Instacart has several processes and resources to safely deliver alcohol, prevent underage drinking, and encourage responsible alcohol decisions.

Do you need to disable alcohol ordering for yourself or someone you care about? Complete the Alcohol Block Request form and our Trust & Safety team will review your request. Disabling alcohol ordering is permanent and includes, but isn’t limited to—

  • Instacart owned or operated platforms

  • Platforms associated with Instacart (Ex. Costco Same-Day)

  • Any Instacart or Instacart related accounts you have

If alcohol is harming you or someone you know, it may be time to seek advice from a professional. Reach out to your primary care provider or use one of the resources listed below.

  • Alcoholics Anonymous (AA) is a worldwide organization of individuals struggling with alcoholism. AA members strive for sobriety through a 12-step program. AA operates as a non-profit, non-denominational, apolitical, and unaffiliated entity. The focus is on abstinence-based recovery from alcoholism.
  • Alcohol Help offers insights on alcohol addiction and recovery, as well as access to treatment resources.
  • SMART Recovery is a globally recognized non-profit organization focused on Self-Management and Recovery Training that offers support to those struggling with substance misuse.
  • National Institute on Drug Abuse is a U.S. federal government research institute that educates the public about drug use and addiction to enhance individual and public health.

Please contact 911 if you need immediate help.

If you’d like additional assistance, please contact SAMHSA’s National Helpline: 1-800-662-HELP(4357)

Food safety

Instacart prioritizes food and product safety and quality. We give shoppers the resources they need to pick, pack, and deliver your items properly.

Shopper best practices—

We guide shoppers on food safety, delivery, and quality by providing resources such as the Food Safety Walkthrough educational module, Health and Safety Guidelines Shopper Help Center article, and other educational modules for best practices for shopping and delivery.

Picking and delivering—

We encourage shoppers to pick the freshest items by paying attention to expiration dates, broken seals, and produce quality. We ask that they bag raw meats or products with chemicals separately to prevent cross-contamination.

Temperature control—

Shoppers keep temperature-sensitive items in fridges and freezers at the store before delivery. We also ask shoppers and drivers to use insulated bags and coolers to make sure your items stay at the correct temperature while in transit from the store to you.

Get help

Let’s talk! We’re here to help.

Instacart powers many grocery delivery orders and knowing which type of order you placed helps you get the support you need as fast as possible.

Get support for your order placed on Instacart

Orders placed on Instacart.com or the Instacart app are fully managed by Instacart, from browsing and checkout to shopping and delivery.

To get help through Instacart:

  • Select Connect with Instacart at the bottom of the Help Center homepage.
  • Select an issue/question under the order, Payments, charges & promos, or Instacart+.
    • Note: Selecting View order details on the order takes you to the order itself.
  • Select Get help at the bottom of any article in the Help Center.
  • Call 1-888-246-7822.
Get support for your order placed on a retailer’s website

Orders placed on a retailer’s app or website are managed by the retailer. Instacart powers the shopping and delivery, but your account, payment, and order details live with the retailer. If you placed an order through a retailer’s website, you can manage and update your orders there, not through Instacart.

  • Lowe’s
  • Meijer
  • Metro
  • Schnucks
  • Shoppers Drug Mart
  • Stater Bros.
  • Wakefern (ShopRite, PriceRite, The Fresh Grocer, Fairway Market, Gourmet Garage)
  • Walmart Canada
  • Wegmans

Remember, if you’re having a technical issue, the Instacart app is only compatible with—

  • Apple devices with an iOs or iPadOs version of 14.0 and higher
  • Android devices with an Os version of 5.1 and higher

To check and update your device’s operating system to the latest version, go here for Android devices and Apple devices. If you can’t update your device’s operating system to meet these requirements, you may have issues using the app. If this happens, please use Instacart.com instead.

Instacart Accessibility

At Instacart, our mission is to create a world where everyone has access to the food they love and more time to enjoy it together. To achieve this vision, we’re committed to making Instacart operable, understandable, and accessible for all of our customers, including individuals living with disabilities.

We aim to meet high standards for web accessibility and are constantly working to improve our online experience. The Web Content Accessibility Guidelines (WCAG) 2.0/2.1/2.2 Level AA are recognized and acknowledged as a way to accomplish accessibility for users with disabilities. We regularly test our site to ensure we are in substantial compliance with these Guidelines and work to remediate any issues that may be found in a timely way.

We work with accessibility and usability specialists, and people living with disabilities to make sure our sites function properly and that our customers are able to navigate easily and consistently through our website and applications.

Customers who are visually impaired or low-vision

Our site works best with the following programs—

Customers using a desktop may use a screen reading software, such as JAWS or NVDA, to assist with text to audio translations. Customers using an Android or an iOS device may enable TalkBack or VoiceOver respectively.

Turning feedback into action

Your feedback matters to us. If you have any recommendations to improve the accessibility of our platform, please email accessibility-feedback@instacart.com. We’re always happy to consider suggestions for improvement.

Instacart and Mastercard® Small and Medium Business promotion

Instacart and Mastercard partnered to launch a new embedded offer where eligible US Mastercard® small business credit or debit card cardholders are eligible for 3 free months of Instacart+ and $20 off their 2nd qualifying order each month.

Terms of the offer

This offer begins on September 1, 2025, and must be redeemed by January 31, 2027.

Qualifying for the offers

To qualify for 3 free months of Instacart+, you must—

  • Have an eligible US Mastercard® small business credit or debit card.
  • Not be an active Instacart+ member or have held an Instacart+ membership on or after September 1, 2025.

To qualify for the monthly $20 offer, you must—

  • Redeem the offer using an eligible US Mastercard® small business credit or debit card.
  • Place an order with an eligible US Mastercard® small business credit or debit card.
  • Remain an active Instacart+ member enrolled with an eligible Mastercard.
  • The $20 coupon applies to a second order paid with an eligible US Mastercard® small business credit or debit card.
  • Instacart+ members prior to September 1, 2025, who have a US Mastercard small business debit card are not eligible for the monthly $20 offer.

Note: Qualifying orders must be paid for with the same card type that was enrolled. For example, if you enrolled an eligible Mastercard small business credit card, both qualifying orders must be paid for with any eligible Mastercard small business credit card (the eligible Mastercard doesn’t necessarily have to be the one you enrolled).

The $20 off automatically applies at checkout, regardless of other active promotions. The $20 off doesn’t apply to any alcohol or Rx items in your order and does not apply to restaurant orders.

The discount expires at the end of each calendar month in which it was earned if unused.

Redeem the 3 month free Instacart+ offer

To redeem the 3 free months of Instacart+—

  1. Go to instacart.com/mastercard-business.
  2. Select Get Started to create your account or log in.
  3. On the enrollment screen, select Choose a payment method, and add your eligible Mastercard.
  4. Select Continue.
  5. Select Enroll now to begin your Instacart+ membership.
Receive the monthly offer

To receive the $20 off monthly offer, you must place your first order with an eligible US Mastercard® small business credit or debit card. You’ll receive $20 off automatically to your Instacart account after the order is delivered.

Once you place a second order with an eligible Mastercard, the $20 off automatically applies at checkout. Note, this coupon doesn’t apply to any alcohol or Rx items in your order.

Instacart Community Guidelines

Our goal is to make grocery shopping effortless so people can live happier, healthier lives. To achieve this vision, it’s important we cultivate and preserve an environment built on a foundation of Safety, Respect, and Integrity for all Instacart customers, shoppers, and partners.

We built our Community Guidelines around these three pillars, and expect everyone to adhere to the standards below while engaging on the platform.

Safety

Safety is our first priority. Every individual should feel comfortable, safe, and secure using the Instacart platform. We don’t permit any activity that could create unsafe conditions or cause harm to others.

To ensure the highest levels of safety on the platform, we reserve the right to investigate and take action on any instances including, but not limited to, the following—

  • Acts of violence or aggression including, but not limited to assault, violence/threats of violence, aggressive gestures, battery, abusive or threatening mental or verbal treatment, harassment, etc.
  • Sexual conduct of any kind involving customers, contractors, or employees of retailers or Instacart while receiving or facilitating services on the platform.
  • Brandishing a weapon while receiving services unless state law expressly prohibits this restriction.
  • Failure to verify your identity while receiving services on the platform.
  • Demanding shoppers enter a residence. Instacart shoppers are expected to deliver groceries to the customer at their desired address, but are never expected or required to enter the residence of a customer and reserve the right to decline the request.
Is this an emergency?

If you’re experiencing an emergency and need immediate assistance, please dial 911. When emergency services have been notified and you’re safe, please feel free to use our customer in-app reporting feature to notify our Trust & Safety team about your recent experience.

Leave at my door deliveries

If you or a loved one don’t feel comfortable engaging with your shopper in person, select the leave at my door option for deliveries, except for orders requiring a valid ID scan. These orders include the following—

  • Alcohol/liquor
  • Prescriptions and select over-the-counter medications
  • High-value items
Pet safety

Out of concern for shopper safety and your pet’s well-being, please make sure all pets are safely secured before your delivery arrives.

Unsafe environment

When your shopper arrives, please turn on your outdoor lights to help your shopper identify your home and be aware of any impediments or debris that may pose challenges in properly delivering your order.

Communicating with your shopper

Shopping for groceries can be a confusing and tedious task. Please be kind, considerate, and patient with your shopper. Threats, profanity, or flirtatious language in-app or in person violates Instacart’s policies and may result in the loss of your account.

Maintaining privacy with your shopper

To maintain privacy, please refrain from sharing any personal information with your shopper. All communication should happen in-app. Your account name and address is temporarily shared but not stored on the shopper’s personal device. After the order is done, shoppers don’t have access to any customer’s personally identifying information via the Instacart app. Personal information not shared with shoppers includes—

  • Personal phone number
  • Full legal name
  • Email address
  • Credit card information
Respect

We strive to create and maintain a welcoming, inclusive, and respectful environment for everyone, at all times. Our expectation is simple: treat others the way you want to be treated.

As part of our continuous effort to promote respect on the platform, we reserve the right to investigate and take action on any instances including, but not limited to, the following—

  • Discrimination of any kind on the basis of race, ethnicity, religion, gender or gender identity, age, marital status, sexual orientation, mental/physical condition or disability, age, profession, national origin or any other basis protected under applicable law.
  • Vulgar, inappropriate, intrusive, harassing, abusive or unwanted language of any kind towards others, whether verbal or written. This includes, but is not limited to, asking overly personal questions of customers, contractors, retailer employees or Instacart employees.
  • Unwanted physical contact or sexual conduct of any kind involving contractors, retail employees, or Instacart employees while receiving services.
  • Intentional property damage or vandalism of any kind.
  • Creating disturbances for the surrounding public (e.g. neighbors, co-workers, store employees, security guards, etc).
Integrity

Trust is a key ingredient in our recipe for success as a community. We expect all members of the Instacart community to be honest and to represent and conduct themselves in a way that fosters trust across the platform.

As part of our continuous effort to promote integrity on the platform, we reserve the right to investigate and take action on any instances including, but not limited to, the following—

  • Violation of any local, state, or federal laws while engaging with the Instacart platform.
  • Engaging in spam or phishing-related activity.
  • Abuse and/or manipulation of referral, coupon, or appeasement offerings, loyalty programs, etc.
  • Encouraging others to participate in fraud or to disrupt the integrity of the platform.
  • Misrepresentation of identity/contact information or providing false information to internal teams during delivery.
  • Instances of theft.
  • Sharing or distributing personal information of customers, shoppers, retailers or Instacart employees. This includes taking screenshots of the Instacart app while displaying personally identifiable or sensitive information.
  • Unauthorized use of Instacart’s trademark or intellectual property.
  • Providing inaccurate or fraudulent information to Instacart during the sign-up process or otherwise.
  • Holding duplicate accounts.

The Community Guidelines above are not comprehensive and Instacart reserves the right to suspend or deactivate customer, shopper, or retailer accounts for other reasons as deemed necessary in Instacart’s reasonable and good faith business judgment to ensure the safe and reliable operation of the platform.

Access to the platform

Instacart reviews all reported violations of these Community Guidelines and reserves the right to permanently remove account access for shoppers or customers found to be in violation.*

*In some instances, a thorough investigation may be needed, during which time the associated account(s) will be suspended pending the outcome.

Legal process and information requests

This page is intended to provide guidelines when seeking records from Instacart. Information on this page may change from time to time and is intended for informational purposes only.

What is Instacart?

Maplebear Inc. (d/b/a Instacart) is a technology company that utilizes a proprietary communications and logistics platform to facilitate quick, on-demand grocery delivery services across the United States and Canada by connecting users with personal shoppers. Instacart partners with local retailers who wish to have their products available on Instacart’s platform for delivery to customers, and engages with independent contractors who provide personal shopping and delivery services. Instacart is not a retailer.

What information is maintained by Instacart?

We collect and maintain information as described in our Customer Privacy Policy, Customer Terms of Service, and Shopper and Shopper Applicant Privacy Policy.

What legal process does Instacart require before producing information?

We generally require that all legal documents be served on our registered specialist for service of process in compliance with applicable laws. The name and current contact information for the registered specialist in each U.S. state is available online here. The name and current contact information for the registered specialist in Canada is available online here.

We do not accept service of legal documents through email unless you have received direct written consent from the legal department.

We are unable to process overly broad, vague, or unduly burdensome requests. Accordingly, we will only consider requests that are narrowly tailored and comply with applicable laws.

We ask that your request include the following:

  • All known email addresses, names, and aliases of the subject about whom you seek information, and/or all known physical addresses and telephone numbers of the subject.
  • The specific information that you’re requesting, why you’re requesting this information, and how the information you are requesting pertains to your investigation.
  • The applicable law under which you have the legal authority to request the information and clear identification that the request is confidential and for legal purposes only.

Third Party Requests

We require service of a valid subpoena in compliance with applicable law prior to releasing any information on users or personal shoppers, unless the legal department has given authorization to provide the information requested to the requesting party. All third party subpoenas must be served through our registered specialist for service of process.

Alternatively, service can be effected on Instacart’s registered specialist for service of process in California: Maplebear Inc. d/b/a Instacart, c/o CT Corporation System, 330 North Brand Blvd., Suite 700, Glendale, CA 91203.

Law Enforcement Requests

Law enforcement representatives can submit legal process to Instacart via the Law Enforcement Portal.

Law enforcement representatives may also contact us at LER@instacart.com. Instacart does not waive proper service. If you are not law enforcement personnel, any emails sent to this address will not be returned or forwarded.

Victims of Identity Theft

Victims of identity theft, or law enforcement agencies on their behalf, may be entitled to certain records relating to the theft under the Fair Credit Reporting Act (“FCRA”). For more information, please visit this link.

Pursuant to Section 609(e) of the FCRA, Instacart requires victims to provide: (1) verification of identity (e.g., valid government issued ID); (2) proof of a claim of identity theft (e.g., police report or FTC issued Identity Theft Report); and (3) relevant information about any transaction alleged to be a result of identity theft. Requests under the FCRA and all required documentation must be submitted in writing to legal@instacart.com.

Nutrition score

The nutrition score is a food rating system powered by Tufts University nutrition research and is currently available for select items.

When you’re shopping on Instacart, you can quickly check how nutritious an item is by looking at its nutrition score. It’s shown as a 1–10 rating, along with a label like Excellent, Good, or Fair to help you understand it at a glance.

You can see the nutrition score on an eligible item’s product page—

  1. Under the item, look for the Nutrition section with a score out of 10.
  2. Tap or click Show more.

Here you’ll see—

  • A quick summary of what affects the score
  • Key positive and negative nutritional factors
  • How the item compares to similar products

Nutrition scores and summaries on Instacart are powered by AI, based on Tufts University’s scoring system. The results are expected to be accurate, but may make mistakes. So please double check information that is especially important to you. It is currently available for select items.

Frequently asked questions

What’s the highest score an item can receive?

The highest score is 10. As Tufts describes it, the higher the score, the more healthful an item is.

What’s the lowest score an item can receive?

The lowest score is 1.

What increases the score?

Food that is—

  • Rich in fruits, vegetables, nuts, beans, whole grains, yogurts, seafoods, and plant oils
  • Packed with fiber, protein, vitamins, and minerals
  • High in healthy unsaturated fats
  • Minimally processed or naturally fermented

What decreases the score?

Food that—

  • Contains refined carbohydrates
  • Contains red and processed meats
  • Is high in added sugar, refined carbohydrates, sodium, or saturated fat
  • Contains artificial sweeteners, colors or flavors, or nitrites
  • Is ultra-processed or deep fried

What contributes to the score?

There are 9 nutrition domains that contribute to the score—

  • Additives
  • Fiber & protein
  • Food-based ingredients
  • Minerals
  • Nutrient ratios
  • Phytochemicals
  • Processing
  • Specific lipids
  • Vitamins

How is the score calculated?

For each item, 54 individual nutrition attributes are assessed and grouped into one of the 9 nutrition domains. Positive attributes receive a score from 0 to 10. Negative attributes receive a score from -10 to 0. And attribute ratios, which could range from positive to negative, receive a score from -10 to 10.

Each of the 9 nutrition domains receive a score which is calculated as the average of their individual attribute scores (or the sum of the individual attribute scores in the case of the “food-based ingredients” domain).

Then the 9 domain scores are added up to get the final score for each food item.

Does every item on Instacart have a score?

No, but thousands of items do have a score and Instacart is working with Tufts University to add more items each week.

How can I compare scores on different items?

You can view an individual item’s score, as well as the average score for similar items. This lets you see how an item compares to similar ones.

Where can I learn more about nutrition scores?

You can visit the Tufts University Food Compass site to learn more.

Nutritional Information on Instacart

Products on Instacart may display nutritional information. We include this information to help customers, but it isn’t a substitute for checking product labels to verify they are appropriate for your dietary preferences.

Instacart doesn’t intend for nutritional or dietary information to act as a substitute for medical advice. Customers shouldn’t rely on this information to diagnose, cure, treat, or prevent medical conditions or to make health-related decisions. Always consult healthcare professionals before making significant dietary changes.

Nutritional labels on products

Third-party sources provide nutritional information for products on Instacart, including product packaging, the product manufacturer, or other information sources. This information is intended for discovery purposes to improve your shopping experience.

We aim to provide correct information for our customers, but we can’t guarantee that the nutritional, allergen, ingredient, or other product information shown is 100% accurate or complete. Actual product packaging in store may contain more and/or different information than shown on Instacart. Instacart assumes no liability for misstatements or inaccuracies of information, and customers assume all risk regarding product information.

Instacart encourages consumers to verify all product ingredients suit their dietary preferences. Please read all labels, warnings, and directions on product packaging.

To set or adjust dietary preferences—

  1. Go to Settings in your account.
  2. Select Smart Shop preferences.

Third-party content

Third-party contributors may create recipes, lists, pop-ups, or product collections focusing on specific diets, lifestyles, and preferences. Contributors select the products and may receive compensation, as identified in the content.

Third-party content exists for discovery and educational purposes only. It is not intended as medical or dietary advice. Instacart isn’t responsible for third-party content and doesn’t claim the content is accurate, complete, reliable, or error-free.

Product recalls

Instacart monitors food and product recall announcements. We’ll send you an email with important safety information if a previous purchase is potentially impacted.

Reach out to the retailer of purchase for a refund on recalled products.

If you have additional questions about a food or product recall, refer to the applicable product recall announcement published by the regulatory authority below—

  • U.S. Food and Drug Administration
  • U.S. Department of Agriculture
  • U.S. Consumer Product Safety Commission
  • Government of Canada: Recalls and safety alerts
Product safety

Instacart always strives to provide the safest, most efficient service for customers—from pickup to delivery. Distinct from product recalls, we’ll help make up for products that arrive damaged. Should you ever receive a damaged product, contact Instacart Customer Experience within 24 hours so we may help with the best possible solution.

Research @ Instacart

At Instacart, we’re focused on making grocery shopping as painless as possible. To help improve the Instacart experience, we conduct user research with both customers and shoppers to better understand what works and what doesn’t.

Feedback from both customers and shoppers directly inform our decisions. On top of contributing to the future of Instacart, we offer rewards to those who participate in user research studies. Customers will receive discounts off future order(s) and shoppers will get a shopper bump deposited directly to the Instacart Shopper app. We know your time is valuable and appreciate you spending it with us to improve Instacart.

Types of user research—

  • Surveys
  • Video interviews
  • Unmoderated studies where we ask you to record your screen as you complete certain tasks on Instacart.
  • In-person shop-alongs where we accompany shoppers as you pick groceries and complete deliveries.
Common Questions

What’s the time commitment?
Our surveys usually take around 5 minutes, and a video interview may take an hour. It all depends on the research initiative and how we’d like to get your feedback.

Who works on the research team?
We have a number of people on the Research @ Instacart team, but you can generally expect to receive research invitations from research-recruiting@instacart.com.

I signed up to participate in a video interview. Now what?
Please find a quiet, private place with stable internet to respond to the questions. Interviews normally take between 30 - 90 minutes, depending on the research study.

Am I allowed to discuss user research with others?
Shopper participants sign an NDA prior to the study. We may share proprietary information during the sessions (e.g. unreleased designs)—it’s important not to discuss the specifics of your study with others.

Interested in participating?

We reach out directly to eligible customers and shoppers via email about potential research studies. We’ll ask you to sign a consent form prior to your research session where you agree to allow Instacart to use your feedback to improve the platform. If the study is a video interview, we provide a link where you can schedule a time to chat with our research team.

Selecting a retailer

You can see multiple retailers available in your area from the homepage when logged in.

To start shopping, simply select the retailer’s image! You can also select the address at the top of your screen and change it to see retailers in other areas.

Have a preferred location you like to shop at? Select Pickup on the homepage or at checkout then select the specific location you want to get your order from. But, for deliveries, we select the best store location for your order based on factors like item and shopper availability.

Change retailer selection

You can also change your original retailer selection if needed.

To change your retailer from the Instacart website—

  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the top left corner of your current store’s homepage. Then, click Stores.
  2. Click on a new retailer image.
  3. To see retailers are available in different areas, update your address by clicking on the address listed under the Search bar at the top right of your screen.

To change your retailer in the Instacart app—

  1. Tap the X in the top left hand corner of your current store’s homepage.
  2. Tap on a new retailer image.
  3. To see retailers available in different areas, update your address by tapping on the address listed under the Search bar at the top right of your screen

Shop In-Store

You can prep for your next in-store shopping trip on Instacart. Browse deals, check store inventory, stay on budget, and build your shopping list. Shop In-Store isn’t currently available on the Groceries Powered by Instacart experience.

Before shopping
Browse deals and clip coupons

Browse current in-store coupons through the Deals tab at the bottom of the screen. Tap the Save checkbox to save the coupons to your account. Show the coupon in-store when checking out.

Build shopping lists

Build and manage your shopping list to prep for an in-store shopping trip. Shopping lists estimate your total and savings beforehand and help save you time in-store. Item prices may vary by availability and store location.

While shopping

Check off items from your list and collapse sections as you shop to make sure you don’t miss anything. When you’re done, reset your list to plan your next shopping trip.

After shopping

For receipt upload terms and conditions, check out our Promotion codes terms and conditions Help Center article.

Sign up for an account

Create an Instacart account by visiting our website. Sign up now with your email address, Google, or Facebook authentication. You can also download the Instacart app on iOS or Android devices.

Can I have two or more accounts?

Instacart allows only one personal account per person or household. However, you may use a secondary account for business purposes.

Accounts aren’t limited to one delivery address, or even one ZIP code. Entering a new delivery address when you log in will show you more store options for that area.

Use of any Instacart account is subject to our Terms of Service.

Take an in-app survey

A short survey may appear in your Instacart app after you use Meals or Health features. Take it in under a minute to share your feedback, or close it and keep shopping, your account isn’t affected either way.

To complete the survey

The survey appears on your screen and asks: “How would you feel if you could no longer use this feature?”

There are two separate surveys, one for Meals, one for Health. You’ll only see one at a time, based on which feature you used most recently.

  1. Read the question.
  2. Tap or enter your response, then tap Submit or Next.
  3. To close the survey without completing it:
    1. Tap the X in the top right corner, or swipe down to dismiss it.
    2. Tap Shop to pick up where you left off.
What happens next

If you submitted a response, your feedback is recorded. The survey won’t appear again.

If you closed it, the survey may reappear in your next session. After 3 sessions without a response, it stops showing up on its own.

Frequently asked questions

Do I have to complete this survey?

No. Tap the X to close it and keep shopping. Skipping it won’t affect your account, orders, or access to Meals or Health features.

What do I get for taking the survey?

No reward or credit is attached to this survey. Your response goes directly to the team building Meals and Health features and they use it to decide what to keep, improve, or build next.

Why am I seeing this?

You’re seeing it because you recently used Meals Hub or Nutrition Score. Not every customer gets this survey, you were selected from users who’ve tried these features.

Why does it keep appearing?

The survey appears once per session. After 3 sessions without a response, it stops automatically. Tap the X to dismiss it.

Is this from Instacart?

Yes. This survey is from Instacart directly, not a third party. It’s not connected to any sweepstakes, gift card, or prize offer.

I accidentally closed it. Can I retake it?

The survey reappears automatically on your next session if you haven’t submitted a response. There’s no way to reopen it manually.

I don’t see this survey on Instacart.com.

This survey appears only in the Instacart app on iOS and Android. It’s not available on the website or mobile browser.

My friend sees this survey but I don’t.

Not all customers receive this survey. It’s shown to a select group of users who have recently used Meals Hub or Nutrition Score. If you don’t see it, no action is needed. It doesn’t affect your access to any features.

Where we deliver

Instacart serves households across the United States and Canada — and we’re always growing. Let an Instacart shopper handle your grocery list for you sometime soon.

Service areas

Instacart’s delivery and pickup services are available in thousands of cities in North America. To see if that includes your area—

  1. Visit our locations page
  2. Enter your ZIP or postal code
Instacart doesn’t deliver to me

Although Instacart is available in many cities across the U.S. and Canada, we’re not everywhere just yet. However, we could be coming to your area soon!

To be notified when we start serving your ZIP or postal code—

  1. Visit our locations page
  2. Enter your ZIP or postal code
  3. Click Get notified when we expand to your area and sign up for updates

Have you placed an order to your address before but we don’t deliver to you anymore? Some zip codes are only partially within our service area. We don’t have an ETA for expanding to these areas, but keep checking back.

Using your account in different countries

Your Instacart account will work in countries where we deliver no matter which country you created your account. For example, if you live in the United States, you can use your account in Canada! There is no need to create multiple accounts.

Visit instacart.com to place an order in the U.S. and visit instacart.ca to place an order in Canada.

Please note that Canadian retailers don’t appear in the U.S. and vice-versa.

Available stores

Instacart Shoppers shop local stores in your area. To see which stores are available to you, visit our locations page. From there, select your state or province, or scroll down to see a list of available areas. Selecting the area name displays a list of available stores in that area.

We sometimes can’t deliver from stores outside of your city limits because of local laws. Stores that used to be available to you may no longer be available.

Adding new stores

We’re always adding new stores you can order from. To stay in the loop as we add more stores, subscribe to Sales and Marketing email updates, or follow Instacart on Twitter, Instagram, or Facebook.

Work for Instacart

Instacart is always looking for great additions to our shopper community and corporate teams—

  • If you love grocery shopping, have a smartphone, and have access to reliable transportation, apply to become a Shopper here.
  • If you’re looking for career opportunities with Instacart’s corporate team, see the current job openings here.

Pricing Discounts Fees

Coupons and promotions

Coupons

Coupons and in-store discounts are sometimes available on Instacart. The coupons you see on Instacart are manufacturer’s coupons. Click or tap on the coupon box below an item to apply it to your order. The coupon applies when you add the item(s) to your cart.

If your shopper replaces your original item with one that doesn’t qualify, you won’t receive the discount. Learn more about changing your replacement preferences.

NOTE: Instacart only honors coupons listed on our platform. However, we often offer discounts and promotions not available in-store.

Buy One, Get One (BOGO) sales

Buy One, Get One (BOGO) sales vary by retailer and state.

Select the coupon box below an item to apply it to your order. If items are eligible for a BOGO sale, it’ll apply at checkout. BOGO deals will only apply if the correct number of items are in the cart.

Stock Up & Save

Find Stock Up & Save deals by Spend $X more, get X. For example, spend $15 on items listed, get $0 delivery fee* or a discount. *Service fees apply.

To locate these sales—

  1. Scroll down the retailer’s store homepage.
  2. Find the Stock Up & Save banner between Sales and Buy it Again on Sale.
  3. Select View More.

Discount applies only when you purchase the required number of qualifying items during the specific time period the discount is valid, as determined by Instacart.

Promotion codes

You can add promotion codes to your account in the Instacart app or on the website.

[TABLE]

To add a promotion code at checkout, select Offers and promo codes. Enter the code in the Add promo or gift card field, then click or tap Apply.

Get a promotion code from 1 of our retailer partners? Apply the code on their Storefront site.

Available credits and active promotions show under the Credits, promos, and gift cards section of your account. The promotion amount automatically applies to your next order during checkout. Any remainder goes back to your account for future orders.

If you received a promotion code from your friends or family, it only works on your account if you meet the requirements of the promotion. You can’t transfer these codes. Find these requirements at the bottom of the email the original recipient received.

We can’t apply promotions or credits toward fees, taxes, tips, or alcohol items, due to applicable laws and regulations.

Promotions without a code

Some promotions don’t require a code. You can add these promotions to your account by clicking the button or link in the email, push notification, in-app banner, or text message you receive. Once activated, the promotion amount automatically applies to your next order(s) at checkout. You can’t use these in combination with another promotion or credit. Any remaining credit goes back to your account for future orders.

You can’t share these promotions. If you share the promotion, or try to use it on another account, the promotion won’t apply. They are only available for the account associated with the original recipient’s email address.

Promotions not applying after delivery

Did your promotion apply at checkout but now it’s not there? Promotions will drop off your order if your shopper replaces or refunds items and the order total drops below the minimum for that promotion.

Multiple promotions on your account

Only $0 delivery fee promotions and select promotion codes combine with additional promotions. Otherwise, you can only use 1 promotion per order.

You can’t combine select promotions and/or credits. If you have multiple promotions on your account, the promotion with the biggest discount value automatically applies at checkout.

To see which promotions applied, select Offers and promo codes on the checkout screen. You can remove applied promotions or apply other available offers. If the promotion you choose cannot be combined with existing ones, a notification will confirm whether you want to proceed with the selected promotion.

To apply any remaining available promotions, you need to place another order.

Any Instacart credits on your account apply to your order based on the earliest expiration date.

Credits and Rewards

Credits and rewards combines—

  • Credits and rewards and credit-back promotions.
  • Credits from referring others to the Instacart platform.

You don’t have to do anything to accept Credits and rewards you’ve earned. Credits and rewards with the earliest expiration date automatically applies to your order first. You can choose not to apply Credits and rewards at checkout.

Access your Credits and rewards

  1. Go to your account.
  2. Select Credits, promos, and gift cards.
  3. View an itemized breakdown of your credits, available promotions, and credit history.

Applied Credits and rewards also appears on the checkout screen.

Refunds that don’t go back to your original payment method appear as promotions, not Credits and rewards.

Credits and rewards awards subject to Credit Back Terms. Credit has no cash value. Void where restricted or prohibited by law. You can’t transfer credit to another Instacart account. We can’t apply promos or credits toward fees, taxes, tips, or alcohol items due to local laws and regulations.

Instacart fees and taxes

Instacart charges fees based on several factors. You can find the breakdown of specific fees below.

Delivery fee

The delivery fee varies based on the retailer, delivery window, and order total. You see the delivery fee when choosing your delivery window at checkout.

Instacart+ members get $0 delivery fees on eligible orders $10 or more per retailer. Service fees apply. Costco orders $35+; restaurant orders $25+.

All orders must be at least $10 to qualify for delivery.

Service fee

What is the service fee on my order?

The service fee helps support the Instacart platform and covers a range of operating costs including shopper operations, insurance, background checks, and customer support. The service fee isn’t a tip and doesn’t go to the shopper delivering your order.

Service fees vary and are subject to change based on factors like location and the number and types of items in your cart. You can view the service fee total on every order at checkout.

Is there a service fee for pickup orders?

Instacart pickup orders don’t have service fees, regardless of Instacart+ membership.

Why did my service fee total change after delivery?

Changes to your order, including replacing or refunding an item, change the service fee. After the order is complete, you’ll see the final cost and service fee total on your digital receipt. For more information about order changes, visit this article.

Shopper tips are separate from service fees. 100% of your tip goes to your shopper. Learn about tipping your shopper.

Long-distance delivery fees

We may charge a long-distance service fee if the delivery route is 15+ minutes or includes a toll.

The “Farther away” label next to the store name means that store is farther from your delivery address than usual. Ordering from it may include a long-distance delivery fee, which you’ll see at checkout before you place your order.

Priority fees

Priority fees apply to customers who choose priority delivery windows during checkout. Priority delivery is the fastest delivery option and varies based on shopper availability. Fees are clearly marked at checkout. Priority delivery isn’t always available.

If your order arrives over 30 minutes after the end of your priority delivery window, Instacart may provide an automated credit or refund of your priority fee. Credits or refunds may not be provided if the order is delayed as a result of significant post-order changes or other unforeseen circumstances.

Pickup fees

Pickup orders from certain retailers have a “pickup fee” (equivalent to a delivery fee for pickup orders).

Convenience fee

Convenience orders typically deliver in as fast as 30 minutes and are available late at night. Service fees and item prices may be higher than other delivery orders. Convenience orders under $35 have a waived priority fee. Orders must be over $10. Non-Instacart+ members may still pay a delivery fee.

Regulatory fees

Some customers might see a Regulatory response fee on their receipt, as a result of local laws and regulations.

NYC regulatory response fees appear in the order summary.

Bottle deposit and bag fees

We collect these fees per local city, state, provincial, territory, or retailer rules where applicable. To find more information about potential bottle deposit fees, please visit bottlebill.org.

To see your state’s bag fees, visit ncsl.org.

Membership fees

If you’re an Instacart+ member you may see an annual or monthly membership charge, depending on your plan. By opting in to Instacart+, you won’t get delivery charges on orders of $10+*.

*Service fees apply. $35 min. for Costco.

Taxes and additional fees

Taxes are in the order total on the delivery receipt we email to you when your order is complete. The tax and/or fees you pay on products purchased through the Instacart platform are the same as in a physical store. Depending on your location, the delivery or service fee may also be subject to tax. Additional fees may apply to your order depending on your city, state, province, or territory.

  • Sales tax: The sales tax may apply to some or all of the items in your order per local laws depending on the store or delivery address. Instacart+ membership fee and other fees are subject to applicable taxes.
  • Sales tax holidays: Some states offer a limited-time waived or reduced sales tax on select items. Please check your state’s website for more information on current and upcoming sales tax holidays.
  • Sugar-sweetened beverage fees: In certain jurisdictions, sugary beverages are subject to an additional tax. These fees may apply to some or all of the items in your order per local laws depending on the store address.
  • Retail delivery fee (Colorado only): A $0.27 retail delivery fee applies to Colorado delivery orders to comply with state tax legislation.

Customers shopping in Oklahoma with a 100% disabled veteran sales tax exemption card from the Oklahoma Tax Commission can contact Instacart for a sales tax refund.

Item pricing

It’s important for our customers to understand the Instacart marketplace pricing.

Instacart marketplace vs. in-store pricing

Retailers set the item prices on the Instacart marketplace. While many retailers offer everyday store prices* on Instacart, some retailers may set prices on the Instacart platform that are different than in-store prices.

*Some in-store sales/promotions may not apply. Refer to the retailer pricing policy for more information.

NOTE: Instacart only honors coupons listed on the platform. However, we often offer discounts and promotions not available in-store. We reserve the right to cancel an order for any reason, including as a result of incorrectly priced items. If we cancel an order, we will refund your account for the amount paid.

You can view each retailer’s pricing policies by looking for the More info pricing link at the top of the retailer’s storefront on both the website and in the Instacart app.

In the app—

  1. Go to the main page of the app and select a retailer
  2. Under the retailer’s logo at the top, you’ll see the pricing policy: “Everyday Store Prices,” “View pricing policy,” etc. (Policies vary by retailer.)
  3. Tap the pricing policy link, then view Pricing information under Info

On the website—

  1. Go to Instacart.com and select a retailer
  2. Under the retailer’s logo at the top, you’ll see the pricing policy: “Everyday Store Prices,” “View pricing policy,” etc. (Policies vary by retailer.)
  3. Click on the pricing policy link, then view Pricing information under Info
Final price for weighted items

Some items, such as loose produce, are weighed in-store. Shoppers always try to get the customer’s requested weight, but final weights and prices may vary. The price displayed at checkout for certain items from the deli, meat, and seafood counters is based on the weight and price shown on the Instacart platform. If the items’ final weight is different from what you requested or what was displayed on the platform, your receipt adjusts accordingly. We will charge you the updated price.

If you use an EBT card and the final weight of the items is different than what you requested, the extra cost of weighted items is charged to your debit or credit card.

Incorrectly priced items

If you think an item is priced incorrectly—

  1. Select the item to open item details.
  2. Select Incorrect Info?
  3. Tell us What is wrong with this item? by selecting an option in the menu.

These alerts help keep our catalog up-to-date for all customers, and we look into them as soon as possible.

Redeeming promotion codes

Add promo codes to your Instacart account or during checkout from the Instacart app or Instacart.com to save on your order. Promo codes are typically shared through email or as a banner message in your Instacart shopping experience.

How to add a promo code to your account

From the Instacart app

  1. Tap the Account icon in the upper right corner
  2. Tap Credits, promos, and gift cards
  3. Tap Redeem
  4. Enter the code and tap Redeem

From Instacart.com

  1. At the top left, click the 3 horizontal lines
  2. Click Add Promo or Gift Card
  3. Enter the code and click Add to account
How to add a promo code at checkout

Click or tap Add promo code or gift card at checkout to add the promotion code to your order.

From the Instacart app

  1. Go to your cart
  2. Tap Go to Checkout
  3. Scroll to the bottom of Your order screen
  4. Tap Add promo code or gift card
  5. Enter the code and click Redeem

From Instacart.com

  1. Go to your cart
  2. Click Go to checkout
  3. Find the price breakdown on the right side of your screen
  4. Click Add promo code or gift card
  5. Enter the code and click Redeem

Retailer promos

You can redeem promotions offered by Instacart’s retail partners directly from the retailer’s Storefront site.

Note—

  • Available credits and active promotions show under the Credits, promos, and gift cards section of your account. The promotion amount automatically applies to your next order during checkout if you meet the requirements of the promotion. For credits and gift cards, any remainder goes back to your account for future orders.
  • If you received a promotion code from your friends or family, it only works on your account if you meet the requirements of the promotion. The requirements are at the bottom of the email the original recipient received.
  • Promotions and credits can’t be applied toward fees, taxes, tips, prescriptions, or alcohol due to applicable laws and regulations.

  • Coupons and promotions
  • Instacart+ partnership promotions
  • Instacart credits

Referrals

Instacart customers can refer friends and family. For each referral, they’ll get up to $40 credit to use across their first two orders, and you’ll get $10 credit once their delivery is complete. You can refer through text, email or social media using your personalized referral code or link. There is no limit to the number of users you can refer.

*Referral credit amounts can vary.

In the app—

  1. Tap the Account icon in the upper right corner
  2. Tap Refer a friend, earn credit
  3. Share your referral link via contacts, text, email, social media or other channels

On the website—

  1. At the top left, click the 3 horizontal lines
  2. Click Invite friends, earn money
  3. Share your referral link or code via contacts, text, email, social media or other channels

Tipping

Tips are optional but a great way to show your shoppers appreciation and recognition for their excellent service. 100% of your tip goes directly to the shopper(s) shopping and delivering your order. If 2 shoppers fulfill your order, the tip splits evenly between them.

Instacart suggests a default tip based on several factors, such as order attributes and your tip history. We suggest a default tip during bad weather.

You can modify the tip while placing an order or after delivery. However, you can’t modify the tip while the order is in progress. You can increase your tip up to 14 days after delivery or reduce your tip up to 2 hours after delivery.

View receipts and charges

You can view your receipt, understand charges on your order, and request a refund directly from your Instacart account. If a charge looks unfamiliar, or a refund isn’t showing up in your bank account, this article explains what to expect and how to take action.

View your receipt

In the app:

  1. Tap the Account icon (head and shoulders symbol).
  2. Tap Orders.
  3. Tap the order you want to review.
  4. Tap your delivery address and payment information and then tap Receipt.

On the website:

  1. Go to Instacart.com and click Your orders.
  2. Click the order you want to review.
  3. Click Receipt on the left of your screen.
Understand charges on your receipt

Your final receipt may show charges that differ from your original estimated total. Here’s what each section and line item means.

Adjustments (top of receipt)

This section shows refunds for out-of-stock items or item issues like missing or damaged items.

If you see Not Charged next to an item that means the shopper refunded before checkout. You were never billed for these, so no refund will appear in your bank statement.

Charges section (bottom of receipt)

This section shows what you were charged and any refunds applied after your order completed.

Receipt line item What it means
Original Charge What Instacart charged you when your order completed.
Additional Charge Items you added after placing your order increased the total. You’re charged the difference between the original charge and the new total.
Adjustment Charge The final item cost was higher than estimated. Ror example, a replacement item at a higher price, or a weighted item that came in heavier than expected. You’re charged the difference.
Refund for order issue A refund Instacart processed after your order completed.
Total Charged Your final order total, after all charges and refunds are applied.

⚠️ Tip adjustments: Tips added or changed after delivery may appear as a separate charge on your card statement.

⚠️ Temporary authorization hold: Before your order is finalized, you may see a temporary hold for a slightly higher amount than your estimated total. This is not a final charge, it adjusts once your order is complete.

Refund not showing in your bank account

Instacart processes refunds immediately after an issue is reported. How long it takes to appear depends on your bank or card provider.

If your refund isn’t showing up, check the following:

  • Same-day refunds: These don’t appear as a separate line item. Instead, you’ll see an adjustment to your original charge. If you received a full refund on the same day, the original charge may disappear from your statement entirely.
  • Bank processing time: Most refunds appear within 5–10 business days, depending on your financial institution.
Dispute a charge

If a charge on your receipt looks incorrect, report it directly from your order:

  1. Go to Your orders.
  2. Select the relevant order.
  3. Click Report an issue.

All entries must be submitted during the applicable Entry Period (as described in the chart above) to be eligible for that Entry Period’s drawing. Entries will not roll over to subsequent Entry Periods, if any. All entries become the exclusive property of the Sponsor. None will be acknowledged or returned. Proof of sending or submission will not be deemed to be proof of receipt by Sponsor. The Sponsor is not responsible for lost, late, incomplete, invalid, unintelligible, illegible, misdirected or otherwise incomplete entries, all of which will not be honored. Subsequent attempts made by the same individual to submit entries beyond the above-stated limits, for example, by using multiple or false contact information or otherwise, will be disqualified. Entries generated by a script, computer program, macro, robotic or other automated means are void and will be disqualified. Entries that are in excess of the stated limit, incomplete, false, late or otherwise not in compliance with the Official Rules will be disqualified. Entrant must be the registered subscriber of the e-mail account by which the entry is made. In the event of a dispute as to entries submitted by multiple users having the same e-mail account, the authorized subscriber of the e-mail account (as specified by the Internet Service Provider) used to enter the Sweepstakes at the actual time of entry will be deemed to be the entrant.
