Order in Progress

Chat with your shopper

Chat with your personal shopper anytime during order fulfillment to request changes, ask about replacements, or communicate other preferences.

You can edit your items, notes, and replacements anytime before your shopper begins shopping.

Chat with your shopper

In the app—

Open the Instacart app while your order is in progress.

  1. Go to Orders.
  2. Tap the current order.
  3. When your current order screen appears, tap the chat bubble icon in the upper right corner.
  4. Send your message.
  5. If you have turned on in-app notifications, you’ll receive an alert when your personal shopper replies.

On the website—

  1. At the top left, click the 3 horizontal lines.
  2. Click Your orders.
  3. Click View order detail on the current order.
  4. Click Leave a message for your shopper here!
  5. To view replies, keep the page open on your browser.

Spanish and French speaking shoppers’ chats should automatically translate to English. If chats appear in another language, tap Translate under the chat.

Review chats on previous orders

After an order is complete, you can review the messages between you and your shopper on the Instacart app or website.

In the app—

  1. Go to Orders.
  2. Tap the order you want to view.
  3. Scroll down and tap Chat log.

On the website—

  1. At the top left, click the 3 horizontal lines.
  2. Click Your orders.
  3. Find the order and click View order detail.
  4. Click Chat log (located under Delivery Details on the right side of your screen).

Order changes

For best results, download the latest version of the Instacart app for iOS or Android.

You can track your shopper’s progress and view item changes in the Instacart app. Turn on notifications to get alerts for all changes, including replacements and refunds.

If your shopper hasn’t begun your order, you can update it on the Order screen. If your shopper has already started on your order, you can chat with them for help.

  • Chat with your shopper
  • Tracking your order and delivery

If you placed your order directly through a retailer’s app or website, you can make any changes or update preferences (ex. You want plastic bags only) in the retailer’s app or on their website.

If your order contains Order Ahead items such as custom deli items, cakes, or catering you scheduled in advance, you can’t add or edit items after you place the order.

Add a new item

You can add items until your shopper checks out.

On the home screen, tap Add items to order.

On the order screen, tap Add or view items or search for the item and tap the plus symbol.

Note: There are some item restrictions. You can’t add alcohol, Rx, prohibited items, items over $49.99, items for Klarna orders, or items from select retailers.

Change quantity or remove an item

You can adjust item amounts in your order. However, some states limit how much alcohol you can order. If you can’t complete your order, try reducing the quantity of alcohol items.

In-app—

  1. Tap the Account icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Tap Orders.
  3. Select the current order.
  4. Select the item you want to edit.
  5. Tap the plus or minus symbol to increase or decrease quantity.

On the website—

  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Click Your Orders.
  3. Select the order you want to update.
  4. Select the item whose quantity you want to adjust.
  5. Click the number and adjust as you like.
Update item replacement choices
  1. Tap the current order button at the bottom of the screen (app) OR
  2. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner, then select the order you want to update under Your orders (web.)
  3. Under the item you’d like to update, select Edit replacement.
  4. Choose your preferred replacement option.
Approving order changes

You can select replacement options, or choose a refund for the item instead.

To see items your shopper already found—

  1. Tap the current order button at the bottom of the screen (app) OR
  2. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner, then select the current order under Your orders (web.)
  3. Select See Shopped Items.
  4. If you approve the change, select Approve.
  5. If you prefer another option, select Other options…
  6. If you don’t see the option you prefer, chat with your shopper.
  7. When shopping is complete, you can view all completed changes and items in the Item Summary.

Note: Depending on your selected retailer and delivery time, your order may be stored at the appropriate temperature for each item until delivery. We’ll let you know on the order screen if that happens.

Items removed from your cart before checkout

If you see a message that says some items are no longer available, those items were automatically removed from your cart. This can happen when:

  • An item went out of stock or is no longer available at the store you selected.
  • You updated your delivery address or switched stores, and the item isn’t available at the new location. You may have seen a message that said: “Item prices and availability may vary at this address.”
  • Local regulations prevent the item from being delivered to your address.

The app will show you similar replacement options. You can browse and add an alternative, or continue to checkout with the items remaining in your cart.

If all of your items were removed and you can’t complete checkout, try selecting a different store or updating your delivery address. If the issue continues, contact us for help.

Tracking your order and delivery

You can track your order from the moment your shopper begins shopping thru delivery.

For best results, download the latest version of the Instacart app for iOS or Android.

Order status

Check your order status with the Instacart app or website.

In the app—

  1. Tap the Account icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Tap Orders.
  3. Tap an order to view its status.

On the website—

  1. At the top left, click the 3 horizontal lines.
  2. Click Your orders.
  3. Click on an order to view its status.
Tracking your order in progress

When your personal shopper begins working on your order, you’ll see a message in the Instacart app. If you have notifications turned on, you’ll also receive a notification.

In the app—

  1. Tap the Account icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Tap Orders.
  3. Tap See Shopped Items to view items already found and changes like replacements and refunds.
  4. You can Approve changes or choose Other options for each item.

Want to chat with your shopper? Tap the Chat link at the top of the screen to let them know if you want to add items to your order or make other changes. Note certain items, such as alcohol, may not be added after your order is placed.

On the website—

  1. At the top left, click the 3 horizontal lines.
  2. Click on an order to view its progress.
Tracking your delivery in progress

When it’s time for delivery, you’ll see a message on the Instacart app’s order screen. (If you have notifications turned on, you’ll get an alert as well.)

The order status page gives an up-to-date estimated delivery range and a map you can tap to follow the driver’s progress.

Need to give your shopper last-minute delivery instructions? Tap the chat icon next to your shopper’s profile. (For safety reasons, they might not reply while they’re en route.)

Shopper not assigned yet

Shoppers may not be immediately assigned due to availability, weather, the retailer, or time that the order is placed.

You can turn on notifications so that you know as soon as a shopper is assigned. You can also make changes to your order while you wait!

Placing Orders

Adding delivery instructions

Adding delivery instructions can help shoppers deliver your order to the right place.

You can add delivery instructions—

  • When adding an address to your account
  • While placing the order
  • After placing the order (but before a shopper starts working on it)

If your shopper is working on your order, contact them directly.

Helpful info to include—

  • Parking locations
  • Building security or gate information
  • Your building or apartment complex’s name
  • Directions from the building entrance to your unit
  • Whether the shopper should knock or ring the bell
  • Specific directions for hard-to-locate front doors
  • Markers on your home that might help the shopper find the best entrance

Additionally, you’re responsible for providing a safe, well-lit delivery path for your shoppers, including securing pets.

During checkout, you can select Leave at my door if I am not around. The shopper will take a picture of the order at your door before they leave.

Please note—If you choose unattended delivery, you accept full responsibility for the order after delivery, including any loss due to theft or temperature-sensitive damage.

To add delivery instructions at checkout—
1. From your cart, tap Go to checkout.
2. Choose a delivery address and tap Confirm address.
3. Tap Delivery Instructions.
To add delivery instructions after checkout (Before a shopper is assigned)—

You can update your delivery instructions after placing an order, as long as a shopper hasn’t started shopping yet.

On the app—

  1. Select your current order.
  2. Tap Delivery Details.
  3. Enter your delivery instructions.
  4. Tap Update instructions.

On the website—

  1. Select your current order.
  2. Select Delivery details.
  3. Enter your delivery instructions.
  4. Select Save changes.

Adding item and replacement instructions

You can add item and replacement instructions through the Instacart app or website.

Item instructions

You can add item instructions from your cart, on the checkout page, or even after you place your order (as long as the shopper hasn’t started working on it yet):

  • From your cart: Click Instructions (website) or tap Add a note (app) on the item.
  • From the checkout page: Select Review to review items and add instructions.
  • After you order: Visit the Order page to review your items and add any instructions.

Your item instructions will carry over to future orders. If your item preferences change, review your notes when ordering the same items in the future.

Preference instructions

When you add certain items to your cart, a preference window will appear. This window lets you customize your preferences so that your shopper can pick those items more accurately for you. Some examples:

  • Set the ripeness level for avocados and bananas
  • Set the slice thickness for deli meats

You can either choose your preference right away or dismiss it. Once chosen, these preferences are saved to your account for future orders, and you’ll be prompted to review them every 30 days. Note that updating a preference removes any previous shopper note for that item, but you can still add a new item note at any time.

Shoppers will see your preference clearly while shopping to help meet your request. You’ll receive an automated update if your preference can or can’t be met.

To set a preference:

  1. Select an eligible item (such as bananas or avocados).
  2. Tap Edit preference under Item instructions.
  3. Choose your option (for example: not ripe, almost ripe, ripe).
  4. Select Confirm.
Replacement instructions

When an item is unavailable, you have three replacement options:

  • Best match: Your shopper chooses the best possible replacement. They’ll also use replacements you’ve approved on past orders.
  • Specific item: You can search the store’s catalog or select a replacement from a list of suggestions.
  • Refund: Refund: If you prefer not to replace an item, get a refund. Your shopper may still suggest a replacement — if they do, you’ll get a notification to approve or decline it.

Your replacement instructions will carry over to future orders.

Replace items in-app:

  1. In your cart, tap Add Instructions on the item.
  2. Tap Choose replacement.
  3. Tap Replace with specific item, Replace with best match, or Refund this item.

Replace items on the website:

  1. In your cart, select Choose replacement on the item.
  2. Select Replace with specific item, Replace with Best match, or Refund this item.

We’ll refund or charge you for the difference in price for replacement items.

If you’ve turned your order notifications on, look for alerts when your shopper replaces or refunds an item.

Checking another store for your out-of-stock items

We know how important it is to get every item you ordered. We may check another store for any refunded out-of-stock items. If the out-of-stock items are at another store and a shopper is available, we’ll let you know we’re working on a new order. This only applies to delivery orders and has some restrictions:

  • No alcohol items

  • No Rx items

  • No gift orders

You can’t reschedule a new order, but you can add items or cancel on the order page. You can cancel a new order without being charged a cancellation fee. Delivery instructions from the original order carry over. You’ll also get order notifications if you’ve enabled them.

The new order creates another authorization hold for the out-of-stock items only. (Adding items to the new order might create additional authorization holds.) The new order includes a portion of the service fee and tip from the original order.

The second shopper will visit a different location of the same retailer. While item prices are based on the original order, please note that the same coupons, discounts, and promotions (e.g., BOGO, order minimum) may not apply.

Age-restricted items policies

In select areas, Instacart delivers the following age-restricted items—

  • Alcohol
  • Hemp-based CBD/THC
  • Over-the-counter (OTC) products
  • Spray paint
  • Prescription medication (Rx)

ID verification is required during delivery. Some states may require a signature for these products. The age required for each product differs between product and state. Check the app or website to see availability in your location.

Valid forms of ID

We accept these IDs for alcohol, OTC, and spray paint deliveries—

  • U.S. driver’s license with photo
  • U.S. military ID
  • State-issued ID card
  • U.S. passport*
  • Green card*

*Green cards and Passports are not accepted from Costco or Walmart.

Alcohol policies
Ordering alcohol
  • You must be 21 or older to order alcohol.
  • Only alcohol items can replace other alcohol.
  • Alcohol can’t be added as a special request.
  • Some states limit how much alcohol you can order. If you can’t complete your order, try reducing the quantity of alcohol items in your cart.
Alcohol delivery
  • Alcohol orders can’t exceed 400 pounds.
  • Shoppers verify IDs by scanning or manually entering details at delivery.* They do not store your information.
  • Alcohol can’t be delivered to visibly intoxicated persons, minors, or those believed to be ordering alcohol for minors. Shoppers will refund the alcohol and return it to the store.
  • Shoppers deliver alcohol directly to recipients and can’t leave it unattended. Delivery fees, tips, or bottle deposit fees may still apply.

*Manual ID entry isn’t available in California, Costco, or Walmart orders.

Delivery to non-retail businesses, such as office buildings, is allowed. However, Instacart does NOT deliver alcohol to the following—

  • Restaurants
  • Storage facilities
  • Locker mailbox or package shipping facilities
  • Correctional facilities (prisons, reformatories, etc.)
  • Retail businesses that sell alcohol (ex. liquor stores, bars, convenience stores, grocery stores)
  • College campuses (4 year, 2 year, or trade schools) or official campus housing
  • Medical facilities (Addiction/rehab/substance abuse centers, mental health facilities, detox centers, etc.)

Delivery to the listed locations may violate laws, regulations, and Instacart’s Terms and Conditions.

Damaged alcohol items

Instacart provides credits or refunds for damaged alcohol due to—

  • Cracked or shattered glass
  • Broken seals
  • Missing or tampered labels
  • Poor bagging

Contact Instacart Customer Experience for damaged items.

CBD/THC delivery

Instacart delivers hemp-based CBD/THC products for select retailers and markets. These items have legal restrictions.

  • You must be 21 or older to receive the item(s)
  • Provide a valid ID showing your birthdate
  • Not be intoxicated
  • Not purchase the items for minors
Age-restricted OTC policies

For age-restricted OTC items you must be 18 or older and show an approved ID at delivery. Shoppers will verify your ID by scanning or entering details manually.

Diet pills and diet supplements

In New York, Colorado, and other select states, you must be 18 or older to order diet pills or supplements for weight loss or muscle building. Approved ID and a signature are required at delivery.

Spray paint policies

To order spray paint, you must—

  • Be 18 or 21 years old, depending on where you live.

  • Show an approved ID at delivery.

  • Let the shopper verify your ID by scanning or entering details manually.

Spray paint may not be available in all areas. Check the app or website for availability.

Community Carts

Community Carts are shoppable lists created to support charitable organizations and others helping those in need. You can easily shop these lists in the Instacart app or on the Instacart website to donate items directly to a food bank or other recipient.

Access Community Carts—

  • In the Instacart app on the home screen or in the Community Carts hub (instacart.com/donate)
  • Through app notifications
  • On the Instacart website
  • Other websites or social media posts when shared by others

You can donate as many or as few items as you want, but there’s a $10 order minimum. Community Carts have no delivery or service fees, and you can tip your personal shopper. Your shopper delivers your items to the food bank within 5 days. Shoppers can message you if a replacement is needed.

You can shop a Community Cart as a guest but need to sign in or create a new account at checkout before placing the order.

For Feeding America food bank donations, please reach out to the food bank you donated to for any questions about tax-deductible donations. You can use your online receipt as proof of purchase and participation.****

Community Carts Terms & Conditions for Feeding America

Starting November 28, 2022, customers can shop certain lists created by participating Feeding America member food banks (“Community Carts”) and purchase items to donate directly to a food bank. Customers can access Community Carts on the Instacart App for iOS or on Instacart.com. A retailer will be automatically selected for each Community Cart order. There is a $10 order minimum before taxes, fees and tips. You can only use a valid credit card, available credits or an Instacart gift card as payment for a Community Carts order. No delivery or service fee applies to Community Cart orders. Other fees may apply. If an item is out of stock, your shopper will select a replacement. Donated items will be delivered directly to the selected food bank’s address within 5 days. You will receive a receipt detailing the items donated once delivery is completed. Gift acknowledgment receipts must be requested directly from the food bank, which may require you to provide your order receipt to verify the order. Available promotions may automatically apply to your Community Carts order. To participate, you must have a valid account on Instacart.com with a valid form of accepted payment on file. Instacart reserves the right to modify or cancel Community Carts at any time. Instacart is not a retailer or seller. Instacart is not a registered 501(c)(3).

Express

Express orders let you get common convenience items, like snacks and essentials, with a faster delivery time from the stores you already shop at. These deliveries can happen in as fast as 30 minutes and are often available late night.

For the best deal, we suggest keeping an order placed with a virtual convenience retailer (ex. Kroger Delivery Now) under $35. If your order goes over $35, we recommend placing the order through your usual storefront (ex. Kroger).

Look for the Express category at the top of the Instacart website or app homepage. Stores in the Express category include traditional convenience stores (like 7-Eleven), pharmacy stores (like Walgreens), and convenience versions of grocery stores (i.e. Kroger, Publix, etc.) with smaller Express-specific product offerings.

Fee breakdown

Convenience orders under $35 currently have a waived priority fee, except during periods of increased demand. Orders must be over $10.

Learn more about Instacart fees and taxes.

Family carts

Customers can invite family or friends to build a cart together. Family carts are not available on Groceries Powered by Instacart orders.

Set up a family cart

Before you start shopping, create a family account.

If you’re new to Instacart, you can create a family account during signup. Click Invite, copy the invite link, and send it directly to your friends and family.

If you already have an Instacart account, follow the steps below.

In-app—

  1. Log in to your Instacart account.
  2. Tap the Account icon
  3. Tap Copy link to send the invite link directly to your friends and family.

On the website—

  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Click Start a family account.
  3. Click Copy link to send the invite link directly to your friends and family.
Join a family cart

Got a link to join a family cart? Select Accept invite, log in to your Instacart account or create an account, and start shopping the family cart.

Switch between a family cart and personal cart
  1. Select the View cart icon in the bottom middle of the screen.
  2. Select the 3 dots in the top right corner of the cart screen.
  3. Choose Switch to a family cart or Switch to a personal cart.

Make sure you’re in a family cart to view the items a family member added. You can’t view each other’s items from a personal cart.

You can each shop individually if you prefer. Items in a personal cart don’t add to a family cart.

Check out with a family cart

Anyone with access to the family cart can check out and place the order using their account payment method. However, cart members can’t split payment.

Everyone in the family cart gets a notification when someone checks out the order. Everyone gets order updates, but only the person who checked out the order can select replacements, edit the existing order, and chat with the shopper.

NOTE: If you deactivate your family account before completing a family cart order or switching to a personal cart, you lose all of the items in your family cart.

Groceries Powered by Instacart

Instacart has partnered with Grubhub to offer grocery delivery services to customers on Grubhub and Seamless. Instacart has also partnered with Airbnb to allow guests to order groceries on Instacart through the Airbnb app and website during their Airbnb stay in select cities.

Customers can order grocery delivery directly through the Airbnb, Grubhub, and Seamless apps or websites by selecting Grocery. After placing your order, an Instacart shopper fulfills and delivers it.

Visit the How to place an order help center article to learn more about placing orders.

For guests staying in Airbnbs, you can order grocery delivery in the following cities—

  • Phoenix, Arizona
  • Los Angeles, California
  • San Diego, California
  • Atlanta, Georgia
  • Orlando, Florida
  • Nashville, Tennessee
  • Austin, Texas
  • Houston, Texas

Using the Airbnb app or website, guests can order groceries and select from two options—

  • Regulary delivery: You receive the order at your booked Airbnb after your arrival. You can place this order up to 3 weeks in advance of your stay.
    • You can also place an order while staying in your Airbnb, up to 1 hour prior to checking out.
  • Kitchen stocking: Airbnb Hosts receive and stock non-alcoholic orders before you arrive. You can place this order up to 3 days before your trip start date.
    • At this time, this feature is only available in Phoenix, Orlando, and Los Angeles.
Check order status

See your order status—

  1. Select the cart icon or order icon (bag) in the top right.
  2. Tap the in-progress order to see its status.

You’ll also get text messages with updates about your order! You’ll receive messages when—

  1. Shopping starts.
  2. Shopping is completed.
  3. Your order is on the way.
  4. Your order is delivered.

Each text message has a link so you can see more details about your order.

Help with an order

To get help during live/scheduled orders—

  1. Select the cart icon or order icon (bag) in the top right.
  2. On mobile, tap Help in the top right.
  3. On desktop, click Need help with something? on the bottom left underneath Receipt.

To get help with past orders—

  1. Select the Account icon in the You section in the bottom right.
  2. Select Help Center.
  3. Contact Instacart Customer Experience for additional help.

Read our Order changes and Cancel or reschedule your order articles for additional support.

Payment for Groceries Powered by Instacart orders

You can only use the following payment types for Instacart orders on Airbnb, Grubhub, and Seamless—

  • Debit
  • Credit
  • Apple Pay
  • EBT/SNAP (Grubhub orders only, for eligible items)

EBT is not yet available for Seamless or Airbnb Groceries Powered by Instacart orders.

All other payment types are not accepted (including but not limited to Google Pay, PayPal, and Venmo).

Eligible payment methods entered on your Grubhub or Seamless account will be automatically available for use on Groceries Powered by Instacart. If you add a payment method on Instacart, it will not automatically be added to your Grubhub or Seamless account.

You may need to submit a valid government-issued photo ID when using a new payment method for the first time. This is required to verify your identity and protect against fraudulent activity.

Receipts and refunds

Your order total might temporarily differ from the receipt if you’ve tipped for services. You can increase your tip up to 14 days after delivery or reduce your tip up to 2 hours after delivery. Read our Tipping article to learn more. Service fees, taxes, and other fees may apply.

To view your receipt or request a refund—

In-app—

  1. Tap the Account icon in the upper right corner > Orders
  2. Tap the order you want to review
  3. Tap Receipt

On the website—

  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner
  2. Click Your orders
  3. Click View order detail for the order you want to review
  4. At the top of the order details page, click View Receipt
Appeasements, credits, or gift cards

Appeasements, credits, or gift cards issued on Groceries Powered by Instacart will only work on that account.

Similarly, if you have an Instacart Marketplace account, credits, or gift cards issued to your Instacart account will only work on that account.

Appeasements, credits, and gift cards do not transfer between Groceries Powered by Instacart and Instacart Marketplace, and they will only work on the account to which they’re issued.

Instacart Marketplace offers may only be available on the Instacart app or Instacart.com. Check eligibility requirements to see if an offer or benefit is available on Groceries Powered by Instacart.

View your balances on the Instacart app—

  1. Tap the Account icon in the upper right corner
  2. Tap Credits, promos & gift cards

View your balances on the Instacart website—

  1. At the top left, click the 3 horizontal lines
  2. Click Account settings
  3. Click Credits, promos & gift cards

How to place an order

We make it easy to find items from your favorite retailers and have them delivered right to your door or conveniently picked up! For delivery, we choose the best store location for your order. For pickup, you can choose a specific store location that works best for you. Learn more about selecting a retailer.

Placing an order
  1. Find your items. Search for specific items or browse by department. We also make recommendations based on what you bought or what’s popular in your area. Can’t find an item? Search for 1 keyword (ex. watermelon instead of watermelon slices). You can also try searching for items before selecting a retailer.
  2. Add items to your cart. Spotted something you like? Add it to your cart by selecting the + / +Add / Add to Cart icons. Adjust the item quantity by using the + or - buttons. You can add items to your cart without signing up for an Instacart account, but you need an account to check out.
  3. Review your cart. You can check what’s in your cart by selecting the green cart icon. Look for the next available delivery time and how much you may need to add to reach any order minimums. You can also view your item total, adjust quantities, add item and replacement instructions, or remove items from your cart.
  4. Confirm your order details. When you’re ready to check out, select the green Go to checkout button. Confirm the delivery or pickup store address and instructions, delivery or pickup window, and payment method.** **If you have items in your cart from multiple stores, you must choose a separate delivery window for each store. Make sure you select the right order type! Once you place your order, you can’t switch a regular delivery to priority or convenience or vice versa.
  5. Check out. Place your order—you’ll get a notification that we’ve received it and when an Instacart shopper has started working on it. We don’t offer the ability to schedule or select a specific shopper, but you can always add item and delivery instructions or chat with your shopper!

Check out this step-by-step guide to create your account and place your first order.

Placing a restaurant order

For steps on placing a restaurant order in-app, check out our Restaurants Help Center article.

How to place an order through Airbnb

Using the Airbnb app or website, you can order groceries and select from two options—

  • Regular Delivery: You receive the order at your booked Airbnb after your arrival. You can place this order up to 3 weeks in advance of your stay.** **
    • You can also place an order while staying in your Airbnb, up to 1 hour prior to checking out.
  • Kitchen stocking: Airbnb Hosts receive and stock non-alcoholic orders before you arrive. You can place this order up to 3 days before your trip’s start date.
    • At this time, this feature is only available in Phoenix, Orlando, and Los Angeles.

Using the Airbnb app or website—

  1. Go to Trips.
  2. Select Order groceries > Continue to Instacart.
  3. Browse stores and add items to the cart.
  4. Pick a delivery time or choose Pre-stock the kitchen.
    1. If you select Pre-stock the kitchen, you can’t add alcohol items.

After placing your order, an Instacart shopper fulfills and delivers it.

Note, you can’t change the delivery address. It’s automatically set to your Airbnb rental address to make sure your groceries go to the right place.

How to build a cart using ChatGPT

You can now use ChatGPT to request meal ideas and recipes, or get help with building a cart on Instacart.

  1. Go to chatgpt.com or open the ChatGPT app.
  2. Go to Settings > Apps > search for Instacart and hit Connect.
  3. Approve or Deny the connection to Instacart and associated data sharing to create a delivery order. If you Approve, confirm the connection by selecting Continue to Instacart.
  4. Enter your Instacart login information and select Continue. You’ll receive a 6-digit code to the email you provided at login. Enter the code provided.
  5. Enter your question or request that relates to meal ideas, recipes or grocery shopping into ChatGPT.
    1. Examples include, but are not limited to—
      1. I want a recipe for chicken enchiladas with ingredients from @Instacart
      2. Create an @Instacart shopping list that includes eggs, milk, and bread.
      3. It’s Friday! I’d like to order pizza and ice cream from @Instacart.
  6. Once you approve Instacart, ChatGPT will ask you to choose your delivery address. If you don’t see your address listed, select the expand arrows icon.
    1. Note, you can only select from existing addresses added to your ChatGPT account when you created your account.
    2. If you need to add a new address, you will need to add the address to your Instacart account and place the order through Instacart’s app or website.
  7. ChatGPT will show a list of available retailers through Instacart. Enter a retailer’s name to start your order.
  8. ChatGPT will then create a cart with items related to your question or request. You can ask ChatGPT to add, remove, or change items.
  9. Select Checkout.
  10. Confirm your address and add an optional tip.
  11. Select Continue to Payment.
    1. Apple Pay and Google Pay are accepted as a form of payment. This functionality is available exclusively for Web and app.
    2. You can use the payment method linked to your ChatGPT account, or add a new payment method during checkout. Note, if you add a new payment it only saves to your ChatGPT account and not your Instacart account.
  12. Select Pay Instacart.
  13. ChatGPT will show your placed order. Select Track on Instacart to see you order’s status.
How to shop Instacart with Claude

You can now browse and build a cart from Instacart directly inside Claude until it’s time to pay.

  1. Open Claude and type a message that mentions Instacart. Examples include, but are not limited to—
    1. Find me chicken enchilada ingredients on Instacart.
    2. Create an Instacart shopping list that includes eggs, milk, and bread.
    3. Order pizza and ice cream from Instacart tonight.
  2. Claude will ask permission to connect to your Instacart account. Select Allow to continue.
  3. You’ll be taken to Instacart to sign in (or confirm your account if you’re already logged in). If you’re new to Instacart, enter your delivery address to get started.
  4. Choose a store from the list of available retailers near you. You can also change your delivery address to a previously saved address here if needed.
  5. Claude builds your cart based on your request. You can ask it to add, remove, or swap items, just type what you’d like to change.
  6. When you’re ready, select Checkout on Instacart. You’ll be redirected to Instacart to confirm your address, add an optional tip, and complete payment.
  7. You can then make order changes or track your delivery through Instacart.com or the Instacart app.
How to shop Instacart with Gemini

Gemini can help you build a grocery list for Instacart. You can only use this for delivery, not for picking up food yourself. After you make your list, you’ll finish paying in the Instacart app. This is only available to users in the US.

How to connect Instacart to Gemini:

There are two ways to tell Gemini you want to use Instacart:

  • Option A: Talk to Gemini
    1. Start a chat with Gemini and ask for groceries, making sure to say the word “Instacart”. For example, you could say: “Help me order groceries for mushroom risotto on Instacart,” or “Where can I get groceries delivered with Instacart?”
    2. A box will pop up asking to connect Instacart. Follow the steps to link your accounts.
  • Option B: Connect in the Settings
    1. Go to the place where Gemini keeps track of its “Connected Apps”.
    2. Find Instacart and follow the steps to connect it.
How to build your shopping list:

Once they are connected, you can tell Gemini what to put in your cart:

  1. Pick a store. If you didn’t say where to shop, a list of stores will show up. Choose the one you want.
  2. Ask for items. You can ask for one thing, a full recipe, or a long list. For example: “Add everything I need to make chicken piccata,” or “Add all these things: eggs, milk, and bread.”
  3. Change your cart. You can always ask Gemini to add, take away, or change the amount of any item.
  4. Look at choices. If you want to see different kinds of something, ask! For example: “Show me options for chips.”
  5. Check out! When your list is ready, press the Checkout button. This will send you to the Instacart app to finish paying and confirming your delivery details.
How to reorder a previous order

In-app—

  1. Tap the account icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Tap Orders at the top.
  3. Find the previous order you want to reorder.
    1. Select Add all to cart to add all items from that order to your cart, or
    2. Select the order and scroll to Reorder these items. Select Reorder.
  4. View your cart. You can remove any items you don’t want to reorder.
  5. Tap Go to checkout.
  6. Tap Place order.

On the website—

  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Click Your orders.
  3. Find the previous order you want to reorder.
    1. Select Add all items to cart to add all items from that order to your cart, or
    2. Click View order detail. Under Reorder these items, select Reorder.
  4. View your cart. You can remove any items you don’t want to reorder.
  5. Tap Go to checkout.
  6. Tap Place order.
Prohibited items

Please keep in mind that some items aren’t available on Instacart. Prohibited items include—

  • Beverage orders exceeding 400 pounds
  • Blank keys
  • Cash donations, including those often found at checkout (ex. St. Jude, United Way)
  • Currency
  • Fireworks, including sparklers
  • Frozen pet food
  • Guns and ammunition
  • Hazardous (HAZMAT) chemicals, including dry ice
  • Helium tanks
  • Hunting style knives and weapons (ex. cross bows)
  • Items containing controlled substances such as pseudoephedrine, ephedrine, phenylpropanolamine, opioids, and methamphetamine
  • Kratom
  • Live animals
  • Live feed animals, including mice and fish*
  • Lottery tickets, including scratch-off tickets
  • Microsoft Office software
  • Pet animals, including live hamsters and live fish
  • Photos
  • Printable coupons
  • Rug Doctor equipment
  • Self-defense tools, including pepper spray and pocket knives
  • Tobacco products, including e-cigarettes and vapes
  • Weapons and weapon supplies

*Live meal worms, crickets, feed, and other insects for pet food are allowed.

Item availability and terms are subject to change without notice. Retailers may have additional size, weight, or price restrictions on certain items. Alcohol is not available for all retailers and areas. Repeated attempts to order prohibited items could result in account deactivation.

Instacart recipes

We suggest recipes based on ingredients you’re already shopping for. When you select a recipe, you can easily order the ingredients.

Shopping for recipes

When viewing items, like chicken or spaghetti, you can browse Related Recipes for more ideas. Or, browse recipes by going to the Collections > Recipes tab in your account. If you find a recipe you like, you can buy the ingredients or favorite it for later.

In the shoppable ingredient list, you can click/tap the trash icon next to any items you don’t want or need. You can also substitute any item in the recipe by selecting the replacement (arrows) icon next to the item.

If a recipe ingredient is out of stock, select a replacement preference during checkout or after placing your order (before Instacart assigns a shopper).

You can also shop recipes from New York Times Cooking. From the Instacart app or website homepage, select the New York Times recipe to view the preparation instructions and easily add items to your cart. Learn more about Instacart+ and New York Times Cooking partnership.

Saving recipes

If you find a recipe you’re interested in and want to save it for later, click/tap the bookmark in the top right corner to save to your favorites.

  • Find favorited recipes in the Instacart app under Account > Collections > Saved recipes.
  • On the website, you’ll see your saved recipes on the sidebar of the home screen.
  • You can also filter your saved recipes by store.
Instacart recipes on TikTok

If you’re watching a food video on TikTok, there might be an Instacart recipe! Tap View recipe on Instacart at the bottom of the video to open the recipe in the Instacart app. Then you can view the preparation instructions and order all the ingredients.

Instacart recipes are currently available to all customers using the Instacart iOS app or Instacart.com. We’re working to expand availability to Android.

Military bases

If you live on a military base, or somewhere with heightened security, give detailed instructions on how to deliver your order.

For a smooth delivery, provide detailed delivery instructions when you check out. You can also chat directly with your delivery driver through the Instacart app.

Please give specific instructions on how to deliver the order, including—

  • Any information your driver needs to give your guardhouse or security gate when attempting the delivery
  • How to access a specific entrance or designated drop-off location they need to use, if applicable

Order catering packs and meal bundles on Instacart

Order catering packs and meal bundles on Instacart

Catering packs and meal bundles let you order a complete set of food items in one place. You’ll find packs on the product page for participating retailers.

How to order a pack
In the app
  1. Open the pack’s product page.
  2. Review the lead time shown at the top. You must order before the cutoff.
  3. Make your selections in each required section.
  4. If an option has a surcharge, you’ll see it listed next to that item.
  5. Once all required sections are complete, tap Add to cart.
On the website
  1. Open the pack’s product page.
  2. Review the lead time shown at the top.
  3. Make your selections in each required section.
  4. Select Add to cart once all required sections are complete.
  5. In some sections, you can choose the same option more than once.
About surcharges

Some choices within a pack cost more than the base price. These surcharges are shown on the product page and are included in your total at checkout.

If a pack is unavailable

A pack may appear unavailable if the items needed to fill a required section are out of stock at your selected store. We aren’t able to override pack availability. You can check back later or try a different store if available.

About replacements and refunds for packs

Packs are prepared and fulfilled as a complete bundle. We aren’t able to replace or refund individual items within a pack. If you have an issue with your pack order, please contact Instacart support.

Cancelling a pack order

Pack orders can’t be cancelled or rescheduled once the lead time has passed. If you receive your order and you’re not satisfied, contact Instacart support.

Ordering Little Big Meals

Little Big Meals from The Fresh Market are available through Instacart for curbside pickup. Each meal kit costs $20 and has everything you need to feed 4 people. The Fresh Market offers 1 kit a week, with new meals every Wednesday.

You can pick the parts of the meal kit you like in the Instacart app. Please note, some items may not be available. Shoppers will try to get exactly what you want, but may need to substitute items if something’s out of stock.

Pickup orders

Instacart Pickup gives you the option to order online, set a pickup time, and collect your order at the store. Here’s how it works—

  1. When your order is ready for pickup, you’ll get a text or app push notification on your phone, along with pickup instructions.
  2. When you get to the store, follow the instructions to let us know you arrived.
  3. Depending on the store, you’ll either pick up your order inside or wait while we bring your order out to your car.

Keep in mind, you can’t switch your order type between pickup and delivery after checkout. Instead, you can cancel your order and place a new one.

Pickup locations

Retailers who offer pickup will have Pickup under their name on the homepage of your account. To find the nearest pickup location—

  1. Select the retailer from which you’d like to place your order.
  2. If the retailer offers both delivery and pickup, select Pickup.
  3. Choose your preferred pickup location on the map.

Once you select your location, you can start shopping for your items!

Item availability

Many items available for delivery are also available for pickup.

You must provide a valid form of ID for orders with age-restricted items. Local laws may require you to sign for these items.

Age-restricted items include but aren’t limited to—

  • Alcohol
  • Spray paint
  • Over-the-counter (OTC) medications, supplements, and diet pills
  • Prescription (Rx) medication
Pickup order fees

Pickup orders from certain retailers have a “pickup fee” (equivalent to a delivery fee for pickup orders).

When to pick up

Your order will be ready to pick up at the time you requested when placing your order. Please follow the pickup instructions you received, and remember to bring your phone with you to get notifications about your order.

Sometimes your order might be ready to pick up early. If this happens, we’ll notify you, just in case you’d like to pick it up before your original order time. (Don’t worry—we’ll keep all your items temperature-controlled until you arrive.)

No need to stress if you’re late! As long as you arrive before the end of the latest pickup window, we’ll have your order ready to go. (Heads up—the latest pickup window may end before the store’s closing time.)

Parking to pick up

For most stores, there’s no need to go into the store to pick up your order—just park in one of the dedicated Pickup parking spots and wait while we bring your order out to your car.

At some select locations, you may need to pick up your order inside. However, we’ll always provide store-specific instructions for your order before you arrive.

Letting Someone Else Pick Up Your Order

Can’t go to the store yourself? You can pick another person to get your order for you. This person is called a delegate.

How to send the link to your helper

Look at your order page and find the “delegate card.” Send that link to the person who is helping you. They will get a link that shows where the order is.

What your helper will see

When they click the link, they will see a page with:

  • The name of the store and directions on how to get there
  • Their own phone number or email
  • A list of all the groceries in the order

If the link doesn’t work

If your helper has trouble with the link, it might be too old. They will need to sign in to an Instacart account to see the order.

Place an order from multiple stores

With Instacart, you can shop from multiple stores when placing an order. You’ll have separate carts for each store and can switch between stores while you’re shopping to find and add items.

Switching between stores

In the app—

  1. Tap the Store name at the top of the screen to see a list of available stores for your location.
  2. Tap the store name for the store you’d like to shop. You’ll arrive at the store’s landing page.
  3. Browse, search, and add items to your cart.

On a computer—

  1. Click Store at the top left of the page to see a list of available stores for your location.
  2. Click the name of the store you’d like to shop. You’ll arrive at the store’s landing page.
  3. Browse, search, and add items to your cart.
Ordering from multiple stores

If you add items from multiple stores to your cart, you’ll see items in your cart grouped by store. This creates a multiple-delivery order, with separate shoppers and delivery windows for each store.

  • Separate delivery fees and minimums apply for each store.
  • Any $0 delivery fee promos or credits require a $35 minimum per delivery. This minimum also applies for Instacart+ members.
  • You can rate each shopper separately, but you cannot leave separate tips. Instead, your tip automatically splits between all shoppers who deliver a portion of your order.

Place an order with ExtraCart

ExtraCart gives you 30 minutes to place an additional order with a different retailer with $0 delivery fee, and no order minimum after you place an order on Instacart.

ExtraCart is available on select orders and varies by location and retailer. When the ExtraCart offer is available, you will see messaging after your first order and at checkout for your second order.

How to place an ExtraCart order

  1. Place your original order as usual.

  2. If you’re eligible, the ExtraCart offer will appear in your Instacart app.

  3. You have 30 minutes from the moment your first order is placed to place an additional order at a different retailer

  4. Before the timer expires, add more items and place your second order to get:

  • $0 delivery fee (service fees apply; $35 minimum for Costco orders)

  • No order minimum

Instacart+ members: Your membership already includes $0 delivery fees. With ExtraCart, you have no order minimum, meaning you don’t need to add $10 to checkout.

A few things to know about your ExtraCart order:

  • It may be assigned to a different shopper than your original order, so delivery times may differ.

  • The offer applies to your second order only. Service fees and service fee minimums apply.

Frequently asked questions

Q: When does my 30-minute window start?

A: Your 30-minute window starts the moment your original order is placed, not when you open the ExtraCart offer. Place your ExtraCart before the timer runs out to get the benefit.

Q: Is there a delivery fee on my ExtraCart?

A: No delivery fee on your ExtraCart ($35 min for Costco orders). Service fees still apply.

Q: I’m an Instacart+ member. Do I get anything extra?

A: Yes. Since your membership already covers $0 delivery fees, ExtraCart gives you the no-order-minimum benefit, meaning you can check out without adding $10 to your cart.

Q: Will my ExtraCart order arrive at the same time as my original order?

A: Not necessarily. Your ExtraCart may be assigned to a different shopper and arrive at a different time than your original order.

Q: What happens if my order is cancelled?

A: You won’t get any ExtraCart benefits if you cancel your original order.

Q: Why don’t I see the ExtraCart offer after placing my order?

A: ExtraCart availability varies by location and retailer. If you don’t see the offer in your app after placing your order, ExtraCart isn’t available for that order.

ExtraCart Terms & Conditions

Eligibility. ExtraCart is available to Instacart customers after placing your eligible original delivery order. You are eligible for ExtraCart only if the offer is displayed to you in the Instacart app after placing your original delivery order. Availability may vary by location and retailer. May not be available on all orders.

Offer window. The 30-minute ExtraCart window begins at the time your original order is placed. Your ExtraCart order must be submitted and confirmed before the window expires to receive the offer benefits. Orders submitted after the window closes are not eligible.

Offer benefits. The ExtraCart order submitted through ExtraCart receives: (a) a $0 delivery fee; and (b) no order minimum. Instacart+ members who already receive $0 delivery as a membership benefit receive the no-order-minimum benefit on their ExtraCart order.

Fees. Service fees apply. Each delivery order — your original order and your ExtraCart order — is subject to its own separate service fees. The $0 delivery fee offer applies only to the delivery fee line on the ExtraCart order; it does not waive or combine service fees across orders. Regulatory response fees apply where applicable.

Shopper assignment and delivery. Your ExtraCart order may be fulfilled by a different shopper than your original order. Delivery timing for the ExtraCart order may vary.

Cancellation. You will not receive the offer benefit if your order is cancelled. You can always contact customer service for assistance with rescheduling an order.

General. ExtraCart is subject to Instacart’s Terms of Service. Instacart reserves the right to modify or discontinue ExtraCart at any time.

Prescription orders — Costco

Instacart offers prescription delivery from participating Costco pharmacies in the United States except in Arkansas, Maine, Montana, North Dakota, New Hampshire, North Dakota, West Virginia, and Wyoming.

Some states require customer signatures when delivering prescriptions.

Ordering prescriptions
  1. Switch your prescription to a participating Costco.
  2. Tap the link in the text message you’ll receive from the participating pharmacy.
  3. Add groceries and other household essentials to your order.
  4. Log in to your account or create one.
  5. Choose a delivery time and receive your prescription as fast as same day.
Text messages

Instacart doesn’t send prescription-related text messages to customers. Please contact your participating pharmacy for text message questions or issues.

Receiving prescriptions

Instacart shoppers are required to scan your ID when delivering your prescription. In addition, some states require the shopper to get a signature from the customer. If the shopper can’t scan your ID or manually input ID information, they must return the items. Contact Instacart Customer Experience for any questions.

Shoppers can’t leave prescriptions unattended. Someone 18 or over must be present to receive the order.

Adding more prescriptions

You can’t add prescriptions to an order once it’s placed.

Payment methods

You can use a credit or debit card during checkout for the cost of your prescription(s) and any groceries you order. We currently don’t accept FSA or HSA cards.

Authorization hold

Seeing an authorization hold on your bank account? Learn more about temporary authorization holds.

Cancel or reschedule prescription orders

You can cancel your prescription order before it gets assigned to a shopper. Contact Instacart Customer Experience through the app or website if the order is already assigned.

Substance abuse support

We’re dedicated to the safety of our customers. Please follow your doctor’s instructions when taking medications.

Prescription orders — Publix

Instacart offers prescription delivery from participating Publix pharmacies.

Some states require customer signatures when delivering prescriptions.

Ordering prescriptions

Order your prescription from a participating Publix pharmacy.

  1. Select Delivery for the prescription order and pay Publix directly for the prescription part of your order.
  2. You redirect to the Publix storefront with your cart pre-populated with a $0 prescription item. There may be an Rx service or delivery fee associated with your order.
  3. Add other items, if needed, and checkout.
  4. Log into or create an account.
  5. Choose a delivery time and receive your prescription as fast as the same-day.
Text messages

Instacart doesn’t send customers prescription-related text messages. Please contact your pharmacy for text message questions or issues.

Getting your prescription

Your Instacart shopper must scan your ID when delivering your prescription. Some states may also require your signature. If the shopper can’t scan your ID or manually input ID information, they must return the items. Contact Instacart Customer Experience for any questions.

​​Instacart doesn’t allow unattended prescriptions. Someone 18 or over must be present to receive the order.

Adding more prescriptions

You can’t add more prescriptions to the order after checkout. You have to place a separate order.

Payment methods

Payment for your prescription will be processed by Publix pharmacy. Contact the Publix pharmacy directly for acceptable payment methods for your prescription. You may pay for groceries added and delivery fees on Instacart with a credit or debit card. Instacart currently doesn’t accept FSA or HSA cards.

Authorization hold

Seeing an authorization hold on your bank account? Learn more about temporary authorization holds.

Cancel or reschedule prescription orders

You may cancel your prescription order before a shopper starts working on it. Contact Instacart Customer Experience through the app or website otherwise.

Prescription orders — Wegmans

Instacart offers prescription delivery from participating Wegmans pharmacies in the United States.

Some states require customer signatures when delivering prescriptions.

Ordering prescriptions
  1. Switch your prescription to a participating Wegmans pharmacy.
  2. Tap Add to cart in the text message you’ll receive from the participating Wegmans pharmacy.
  3. After tapping Add to cart, you will be redirected to the Instacart Marketplace.
  4. Tap Members sign in if you have an existing Instacart account or tap Non-members continue here.
  5. Tap Add groceries to this order or No, continue to checkout* (if selected, you cannot go back and add groceries)
    • Add groceries and other household essentials to your order if you tapped Add groceries to this order.
  6. Log in to your account or create one.
  7. Choose a delivery time and receive your prescription as fast as same day.

* If you select No, continue to checkout, you’ll immediately go to checkout and will need to pay a prescription delivery fee. Select Add groceries to this order to waive the prescription delivery fee.

Text messages

Instacart doesn’t send prescription-related text messages to customers. Please contact your participating pharmacy for text message questions or issues regarding the text you receive about your prescription delivery.

Receiving prescriptions

Instacart shoppers are required to scan your ID when delivering your prescription. In addition, some states require the shopper to get a signature from the customer. If the shopper can’t scan your ID or manually input ID information, they must return the items. Contact Instacart Customer Experience for any questions.

Shoppers can’t leave prescriptions unattended. You must be present to receive your prescription delivery. Shoppers will return the prescription to the pharmacy if you aren’t there at delivery.

Adding more prescriptions

You can’t add prescriptions to an order once it’s placed.

Payment methods

You can use a credit or debit card during checkout for the cost of your prescription(s) and any groceries you order. We currently don’t accept FSA or HSA cards.

Authorization hold

Seeing an authorization hold on your bank account? Learn more about temporary authorization holds.

Cancel or reschedule prescription orders

You can cancel your prescription order on your account before it gets assigned to a shopper. Contact Instacart Customer Experience through the app or website if the order is already assigned.

Substance abuse support

We’re dedicated to the safety of our customers. Please follow your doctor’s instructions when taking medications.

Restaurants

Instacart and Uber Eats partnered to provide restaurant delivery powered by Uber Eats to eligible customers. You can access this feature only in-app for now.

You can find more information about restaurant orders powered by Uber Eats in our help center.

Place a restaurant order

To place a restaurant order in-app—

  1. Tap Restaurants at the bottom of your screen. Here you can browse a large selection of restaurants in your area!
  2. Select the restaurant.
  3. Tap on the item and select Add to order.
  4. Tap View cart.
  5. Tap Go to checkout.
  6. Review your order and select Next.
  7. Select Place order.
Help with an order

If you have an issue with an order, you can get help through the Instacart app.

For scheduled and live orders—

  1. Tap Restaurants at the bottom of your screen.
  2. Tap the order at the top of your screen.
  3. Tap Help in the upper right corner.

For past orders—

  1. Tap Account at the bottom of your screen.
  2. Tap Orders in the upper left corner.
  3. Tap Restaurants at the top of your screen.
  4. You’ll see a list of past orders. Select the order you need help with.
  5. Tap Get Help under the order.
  6. Tap Past order to find a list of common order issues. Choose one to contact support.
  7. Tap Chat with us to start a chat with Uber Eats.
Switch between grocery and restaurant carts

To switch between grocery and restaurant carts—

  1. From the Instacart homepage, tap View cart at the bottom of your screen.
  2. Under Your carts, select Grocery or Restaurant.
Check order status

You can see your restaurant order status in the Instacart app—

  1. Tap the Account icon.
  2. Tap Orders in the upper left corner.
  3. Tap Restaurant orders at the top of your screen.
  4. Tap the in-progress restaurant order to see its status.
Receipts and refunds

Your order total might temporarily differ from the receipt if you’ve tipped for services. You won’t see tips on your receipt for up to 48 hours. You have up to 90 days post-delivery to add a tip if you’d like.

You’ll get a new receipt within 48 hours reflecting your order total with tip included.

To view your receipt or request a refund—

  1. Tap the Account icon.
  2. Tap Orders at the upper left corner of the screen.
  3. Tap Restaurant orders at the top of the screen.
  4. Select the order you want a receipt for.
  5. Tap View receipt.
Payment for Restaurant Orders

Uber processes payments for the Instacart restaurant experience powered by Uber Eats. Apple Pay and Google Pay are accepted as a payment method. You may need to submit a new payment method during checkout, even if you have an active payment method on the Instacart app.

If you update or add a new payment method for a restaurant order, it only applies to that order and not your grocery orders.

Instacart appeasements, credit back credits, gift cards, credit card pay with points integrations (e.g., Chase Pay with Points), EBT card via Instacart are not accepted for restaurant orders. Statement credits offered by credit card providers (e.g., Chase) or payment networks (e.g., Mastercard) are not available for restaurant orders placed via Instacart.

First restaurant order promotion

Instacart customers may receive $5 off their first restaurant order of $25 or more through the Instacart app (Instacart+ members may receive $10 off if they are an active member when they receive the promotion). This is a limited time offer.

Grocery promotion

Eligible customers could also receive the following when they spend $100 or more on non-restaurant orders—

  • Non-Instacart+ members get a $5 coupon for restaurant orders via the Instacart app.
  • Instacart+ members get a $10 coupon for restaurant orders via the Instacart app and the continued Instacart+ benefit of $0 delivery fee on eligible orders of $10 or more.

You’re only eligible for this offer if you see the offer banner before placing an eligible non-restaurant order.

You must manually activate the offer by tapping on it. You’ll see an offer banner on the Instacart homepage or receive a push notification. Tap on the banner or notification to redeem.

Coupon eligibility is based on the final total amount after delivery before any taxes, tips or fees. If your order total is less than $100 because of replacements, out-of-stock items, etc., your order isn’t eligible for the coupon.

Once redeemed and you meet the order requirements, you’ll receive the promotion. It applies automatically at checkout on your next restaurant order of $25 or more via the Instacart app, subject to additional terms.

The discount applies to the total purchase price for all items in your order, and excludes tips, taxes, service and other fees.

This is a limited time offer. For more information on the terms and conditions of this offer, go here.

Note: You can only use these coupons, and Uber Cash given through the restaurant experience on Instacart powered by Uber Eats, for restaurant orders placed via the Instacart app. Any promotions and Uber Cash through Uber Eats outside the Instacart app aren’t accepted.

Discount expiration

The discount expires. You can see the details of your individual offer in the Credits, promos, and gift cards section of your Instacart account once redeemed.

Instacart Mastercard - 2% cash back on Restaurants

You will earn 2% cash back on restaurant purchases powered by Uber Eats as you do on all other purchases at restaurants including takeout and eligible delivery services with your Instacart Mastercard®. You will continue to earn 5% cash back on other Instacart app and Instacart.com purchases.

You also still have the ability to use points at checkout on grocery orders. You will be unable to Pay with Points or use Instacart Credits towards restaurant purchases powered by Uber Eats.

See full program terms.

Saved and shopping lists

You can create two types of lists in Instacart:

  • Shopping list - Your personal list for weekly essentials
  • Saved lists - Reusable, shareable lists for your favorite items

You can make personal, shareable, and reusable shopping lists to easily buy your favorite items. Saved lists can be public or private, but both are shareable with friends and family.

Private lists are not searchable, not discoverable, and can only include one store. Public lists are searchable and can include multiple stores.

Shopping lists

The Shopping List helps you build your cart faster. You can add items in many ways.

If you have an iOS device, you can use Siri or Spotlight Shortcuts to add items directly to your Shopping list in the Instacart app. Just say “Siri, add [item] to Instacart” or search “Instacart” on your device and tap Add to list. Tap View list to open your list in the app.

Once you’re in the app, you can access your shopping list in 2 places—

Search bar In header

On the Shopping list screen, search for the items you want to add or choose an item from the Suggested list. Tap the green + icon to add the item to your cart.

You can also access your shopping list under: Account > Shopping List > Collections

Ways to add items to your shopping list

Take a Photo
Take a photo of your paper list, recipe, or pantry items.

What you can photograph:

  • Handwritten shopping lists
  • Printed recipes showing ingredients
  • Screenshots of recipes
  • Multiple pantry items you want to buy

How it works:

  1. Open your Shopping List
  2. Tap “Add by photo”
  3. Take a clear photo of your list or items
  4. The app reads your photo and adds items to your list

If the photo quality is poor:

The app will show: “No items found. Try again or add items another way.”
Tap “Retake photo” to try again with better lighting or a clearer view.

Copy and Paste Text

Paste your list from anywhere - notes, emails, texts, or websites.

How it works:
  1. Copy your list from any source
  2. Open your Shopping List
  3. Tap “Paste items”
  4. Paste your text
  5. The app reads your text and adds items to your list
Format tips:
  • You can list items on separate lines
  • You can separate items with commas
  • You can even paste items without any separators - the app figures it out!

Item limit: 200 items per paste

Some example formats that work:

[TABLE]

Import from Reminders App (iOS only)

Link your Apple Reminders to add items from your existing reminders.

How it works:
  1. Open your Shopping List
  2. Tap “Import from Reminders”
  3. Choose which reminders list to import
  4. Items from your reminders appear in your Shopping List

Please note: You will need to manually import reminders each time. The app doesn’t sync automatically yet.

Use Siri (iOS only)

Say “Siri” add [item] to Instacart to add items hands-free.

How it works:
  1. Say: “Hey Siri, add milk to Instacart”
  2. Siri confirms the item was added
  3. The item appears in your Shopping List
You can also:
  1. Search “Instacart” on your iPhone
  2. Tap “Add to list” in Spotlight
  3. Type or say the item name
  4. Tap “View list” to open the app
Type Items in the app

Search and add items one at a time directly in your Shopping List.

How it works:
  1. Open your Shopping List
  2. Search for items you want
  3. Tap the green + icon to add each item
  4. Or choose from the “Suggested” list
Add Items to Your Cart

Once your list is ready, add items to your cart in seconds.

How it works:
  1. Open your Shopping List
  2. Tap “Add all items to cart” to add everything at once
  3. Or tap the + next to each item to add items one by one
  4. Proceed to checkout
Family Account Sharing

Everyone in your family account can add items to the Shopping List.
You’ll see initials next to items to know who added what.

Two types:

Frequently asked questions

Q: What’s the difference between Shopping List and Saved Lists?

A: Shopping List is your quick, personal list for weekly shopping. Saved Lists are reusable collections you can share and use for recurring orders.

Q: Will the app sync with my Apple Reminders automatically?

A: Not yet. You need to manually import from Reminders for now. Auto-sync is coming in a future update.

Q: What if my photo is blurry or the app can’t read it?

A: The app will tell you “No items found. Try again or add items another way.” Tap “Retake image” and try with better lighting or a clearer photo.

Q: Can I take a photo of a recipe?

A: Yes! You can photograph printed recipes, screenshots of recipes, handwritten lists, or even pantry items you want to buy.

Send a gift through Instacart

Send a gift through Instacart in-app or on web—

  1. Enter the recipient’s address at the top of the homepage to find a store near them
  2. Select a store and add item(s) to your cart
  3. Select Make it a gift at checkout
  4. Enter your recipient’s name, phone number, and a personal message
  5. Select Save
  6. Select Place order

If you add your recipient’s mobile number at checkout, we’ll text them to let them know you sent a gift via Instacart and when they can expect it to arrive.

Some high-value items require a certified delivery. Recipients must sign for the delivery or have their ID scanned as proof of receipt.

Sending a gift order that contains alcohol? A valid ID must be shown during delivery. Shoppers can’t leave these orders unattended.

For more information about ordering on Instacart, visit How to place an order.

Receiving an Instacart order as a gift

If the sender includes your mobile number at checkout, we’ll send a text message with a link to your gift tracking page. The tracking page shows everything you need to know about your delivery and lets you take action if needed.

What you’ll see on the tracking page

Your tracking link opens a page with two sections:

  • Gift details — shows who sent the gift, your delivery details, the items in your order, and an option to reschedule your delivery window if needed
  • Digital gift card or message — displays the personal message your sender included

Rescheduling your delivery

You can reschedule your delivery using the option on the tracking page. Cancellation is not available through this link — if you need to cancel, please contact the person who sent you the gift.

If we don’t hear from you 2 days after sending the text message, or the sender didn’t provide your phone number, we’ll schedule the delivery based on the sender’s directions.

If your tracking link doesn’t work

The link may have expired or become invalid. If you’re having trouble opening it, log into your Instacart account to view the order.

Please note: If your gift contains alcohol, you must show a valid ID at delivery. Local laws may require you to provide a signature for age-restricted items.

The Wine Shop

The Wine Shop offers customers of legal age to order pre-determined wine packs through the Instacart app! This offering is currently available in these states/zones—

[TABLE]

How The Wine Shop works

Choose your 3, 6, or 12 pack option through the Instacart platform and place your order which will be shipped via FedEx (you will receive an email with tracking information from FedEx). Please note that the FedEx delivery person is required to check a valid ID upon delivery. The customer, or someone of legal age, must be present for the shipment to be delivered. The FedEx delivery requires the signature of someone 21 or older.

Delivery

FedEx delivery occurs 7 days a week, from 9am-8pm, with limited delivery on the weekends. In most cases, FedEx will attempt to deliver your package three times before returning it to the shipper. When a FedEx courier is unable to deliver the package, the driver will leave a tag on the door informing customers of the Air Waybill number and the next delivery attempt. Customers can also contact FedEx Customer Service at 800 FedEx at 800-463-3339 to specify an available date and time for delivery. You can also request FedEx to hold their shipment at any FedEx Service Centers for a self-pickup (click here to view a list of centers). FedEx can hold shipments at an official FedEx location for 5 days.

Track your delivery—

  1. Go to your Wine Shop order on Instacart
  2. Select Notifications
  3. The FedEx tracking number is displayed on the Notifications page that appears

Please note, Instacart doesn’t reschedule The Wine Shop orders because FedEx handles the delivery. Instacart doesn’t control FedEx shipping. Contact FedEx Customer Service at (800) 463-3339 for rescheduling questions or concerns.

Using Apple Intelligence to Shop on Instacart

Help Center › App & Account › Shopping Features

Using Apple Intelligence to Shop on Instacart

This feature requires iOS 18 or later and the latest Instacart app update.

If you have an iOS device, you can use Siri to shop on Instacart. This feature is available on iPhone, HomePod, and AirPods.

Supported Siri Voice Commands

You can use Siri by speaking on iPhone, HomePod, or AirPods.

Just say… What happens
“Hey Siri, shop Instacart” Opens a Buy It Again flow to add your usual items to cart
“Hey Siri, shop at [store name] on Instacart” Opens Buy It Again for that specific store
“Hey Siri, shop [product name] on Instacart” Adds the item to your cart (limited to your top 25 Buy It Again items — say the product name clearly)
“Hey Siri, open [product name] on Instacart” Opens the product page in the Instacart app

Type to Siri

If you prefer to type to Siri instead of speaking, you can enable Type to Siri in your device settings. It supports all the same Instacart voice phrases via text input.

  1. Go to Settings.
  2. Select Accessibility.
  3. Select Siri.
  4. Toggle on Type to Siri. When you press and hold the side button, Siri will allow you to type your request rather than speaking it.

Buy It Again Shortcut

Create a custom shortcut using the Shortcuts app on iPhone. This lets you quickly add your essentials to cart without using a voice command.

To create the shortcut:

  1. Open the Shortcuts app and tap the + icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Tap New Shortcut, then tap Rename and type Buy It Again.
  3. In the Search Actions section, type Instacart.
  4. Tap Shop “Buy It Again”.
  5. Add Quantity / Weight (lb), Product, and Store.
    Example: Add 2x Fresh Large Green Bell Peppers to Kroger Cart

To use the shortcut:

  1. Open the Shortcuts app.
  2. Tap Shop — your “Buy It Again” items will be automatically added to your cart.

Core Spotlight Search

Core Spotlight search is only available on iPhone.

Your recently purchased Instacart products now show up in your iPhone’s system search. To use it:

  1. Swipe down from the middle of your home screen to open Search.
  2. Type the name of a product you’ve previously purchased on Instacart.
  3. Tap the Instacart result to open the product directly in the app.
