Troubleshooting

Siri doesn’t respond to my Instacart commands

  • Check that your device is on iOS 18 or later: Settings > General > Software Update
  • Update the Instacart app in the App Store
  • Enable Siri for Instacart: Settings > Siri & Search > Instacart > turn on all options

My product wasn’t added to cart

  • This only works for your top 25 Buy It Again items
  • Say the product name as exactly as possible

Siri asked me to choose a store

This is expected — Siri prompts you when the same product was bought at more than one store.

Delivery

Authorization holds, recurring payments, and unknown charges

Authorization holds and adjusted totals

Instacart places temporary authorization holds on your card to cover potential changes to the total due to added items, replacements, and weighted items. You might see multiple authorization holds if you add items after placing an order on Instacart. Authorization hold amounts vary and may be higher than your order total. Banks typically release the authorization hold and update your final total after the order delivers or cancels. Depending on your payment method and financial institution, it can take additional time for the pending charge to drop from your account.

You could see more than 1 authorization hold on an order if adjustments made after checkout exceed the original authorization total amount, or if you place the order several days in advance of delivery and the original authorization hold expires prior to delivery.

Authorization holds and EBT SNAP

We hold 10% extra for weighted EBT eligible items (ex. produce and deli items) in case we fulfill more than requested. Authorization amounts never exceed approved EBT funding.

Why different final totals?

The total you see when you place an order on Instacart includes taxes, fees, and the estimated total. The final total could change because—

  • You got a refund for out-of-stock items
  • Your shopper replaced out-of-stock items with something of a different price
  • You added additional items after placing the order
  • You bought something like fruits or veggies and the actual weight is slightly different
  • If you increased a shopper tip after delivery
Recurring charges

These only actually occur if you renew an Instacart+ subscription.

If something looks off and doesn’t fit the descriptions above, reach out to us or your bank. If you contact us, we need to confirm some details to get started. We can sort it much more quickly than going through a bank dispute, which may freeze your account and take up to 90 days to resolve.

Cancel or reschedule your order

You can cancel an order before a shopper begins shopping or you can reschedule to a different date and time up to seven days from your original delivery date.

What to know before you start
  • To cancel for free: Cancel before a shopper has accepted your order. Canceling after a shopper accepted may result in a $15 cancellation fee.
  • To reschedule for free: Reschedule before shopping begins. You may be charged for any difference in delivery fees.
  • Custom orders: You can’t cancel or reschedule meal combos, catering orders, bundled holiday meals, cakes, deli sandwiches, or deli items (ex. sliced meat and cheese) within the 24-48 hour window, depending on the retailer.
Cancel an order

On the app—

  1. Tap the Account icon (head and shoulders symbol).
  2. Tap Orders.
  3. Select the order you want to cancel.
  4. Tap Help at the top of the order page, then tap Cancel order.
  5. Select a reason for canceling then tap Save.

On the website—

  1. Go to Instacart.com and click Your orders.
  2. Click View order detail on the order.
  3. Click Need help with something?
  4. Click** Cancel order**.
  5. Select a reason for canceling then Submit.

⚠️ Canceling after a shopper accepted your order may result in a $15 cancellation fee. If shopping has already started, select Get help at the bottom of any Help Center article to contact support.

Reschedule an order

You can reschedule your order up to seven days after the original scheduled delivery date, as long as shopping hasn’t started. There isn’t a fee for rescheduling, but there may be an added charge for any difference in delivery fees.

On the app—

  1. Tap the Account icon (head and shoulders symbol).
  2. Tap Orders.
  3. Select the order you want to reschedule.
  4. Tap Help at the top of the order page, then select Reschedule.
  5. Select a new date and time, then tap Reschedule.

On the website—

  1. Go to Instacart.com and select Your Orders.
  2. Select View order detail on the order.
  3. Select Need help with something?, then select Reschedule.
  4. Select a new date and time, then click Save changes.
If your order was unexpectedly canceled

Instacart may cancel or reschedule your order due to shopper availability, out-of-stock items, store hours, or severe weather. If this happens, you can:

  • Place a new order at a different retailer.
  • Order on a different day or time.
  • Switch between delivery and pickup by placing a new order. You can’t change the order type after checkout.
When you can’t cancel or reschedule

The following orders can’t be canceled or rescheduled within the retailer’s cutoff window, which is typically 24-48 hours before the scheduled time:

  • Meal combos
  • Catering orders
  • Bundled holiday meals
  • Cakes
  • Deli sandwiches and deli items (ex. sliced meat, cheese)

Check the retailer’s cutoff window at the time of placing your order.

When to contact support

Contact us if:

  • A shopper has already accepted your order and you need to cancel.
  • You’re trying to cancel a custom or deli order within the cutoff window.
  • Your order was unexpectedly canceled, and you need help placing a replacement.
  • You think you were charged an incorrect cancellation fee.

Certified deliveries

If you place an order that contains high-value items, it may require certified delivery. Certified deliveries require you to either sign for the delivery or have your ID scanned to certify that you have received the order. If you order something that requires a certified delivery, you’ll be notified when you place the order and again at the time of delivery.

Certified delivery details—

  • You must be present for the delivery and sign when prompted by your Instacart app.
  • If you’re unable to sign on your device, the shopper can scan your ID. If the shopper can’t scan your ID or manually input ID information, they must return the items. Contact Instacart Customer Experience for any questions.
  • High-value items vary by retailer and are subject to change.

If you cannot be present for a delivery for any reason, you will need to complete your purchase at a later date when you can be there to sign for the item(s). Failure to do so will result in cancellation of your order and a $15 late cancellation fee.

Retailers requiring certified delivery—

  • Bed Bath and Beyond
  • Best Buy
  • BJ’s
  • Costco
  • Christmas Tree Shop
  • Dick’s Sporting Goods
  • Disney Store
  • DTLR
  • Indigo
  • Finish Line
  • JD Sports
  • Kohl’s
  • Meijer
  • Michaels
  • Office Depot / Office Max
  • Pet Supermarket
  • Sephora
  • Spirit Halloween
  • Staples
  • Total Wine & More
  • Walmart
Item restrictions

Not all retailers have item restrictions. Some retailers don’t allow alcohol. You can verify item availability on the retailer’s site.

Unavailable items include—

  • Items that weigh more than 63 lbs
  • Items larger than 2 feet x 2 feet x 4 feet
  • Propane
  • Fireworks
  • Lumber
  • More than 10 1-gallon cans of paint
  • Items greater than $300

Rating your order experience

Rating your order is a great way to recognize your shopper for—

  • communicating proactively,
  • picking excellent replacements, and
  • going above and beyond.

We also appreciate hearing about your experience when it’s not what you expected. You have 14 days after your order is complete to share your feedback. Sharing this feedback helps your shopper improve their service.

How to rate your order

To rate your order in-app, follow the prompts in the rating pop-up after your order is complete. You can also select the order under Account > Orders and tap Rate.

To rate your order on the website—

  1. Go to Your orders.
  2. Click Rate & Tip on the order and follow the prompts.

Include details about your service that affected your experience.

Considerations for rating your order

If you’re not satisfied with your experience, here are a few areas where your feedback is helpful—

  • Responsiveness: Let us know if you felt that communications with your shopper could have been better.
  • Order and delivery instructions: You may put notes on your order for selecting certain items and replacements, or putting them in a particular place when they’re delivered. We understand it’s important to you for your shopper to follow these instructions, and we want to know when this doesn’t happen.
  • Professionalism and courteousness: If you have concerns about how your shopper handled an interaction, including discussions about tipping, your feedback is essential.

Your feedback is valuable and helps shoppers provide the best possible service.

Thanks for taking the time to help shoppers improve!

Receiving your delivery

After your shopper finishes shopping for your items, they’ll start the delivery process.

To make sure you get your delivery as expected, we recommend—

  • Turning on notifications for the Instacart app,
  • Keeping an eye out for text messages and phone calls from your shopper, and
  • Leaving helpful instructions for parking, gate codes, or other tips to find your home.

Some orders may require you to share a 4-digit PIN with your shopper before they can deliver it.

Shopper can’t find your location

If the shopper can’t find your location, they’ll contact you via text or phone call. Shoppers wait 10 minutes before leaving the order at your door. However, they can’t leave orders with alcohol, prescriptions, or certified delivery items unattended.

We may cancel the order if the shopper can’t find or contact you.

Issues receiving delivery

If you can’t receive your delivery in person, ask a friend or neighbor to get it for you. Just let the shopper know by adding a note in the delivery instructions or sending them a message.

Alternatively, you can ask them to leave the order unattended in a designated spot. We recommend adding the specific location you want them to leave your order in the instructions to ensure your delivery goes smoothly.

If your order is marked as delivered but you can’t find it, you can still chat with your shopper for up to 20 minutes after delivery. Try chatting with them first before you reach out to us.

Wrong address on order

Place an order and realize too late that it’s headed to the wrong address? You’ll need to select Get Help at the bottom of any article in the Help Center for live chat.

Payment issues

All payments for your Instacart order occur in the Instacart app or website. External payments such as cash, cheks, or 3rd-party apps (Venmo, Zelle, etc.) aren’t necessary. You don’t need to reimburse your shopper for any reason.

Contact support if you have issues paying for your order.

Refunds

Refunds
Request a refund for grocery or other retailer orders

You can self-report any problems with your order within 3 days of your pickup or delivery, excluding restaurant orders, for a credit or refund.

Let us know as soon as possible if your order—

  • Is missing items that you were charged for on the final receipt
  • Contains damaged, spoiled, or otherwise unusable items

For missing or damaged items, we can issue a refund or credit towards a future order up to the amount you paid for an item. Instacart may issue a partial refund or credit for an item if only a portion of an item is missing or damaged. If the missing or damaged item is high-value/certified delivery, you can return it in-store at eligible retailers for a refund or store credit, depending on the store’s policies.

Instacart processes refunds immediately. Depending on your payment method, your financial institution may take additional time to make funds available in your account.

You can see any Instacart credits in the Credits, promos & gift cards section of your Instacart account. Credits will automatically apply during checkout on your next order.

Note: Refunds are subject to certain conditions.

Request a refund for restaurant orders

Go to our Restaurants help center article.

Missing refund for missing items or canceled orders

If you request a refund within 24 hours of receiving your order, you may receive a same-day refund. Same-day refunds don’t appear as separate line items in your bank or credit card statement. Instead, you’ll see an adjustment to the original charge.

For example, if you place a $50 order and receive a same-day refund of $10, the original $50 charge in your statement changes to $40. If you receive a same-day refund for the entire order total, the original charge disappears from your statement.

To check if you received a same-day refund—

  1. Open your digital receipt.
  2. Scroll to the Charges section at the bottom of your digital receipt.
    • Original Charge shows what Instacart charged you when your order completed.
    • Refund for order issue shows any refunds that Instacart processed after your order completed.
    • Total Charged shows your final order total, including any refunds applied after your order completed.
  3. Open your bank or credit card statement and find the original charge you received.
    • If you received a same-day refund for your entire order total, the original charge should no longer appear in your statement.
    • If you received a same-day refund for part of your order, the original charge in your statement should match the Total Charged from your digital receipt.

If you didn’t receive a same-day refund, please wait at least 24 hours for the charge to appear. If you used Pay-Pal or Klarna, the refund will be issued directly to those accounts. If you’re experiencing issues with those refunds, you can reach out to them directly—

  • To contact Klarna customer support online for more information at klarna.com/us/customer-service or by phone at (844) 552-7621.
  • To contact PayPal support online at paypal.com, by phone at (888) 221-1161, or through the PayPal app.
EBT SNAP and OTC Network/Medicare Card refunds

To request a refund for EBT SNAP-eligible or OTC Network/Medicare-eligible items, please contact Instacart Customer Experience instead of the retailer.

Only Instacart can process a refund for EBT SNAP and OTC Network/Medicare card online purchases, not the retailer. Instacart processes refunds for EBT SNAP-eligible items to your EBT card. Refund processing times for OTC Network/Medicare cards will vary depending on your plan.

Report a problem with your order

If your order arrived late, has missing items, or the wrong items, you can report the issue through the Instacart app or website within three days of pickup or delivery. Instacart can issue a credit or refund for eligible issues.

You can report issues like:

  • Missing items
  • Incorrect items
  • Expired, damaged, or spoiled items
  • Poor replacements
  • Late or delayed orders
  • Wrong order delivered
  • Order never arrive

If you received extra items or an entirely wrong order, keep, donate, or discard the items. You’re only charged for what you ordered.

Reporting an order issue

You have up to three days after pickup or delivery to self-report an issue. Report each issue separately.

In the app

  1. Tap the Account icon (head and shoulders symbol).

  2. Tap Orders.

  3. Tap Get help on the relevant order.

  4. Tap the issue you experienced. If you selected an item issue, choose the affected item(s).

  5. Select your preferred resolution.

On the website

  1. Go to Instacart.com and click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.

  2. Select Your orders.

  3. Click Report a problem on the relevant order.

  4. Select the issue you experienced. If you selected an item issue, choose the affected item(s).

  5. Select your preferred resolution.

After you submit your report, you’ll get an email confirmation for any credits or refunds applied to your account.

Instacart processes refunds immediately. Depending on your bank or payment method, it may take additional time for funds to appear in your account. Credits appear in the Credits, promos & gift cards section of your account and apply automatically at checkout on your next order.

Refund and credit details

Not all issues automatically qualify for a credit or refund. Here’s what to know:

  • Refund amount: Instacart may issue a refund or credit up to the amount you paid for an item. If only a portion of an item is missing or damaged, you may receive a partial refund or credit.
  • EBT SNAP orders: EBT orders are not eligible for credit. If you used EBT SNAP, you will only see a refund option. Contact Instacart Customer Experience directly for EBT refunds. Only Instacart, not the retailer, can process these.
  • In-store returns: Some items may require an in-store return for a refund. High-value or certified delivery items from eligible retailers can be returned in-store with your digital Instacart receipt.
  • Same-day refunds: If you report an issue within 24 hours of receiving your order, you may receive a same-day refund. Same-day refunds appear as an adjustment to the original charge, not as a separate line item.
  • PayPal and Klarna: Refunds for these payment methods are issued directly to those accounts. Contact PayPal or Klarna directly if you experience issues with these refunds.

Retailer return policies

Returns are subject to the store’s return policies, which vary by retailer and are not all listed below. Certain retailers may not accept Instacart customer returns. Only Instacart processes refunds for EBT SNAP purchases, not the retailer. Service fees are generally not refundable for returned items.

If your return is approved, your refund may be issued as:

  • A refund to your original payment method
  • An Instacart credit
  • Store credit (depending on the retailer)

Funds to your original payment method may vary depending on your Financial Institution.

Credits appear in the Credits, promos & gift cards section of your Instacart account and apply automatically at checkout on your next order.

Requirements for an in-store return

To complete a successful in-store return, you must meet all of the following requirements:

  • Return the item to a retailer that accepts in-store Instacart returns. Check the list below to confirm your retailer is eligible.
  • Make the return within the retailer’s return window. Return windows vary by retailer and are listed below.
  • Present your digital Instacart receipt at the store. You can find both in Your orders in the app or on the website.
  • Use the same payment card at the store that you used for your original Instacart order — refunds to your original payment method can only be processed if the card matches.
  • Bring a government-issued ID if required. Some retailers require ID for all returns.
Making a return to the store

To make a return:

  1. Open the Instacart app or website and go to Your orders.
  2. Find the order with the item you want to return and open your digital receipt. You’ll need this at the store.
  3. Check the retailer’s return policy before heading out, as policies vary by store (return window, refund form, and ID requirements differ by retailer).
  4. Head to the retailer’s store and go to the customer service desk or checkout counter.
  5. Show your digital Instacart receipt to a store associate and let them know you’re making an Instacart return.
  6. If your refund wasn’t processed automatically at the store, contact Instacart Customer Experience using the Get Help button below, and we’ll take care of the rest.

Not all items are eligible for in-store returns. Grocery items like fresh produce, deli items, and beverages cannot be returned in-store. For those items, contact Instacart Customer Experience directly. We’ll issue a credit or refund depending on the situation.

If you have a Beauty Insider account and entered it during your order, you can provide that to the cashier instead of a receipt. Sephora offers refunds for approved returns within 30 days of purchase to your original payment method.

Refunds for electronics, DVDs, video games, CDs, software, bedding, and mattresses are only eligible if the items are returned in their original sealed packaging and in saleable condition. Bedding and mattress returns must be processed by mail using a return shipping label; in-store returns are not accepted. Contact Customer Service to request a shipping label.

Return window: 30 days

  • Have the original accessories.
  • Are in new and resalable condition.
  • In the original packaging.
  • You also need to have your Instacart receipt.

After October 24, 2024, all sales become final.

Sports Basement

If at any time you don’t love your purchase, bring it back for a refund, credit or repair (Instacart proof of purchase required).

  • Ink and toner: Return unopened and unexpired products at any time .
  • Software: Return unopened boxed software that Staples currently sells for a full refund within 30 days. Open boxed software is not returnable or refundable.
  • Office supplies: Return at any time.

Safety incident reporting

Your safety always comes first. If you experience an issue related to your shopper, including intoxication or discrimination, food safety, personal injury, or property damage, you can report it directly on the Instacart app or website.

Our Trust & Safety team investigates all reports and will reach out to resolve your issue as quickly as possible.

To report a safety incident in real time on the app—

  1. Select Get help on the relevant order
  2. Select Report safety issue under Other issues
  3. Select the issue(s) you experienced and provide any additional information
  4. Select Submit

Instacart takes auto and non-auto claims seriously. To report a claim after an event has occurred, fill out one of the following— ****

  • Instacart Auto Liability Claim Form
  • Instacart Non-Auto Related Claim Form

If you have any item or order issues, please see this article.

If you feel unsafe or need emergency services, please dial 911.

Instacart Business

Business tax exemptions

Eligible Instacart Business customers may qualify to make tax-exempt purchases in certain states.

To view and manage tax exemptions on the website—

  1. Log in to your Instacart Business account.
  2. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left-hand corner.
  3. Select Business Settings under Manage your business.
  4. Select Tax exemptions at the top of the page, under Business Settings.

To view and manage tax exemptions in-app—

  1. Log in to your Instacart Business account.
  2. Tap the account icon at the top right corner.
  3. Tap Business Settings.
  4. Tap Tax exemptions.
Upload tax exemption certificate

You can upload tax exemption certificates through the tax exemption page on the website—

  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left-hand corner.
  2. Select Business Settings under Manage your business.
  3. Select Tax exemptions under Business Settings.
  4. Select Add form.

You can upload tax exemption certificates through the tax exemption page in-app—

  1. Tap the account icon at the top right corner.
  2. Tap Business Settings.
  3. Tap Tax exemptions.
  4. Tap Add form.

You’ll be directed to a third-party vendor portal that accepts new certificates. Complete all information and upload your certificate.

You can add 1 certificate per state. The certificate appears under the Certificates section of the Tax exemptions page immediately after uploading.

Note, certificates expire. You receive an email with reminders to upload a new certificate before the certificate expires.

If your state isn’t listed when adding a certificate

When you add a tax exemption form, you’ll choose a state from the portal. That list is controlled by which states currently support tax exemptions through our provider and configuration.

If your state doesn’t appear in the dropdown:

  • We don’t currently support tax‑exempt purchases for that state through Instacart Business.
  • You can still place business orders; they’ll simply be taxed according to local law.

We’re actively working to expand support for more states and retailers over time, especially in states where retailers need to opt in individually to enable tax exemptions.

Identifying tax-exempt retailers

Qualifying tax-exempt retailers appear as a tag on the Business home screen. Each individual Business account needs to upload their own tax certificate.

You can also filter for tax-exempt retailers from the home page—

  1. At the top of the home page, select Show all to see a full list of stores in your area.
  2. Scroll through the gray filters at the top and select Tax exemptions.

Note, if you have a tax form associated with your retailer loyalty card, this doesn’t automatically transfer over to Instacart.

If you need more help, select Tax exemptions under the support section of the Tax Exemptions page.

Instacart Business

Instacart Business makes it easy to get the supplies and groceries you need to keep your business running smoothly—delivered from your favorite retailers.

With a free business account, customers in Canada or the U.S. can track business purchases separately from personal purchases made on Instacart.

Instacart Business unlocks additional tools that aren’t available on standard Instacart accounts, such as:

  • Team management to invite coworkers with controls such as spend limits and order approvals.
  • Flexible, hassle-free payment options such as invoicing, credit cards, and more.
  • Simplified reporting and bookkeeping with bulk receipt exports, scheduled automated reports, and insights.

Instacart Business accounts aren’t available for Groceries Powered by Instacart users.

Sign up for an Instacart business account
  1. Go to https://www.instacart.com/business.
  2. Select Create free business account.
  3. Enter your business email address.
  4. Select Continue.
  5. Enter your 6-digit code sent to your business email address.
  6. Add your business name and type.
  7. Select Create business account.

After signing up, you gain access to free features and tools to help streamline and manage your business purchasing.

You can either import information from your existing personal profile to your new business account or use a business email address that isn’t attached to an existing Instacart profile to create a separate business account. Explore either option by following the Add Business Profile prompt.

Change your mind? Exit the sign-up process to switch back to the standard Instacart experience.

View Task Center

This centralized section in the Instacart app, lists a variety of tasks you can complete to get the most out of your business account.

Tasks include adding business details, signing up for Instacart+, and adding tax exemption forms. You can also manage your team members or refer other businesses. Instacart doesn’t offer these features in all locations.

Select Get started under each task and follow the prompts in order to complete and have the task automatically removed from your task center. If you don’t want to complete a task or don’t want it showing in your task center, select the x in the top right of the task to remove.

Refer another business

Get $40 when another eligible business you invite places their first order. For full referral program terms and conditions, see Referral Terms and visit Instacart Business Referral Program.

In-app—

  1. Login to your Instacart Business account.
  2. Tap the account icon in the upper right corner.
  3. Scroll down and tap Refer a business, earn credit.
  4. Send a link via email or text message to the business you want to refer. Alternatively, you may tap Shop QR code to share a scannable code in person.

On the website—

  1. Login to your Instacart Business account.
  2. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  3. Select Refer a business, earn credit.
  4. Send a link via email or text message to the business you want to refer.

Instacart+ for Business

Instacart+ offers all members $0 delivery fees on eligible orders of $10 or more. You must meet the $10 order minimum for each retailer and on each order through Instacart. Additional fees, taxes, and tips may apply.

Instacart Business accounts get these additional Instacart+ benefits—

  • Share Instacart+ benefits with up to 4 additional business members (benefits shared may vary). The Instacart+ membership must be purchased on an Instacart Business account.
  • Instacart Business Owners can use their Instacart+ membership on both their Business and personal accounts if they have them
  • Get a 2% credit back on eligible orders over $250

Note, Instacart+ memberships purchased on Instacart Business accounts don’t include Family Sharing through a personal account.

For more information on Instacart+ and its benefits, check out our Instacart+ benefits Help Center article.

Auto-assigning Instacart+

If your business has Instacart+ seats available to share, Instacart may automatically assign it to your most active team members as a one-time benefit. Activity is based on order volume over the past 12 months.

When this happens—

  • As the business Owner, you’ll receive an in-app notification and an email letting you know which team members received access.
  • Team members who receive access will get an email notification.

You can reassign or revoke any team member’s Instacart+ access at any time from the Team Members page. See Share Instacart+ benefits below for instructions.

Share Instacart+ benefits

Businesses can share Instacart+ benefits, including $0 delivery fees on eligible orders over $10, with up to 4 additional business members. The 2% credit back benefit on eligible orders over $250 applies to the buyer’s account.

The account owner must have the Instacart+ membership. If the owner has an Instacart+ membership on their personal account, they can transfer it to their Business account.

Shared Instacart+ benefits are subject to the Shared Instacart+ terms. Available benefits may vary, see Instacart+ benefits for more details.

Owners and Admins can assign Instacart+ access to existing or new team members.

To share Instacart+ benefits with an existing member on the website—

  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Select Team members.
  3. Select the member.
  4. Select Assign seat or Revoke access to update a member’s access.

To share Instacart+ benefits with an existing member in-app—

  1. Tap the account icon at the top right corner.
  2. Tap Team members.
  3. Tap the member.
  4. Tap Assign seat or Revoke access to update a member’s access.

Members will get an email when access is granted or removed.

To share Instacart+ benefits with a new member on the website—

  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Select Team members.
  3. Select Invite members.
  4. Enter the email address of the member(s) you want to invite.
    1. If multiple members, separate each email by a comma.
  5. After entering the members’ email addresses, enable the Assign seats toggle under the Instacart+ section.
  6. Select Send invites.

To share Instacart+ benefits with a new member in-app—

  1. Tap the account icon at the top right corner.
  2. Tap Team members.
  3. Tap Invite members at the bottom of the screen.
  4. Tap the role you’d like to assign.
  5. Enter the email address of the member(s) you want to invite.
    1. If multiple members, separate each email by a comma.
  6. After entering the members’ email addresses, enable the Assign seats toggle under the Instacart+ section.
  7. Tap Send invites.

Need more than 5 seats?

Business Instacart+ holders may share their subscription with up to 4 additional business members in their team in the base subscription. If you’d like to purchase additional seats or to learn more about other subscription tiers, contact business@instacart.com.

Transfer Owner’s membership to their Instacart Business account

Owners can transfer their Instacart+ membership from their personal to their Business account. Only Instacart+ memberships bought on the Instacart app or website are eligible for transfer.

To transfer the membership on the website—

  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Select Instacart+.
  3. Under Explore your benefits select Transfer membership.
  4. Add or select a payment method.
  5. Select Buy Now and complete the transfer.

Once transferred, the Instacart+ personal membership is canceled, your remaining days are added to the Instacart Business account, and you’re charged for a new one-year membership. The owner keeps the Instacart+ benefits on their personal account, except Family Sharing which isn’t available on Instacart Business.

For more information on creating an organization and switching a team member’s role, go to our Manage your business organization Help Center article.

2% Back on $250+ Orders

The 2% back credit on Instacart Business is an Instacart+ benefit for business accounts when users spend a minimum of $250 on a single eligible order. To be eligible, you need to:

  • Be using an Instacart Business account
  • Have an active Instacart+ membership tied to that account (or have the membership shared with you)

The 2% credit back benefit on eligible orders over $250 applies to the buyer’s account.

Once granted, the 2% is issued as Instacart credit on the account’s promo/credit balance, visible in promo/credit history.

Access your Credit Back on the website—

  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Select Credits, promos, and gift cards.
  3. View an itemized breakdown of your credits, available promotions, and credit history.

Access your Credit Back in-app—

  1. Tap the account icon at the top right corner.
  2. Tap Credits, promos, and gift cards.
  3. View an itemized breakdown of your credits, available promotions, and credit history.

Applied credit also appears on the checkout screen.

Invoices and billing for business account

Balance Payments Inc.

Balance powers Instacart’s Pay by Invoice program, enabling Instacart to offer deferred payment terms to customers.

As part of this program, Balance manages the invoicing application and process, and handles all aspects of payment collection.

There are no fees for participating in the program when you pay on time.

How it works

Pay by Invoice allows your business to defer payment until 30 days after receiving an invoice for purchases. This period begins on the order delivery date. Once the invoice is paid, the corresponding amount is restored to your available credit limit, allowing you to continue making purchases.

Apply for Pay by Invoice

To apply for Instacart’s Pay by Invoice program on the website—

  1. Log in to your business account.
  2. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper-left corner.
  3. Select Apply for invoicing.

To apply for Instacart’s Pay by Invoice program in-app—

  1. Log in to your business account.
  2. Tap the Account icon in the upper right corner.
  3. Tap Apply for Invoicing.

When applying for Pay by Invoice, you will choose the currency you are applying for, and the approved line of credit may only be used in that country. If you require credit in both USD and CAD, please contact business@instacart.com for information on applying for a second line of credit.

Applications may be approved instantly, however in some cases may take longer, typically completing within 3 business days. Once a decision is made, you’ll be notified via email or you can check the status in the Invoicing section of your business account. Approval notifications include instructions on how to activate Pay by Invoice for your account.

Once you submit your application, you can’t edit or change it. If you notice any errors or need to make updates, please email support@getbalance.com for assistance.

Using your credit

After your application is approved, all team members in your Business Organization on Instacart will have access to place orders using Pay by Invoice. Orders containing alcohol cannot currently use Pay by Invoice.

Individual invoices will be issued immediately after the order is completed.

Managing your credit

Administrators can monitor usage of credit, configure settings, and access the payment portal from the Instacart site and mobile application.

To monitor your credit usage and view your remaining credit limit on the website—

  1. Log in to your business account.
  2. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper-left corner.
  3. Select Invoicing under Manage your business.

To monitor your credit usage and view your remaining credit limit in-app—

  1. Log in to your business account.
  2. Tap the Account icon in the upper right corner.
  3. Tap Invoicing under Manage your business.

From the Invoicing page, click on the Billing Portal button to access the Payments site managed by Balance.

Your credit limit is determined using the information you provide, combined with external data sources. Balance analyzes this data to recommend a credit limit tailored to your business.

To increase a credit limit, ensure invoices are paid on time. Balance actively monitors payment history and automatically increases credit limits over time based on consistent, timely payments.

Invoicing Settings

Business administrators can manage settings related to Pay by Invoice.

To access settings on the website—

  1. Log in to your business account.
  2. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper-left corner.
  3. Select Invoicing under Manage your business.
  4. Click on Settings.

To access settings on the app—

  1. Log in to your business account.
  2. Tap the Account icon in the upper right corner.
  3. Tap Invoicing under Manage your business.
  4. Tap Settings.
Invoice Type

Individual invoices are issued for each order placed.

Consolidated monthly invoices cover orders from the first to last day of the month. This invoice is created on the first day of the following month. Payment terms will be Net-30 from the last day of the statement period.

You can select the frequency in which you receive invoices through your business account settings. You can also switch between individual invoices and consolidate monthly invoices at any time.

You can choose how often and how your invoices are generated—

  • Individual invoices (per order)
    Individual invoices are issued for each order placed. Each invoice is created shortly after the order is completed, with payment due according to your net terms (for example, Net 30 from the order delivery date).

  • Single monthly invoice
    Consolidated monthly invoices cover orders from the first to last day of the month. This invoice is created on the first day of the following month. Payment terms will be Net-30 from the last day of the statement period.

  • Monthly (grouped) invoices
    Multiple invoices are issued each month, grouped by a custom attribute you choose (for example, department, location, cost center, or another custom field).

    • All eligible orders placed between the first and last day of the month are included.

    • Invoices are created on the first day of the following month.

    • Payment terms will be Net 30 from the last day of the statement period.

You can select the frequency in which you receive invoices through your business account settings. You can also switch between invoice types at any time. Changes apply to new orders going forward; existing orders keep the invoice type that was active when the order was placed.

Notifications

Invoices are sent to the primary email associated with your Business Organization Owner’s account. You may list additional email addresses, such as your accounts payable team, who should receive copies of invoices and other notifications in your invoicing settings. The listed email addresses do not need to have Instacart accounts.

You may optionally enable the Send invoice to buyer setting which will include the team member on invoice emails for orders they place.

Your credit limit doesn’t expire.

Invoices and billing

If you’d like invoices sent to an additional email address, you can specify this during the activation of your Pay by Invoice account or in your Instacart Business notification center.

Payments

Invoices may be paid through Balance’s Billing Portal accessible via the Instacart website and app. In the United States, they may be paid via ACH Debit, and in Canada via Pre-Authorized Debits (PADs). Up to 20 invoices may be selected and paid for at once.

US Invoices may also be paid using ACH Credit via your financial institution. ACH account details are available on the invoice emails and PDFs you receive. You can’t pay invoices by check.

The invoice is due according to the specified net terms. For example, with Net 30 terms, the invoice is due 30 days after the invoice is issued.

Autopay and scheduled payments

You can manage payments for your invoices directly in Balance’s Billing Portal:

  • You can schedule one-time payments or enable autopay for your invoices.
  • When autopay or a scheduled payment is set up, your bank account is debited on the invoice due date.
  • If an invoice becomes past due, Balance may attempt to charge an alternate payment method on file to bring your account back into good standing.

To manage these settings—

  1. Log in to your business account.
  2. Go to Invoicing and select Billing Portal to open the Balance Payments site.
  3. From there, you can view open invoices, set up or update autopay, and manage payment methods.
Invoice

You can make the payment as soon as you receive the invoice. You can schedule payments in the Balance Buyer Portal which you can access in your business account settings or through the Balance invoice and collection emails.

You will receive an email notification from Balance Payments about upcoming payments. The email includes the invoice details and provides a link for submitting payment online or instructions for completing a bank transfer.

Refunds

Refunds for Instacart orders vary depending on whether your invoice is paid or not. If you haven’t paid your invoice, a refund will be issued as a Credit Note, which will reduce the amount owed on an outstanding invoice.

If you paid your invoice, Balance processes your refund via deposit to a bank account. Upon receiving a first refund for a paid invoice, you will receive an email requesting bank account details which will be used for that and subsequent refunds.

For questions about or issues with a refund, contact support@getbalance.com.

Post-Delivery Tips

As invoices are issued immediately after delivery, any changes to Tip amounts after receiving delivery will result in adjustments to the original invoice. For tip increases, an additional invoice will be issued to your business, due on the same day as the original invoice. The additional invoice will have the same Invoice ID, followed by an additional identifier, for example, if your order invoice is INV-74621 a first tip increase will have the ID INV-74621-01.

Tip decreases will be handled as a refund, either a credit note or deposit to your bank account, per the Refunds section above.

FAQs

Where can I learn more about all of the invoicing features available to me on Instacart Business?

You can learn more about the invoicing features here.

Can I put information on my invoice such as PO# or Cost Center?

Yes. Business administrators can add custom fields that appear on your invoices, such as:

  • Purchase order (PO) number
  • Cost center
  • Department or location
  • Other internal reference IDs

To set up custom invoice fields on the website—

  1. Log into your business account.
  2. Select the 3 horizontal lines on the upper left corner.
  3. Select Invoicing.
  4. Select Settings.
  5. Under Invoice details or Custom fields, add the fields you’d like to collect (for example, PO#, cost center, department).
  6. Save your changes.

To set up custom invoice fields in-app—

  1. Log into your business account.
  2. Tap the account icon on the upper right corner.
  3. Tap Invoicing.
  4. Tap Settings.
  5. Under Invoice details or Custom fields, add the fields you’d like to collect (for example, PO#, cost center, department).
  6. Save your changes.

Once custom fields are set up—

  • You can choose which custom fields appear on your invoices.
  • If you’re using Monthly or Monthly (grouped) invoices, you can choose which custom fields appear on your summary invoice.
  • If you’re using Monthly (grouped) invoices, you can choose a custom field (for example, department or cost center) to group orders into separate monthly invoices.

What is Balance’s role in this program?
Balance powers Instacart’s Pay with Invoice program, enabling Instacart to offer deferred payment terms to customers. As part of this program, Balance handles evaluating applications, issuing invoices and collecting payments.

What is Balance’s track record in B2B payments?
Established in 2020, Balance has a proven track record of supporting over 100,000 businesses with secure and seamless payment solutions. Balance works with enterprise businesses to streamline payment processes and enable the effective use of payment terms by their customers.

Manage business orders

View and export receipts

You can export your order receipt history to a PDF and CSVs.

In the app—

  1. Tap the Account icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Tap the Orders button at the top left corner, under your business name.
  3. Tap the download icon on the top right corner.
  4. Fill out the requested information (date range, user roles, files) and tap Export data. You’ll receive an email notification and the files of your choosing.
  5. Tap the relevant order.

Select the Receipt icon.

On the website—

  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner
  2. Click Your orders.
  3. Click Export on the top right corner.
  4. Fill out the requested information (date range, user roles, files) and click Export. You’ll receive an email notification and the files of your choosing.
  5. Click View order detail on the relevant order.
  6. Click View Receipt.

Admins can view and export receipt history for their organization as PDFs and CSVs in their team’s Dashboard.

Dashboard

In-app—

  1. Tap the Account icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Tap Dashboard.
  3. Tap the month from the drop down selection to see order history and receipts.
  4. Tap the Export icon in the top right corner to get PDF or CSV copies of your team’s receipts.
  5. You can download your receipts from the link sent to your email.

On the website—

  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Click Dashboard.
  3. Click the month from the drop down selection to see order history and receipts.
  4. Click Export in the top right corner toPDF or CSV copies of your team’s receipts.
  5. You can download your receipts from the link sent to your email.
Scheduled exports

Scheduled exports let admins set up recurring, automated report deliveries—so your team gets order data sent to email on a regular cadence without logging in manually each time. Access scheduled exports through Business Settings > Reports > Scheduled Exports tab.

Team member groups

Team member groups allow Owners and Admins to organize team members and manage certain settings at the group level instead of individually.

Each team member can belong to one group (or none). Buying policies apply only to Buyers within the group. Team members with Admin or Approver permissions retain access to administrate or approve orders across the entire organization, except as limited by specific Order Approval rules configured on individual groups.

Owners and Admins can:

  • Create and delete groups
  • Add or remove members (including in bulk)
    Set group-level Spend Limits
  • Set group-level Order Approval

If a team member is assigned to a group, spend limit and order approval settings are no longer available to be modified for the individual team member and past settings no longer apply.

Order guides

Order guides help you create a standardized list or catalog of food products, beverages, and supplies most commonly purchased by your company.

On the app—

You can create order guides through your Instacart Business account—

  1. Tap the Account icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Tap Order guides.
  3. Tap the + icon on the top right corner.
  4. Choose a store to see available items.
  5. Give your order guide a name and description. Select Next.
  6. You can then search and select items you want added to your order guide. You can also add more items later. Select Done.

On the website—

You can create order guides through your Instacart Business account—

  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Select Order guides.
  3. Select Create order guide on the top right corner.
  4. Choose a store to see available items.
  5. Give your order guide a name and description. Select Next.
  6. You can then search and select items you want added to your order guide. You can also add more items later. Select Done.

You’ll then see your order guide page. Here you can add and remove items, add and remove team members, and add all items to your cart.

If you want to add more items or remove an item in your guide, select Edit items.

To add items—

  • Search for the item you want to add.
  • Select the circle in the upper right of the item to add a green check mark.
  • Select Done.

To remove items—

  • Select the circle green check mark to the right of the item so it becomes blank.
  • Select Done.

To add team members—

  1. Select Manage members.
  2. Select Add team members.
  3. Select the box next to the team member you want to add.
  4. Select Add team members.

To remove team members—

  1. Select Manage members.
  2. Select the box next to the team member you want to remove.
  3. Select Remove team member.

Admins and owners of an Instacart Business account can create order guides, shop from the guides they created or that were shared with them, and share guides with individual team members within their organization.

Buyers can shop from order guides they created, or ones that were shared with them. They can’t share their order guides.

Troubleshooting Order Guides

To create order guides, your organization must have team members. If you see the error “Add team members. Invite team members to your business to get started with sharing order guides,” invite team members to proceed.

To invite others to join your organization on the website—

  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Select Team members.
  3. Select Invite members.
  4. Select the role and enter the email address of the member(s) you want to invite.
    1. If multiple members, separate each email by a comma.
  5. Select Send invites.

To invite others to join your organization in-app—

  1. Tap the account icon at the top right corner.
  2. Tap Team members.
  3. Tap Invite members.
  4. Select the role and enter the email address of the member(s) you want to invite.
    1. If multiple members, separate each email by a comma.
  5. Tap Send invites.
Spend limits

Spend limits allow businesses to set control limits on how much their buyers can spend across different time periods. These limits are set and updated by the account Admin(s), and can be set at a daily, weekly, and/or monthly level. You can also set a per-order limit.

This feature is available in the United States and Canada.

To set a spend limit on a buyers account on the website—

  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Click Team members.
  3. Click on the team member you want to set a spend limit for.
  4. Under Spend limits, select Edit next to United States or Canada, depending on the local currency.
  5. Enter the amount you’d like to set in the limit options (i.e., per-order, daily, weekly, or monthly). Leave blank if you don’t want to set a spend limit.
  6. Click Save.

To set a spend limit on a buyers account in-app—

  1. Tap the account icon at the top.
  2. Tap Team members.
  3. Tap the team member you want to set a spend limit for.
  4. Under Spend limits, tap either United States or Canada, depending on the local currency.
  5. Enter the amount you’d like to set in the limit options (i.e., per-order, daily, weekly, or monthly). Leave blank if you don’t want to set a spend limit.
  6. Tap Save.

You can also disable spending completely for a buyer which prevents them from placing orders in that country.

To disable spending on the website—

  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Click Team members.
  3. Click on the team member you want to set a spend limit for.
  4. Under Spend limits, select Edit next to United States or Canada, depending on the local currency.
  5. Toggle on Disable spending.
  6. Click Save.

To disable spending in-app—

  1. Tap the account icon at the top right corner.
  2. Tap Team members.
  3. Tap the team member you want to set a spend limit for.
  4. Under Spend limits, tap either United States or Canada, depending on the local currency.
  5. Toggle on Disable spending.
  6. Click Save.
Spend limits and team member groups

If your organization uses team member groups, Spend Limits can be set at the group level.

  • Group-level Spend Limits apply to all members of the group.
  • When a team member is assigned to a group with a Spend Limit, individual Spend Limit settings no longer apply.
  • To edit Spend Limits for a team member in a group, update the group settings or remove the user from the group.

To set a Spend Limit for a group on the website—

  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Click Team members
  3. Click Groups.
  4. Select the group.
  5. Click Permissions
  6. Under Spend limits, select Edit.
  7. Enter your limits and click Save.

Spend limit applies to the entire cost of the order (including taxes, fees, and tip). Changes to the order made after checkout by the Buyer are checked against the spend limit. If the shopper makes changes while fulfilling the order, it may go above the designated limit. The final order amount applies against the limit for subsequent orders. Also, orders may be above spend limit due to tip changes post delivery.

Spend limits apply to each team member individually and are not a shared limit across the members of the group.

Spend limits apply only to Buyers in the group, even if the group contains Owners, Admins or Approvers.

Order approvals

You can require members of your organization to submit an order for approval before the order is placed. Admins can approve orders but they can also designate a member of their organization to have the role of an Approver.

Approvers can submit their own orders without approval, and can also approve orders for selected team members. Orders subject to approval can’t have items added to the order after checkout, but may still have substitutions made based on the buyer’s preferences or by the shopper.

Admins and Approvers can view all pending orders in Order approvals through the account menu. Orders are sorted by those needing approval first.

To require order approval for an active team member on the website—

  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Click Team members.
  3. Click on the member you want to approve orders for.
  4. Under Order approvals, toggle on Enabled order approvals.

To require order approval for an existing team member in-app—

  1. Tap the account icon at the top.
  2. Tap Settings.
  3. Tap Team members.
  4. Tap the member you want to approve orders for.
  5. Under Order approvals, toggle on Enabled order approvals.

When this team member goes to place an order, they will see Submit for approval at checkout. The Approver will then receive an email and push notification notifying them that a new order needs approval. Within the email, the Approver can select Review order details for more information.

Once the Approver reviews the order, they can select Approve or Reject. If Reject is selected, the approver can enter a reason for rejection that the buyer can see. The Approver must review the order within a certain amount of time to avoid the order being cancelled.

The team member placing the order will receive a notification letting them know if their order was approved or denied. If approved, they will also see if there is a change to the delivery time of the order.

Order approvals and team member groups

If your organization uses team member groups, Order Approval can be managed at the group level.

  • Group-level Order Approval applies to all members of the group.
  • Available approvers include team members with the Owner, Admin, or Approver role.
  • Order Approval cannot be set individually for team members who are assigned to a group.
  • If a designated approver loses approval permissions, they’ll stop receiving approval requests.

You may configure who the approvers are for a given group in one of several ways—

  1. Approvers in my organization:
    1. When selected, all team members with permission to approve orders in your organization can approve orders for Buyers in the group.
    2. Use this when you do not want to limit who may approve orders.
  2. Approvers in this group:
    1. When selected, only the team members that have permission to approve orders of members of the group will be able to approve orders for Buyers in the group.
    2. Use this when your organization has placed the Buyers and Approvers in the same group.
      1. For example, if you have New York and San Francisco offices, and create corresponding groups, place the Buyers and Admins in the same group, and the New York Approvers can approve for the New York Buyers.
  3. Approvers in another group:
    1. When selected, you will select another group which contains the team members you want to approve for Buyers in this group.
    2. Use this when your organization has placed team members of different roles in different groups.
      1. For example, if you create a “East Coast Managers” group, and then separately “New York”, “Boston” and “Philadelphia” groups, you could designate the “East Coast Managers” group to approve for each of the other groups
  4. Specific approvers:
    1. When selected, you will select individual team members to be responsible for approving Buyers in this Group.
    2. Use this when there are specific individuals to approve, who may not be organized into a group.
      1. For example, if you have 2 store managers and 1 regional manager, and want the 1 regional manager to approve for 2 groups, and the store manager to approve only for their store, select the 2 specific team members responsible for approving for each group

Only the Approvers designated to approve for the group will receive notifications for approval, and see the orders in the pending list.

To require Order Approval for a group on the website—

  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Click Team members.
  3. Click Groups
  4. Select the group.
  5. Click Permissions
  6. Toggle on Require order approval.
  7. Select the approval mode, and necessary options.
  8. Save.

Order approval rules apply only to Buyers in the group, even if the group contains Owners, Admins or Approvers.

Custom fields

Admins can configure custom fields, such as, Purchase Order Number, Department, or Location, to collect additional information from your team at checkout. Collected information is available in Business Dashboard and Exports, allowing admins to better track orders made by their team.

Each custom field can be Free Form Text entered by the team member, or a pre-defined list determined by the Admin. The Admin can also determine which fields are optional or required to answer before checking out.

To set up a custom field on the website—

  1. Click the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Click Business settings.
  3. Click Custom fields.
  4. Click Add field.
  5. Enter the information you want to gather and click Done.
  6. You’ll then see the custom field(s) you created. You must click Save in order for the custom fields to show to your team members at checkout.

To set up a custom field in-app—

  1. Tap the account icon at the top right corner.
  2. Tap Business settings.
  3. Tap Custom fields.
  4. Tap Add field.
  5. Enter the information you want to gather and tap Done.
  6. You’ll then see the custom field(s) you created. You must tap Save in order for the custom fields to show to your team members at checkout.

Manage your Business account

You can update your Instacart Business account information in the Instacart app or on the website.

In-app—

  1. Tap the account icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Tap Settings.

On website—

  1. Click on the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Click on Your account settings.

If you’ve set up your business organization on Instacart Business, you can also update your business information such as business type, size, and Tax ID (EIN/BN).

In-app—

  1. Tap the account icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Tap Business settings.
  3. Tap General.
  4. Tap on any of the business information you’d like to update.

On website—

  1. Click on the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Click on Business settings.
  3. Click the edit button next to whatever business information you’d like to update.

Note: You may not edit or change your Business Name after the business owner sets it up. For special circumstances, reach out to business@instacart.com.

Switch between Business and personal account

When you open Instacart in-app or on the web, a pop-up screen asks whether you want to use your business or personal account.

To switch between profiles in-app—

  1. Tap the account icon in the upper right corner.
  2. Tap on the drop down arrow next to your business’s name at the top of the screen.
  3. Tap your personal account.

To switch between profiles on the website—

  1. Click on the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Click Switch to personal/ business.
Convert personal account to business account

You can now request to convert a personal account to a business account. To get started, contact Instacart Support by phone at 1-888-246-7822 or through the Help Center chat.

Good to know about converting accounts

If your personal account is converted to a business account, you:

  • Lose access to certain personal features, including:
    • Restaurant orders
    • Family accounts
    • EBT SNAP payment methods
  • Can request to switch that same account back to a personal account after conversion by contacting Instacart support.

You can have both a personal and business account with Instacart, but if you convert an existing personal account into a business account, that specific account:

  • Will be treated as a business account going forward
  • Will not regain access to personal‑only features (restaurant, family accounts, EBT SNAP) even if you later also use it for personal needs

If you’re unsure whether you should convert or instead create a separate business account, contact Instacart Support by phone at 1-888-246-7822 or through the Help Center chat and our team can help you choose the best setup for your needs.

Connect to a business account

Connecting to a business account lets you order through the business you work for. You’ll receive an email invitation from the account owner, and once connected, you can start placing orders.

Your new account connects to your company’s business account.

For information on creating an organization in your Business account and adding team members or transferring ownership, check out our Manage your business organization Help Center article.

Manage your business organization

An organization is made up of an invited group of Business profiles that can place orders for the business through Instacart.

Any business profile that isn’t already associated with an organization can create their own organization. When creating an organization, you get the Admin role by default. You must also specify an organization name.

Create an organization

To create an organization on the website—

  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Select Team members.
  3. Select Create next to Create an organization.

To create an organization in-app—

  1. Tap the account icon at the top right corner.
  2. Tap Team members.
  3. Tap Create next to Create an organization.
Invite team members to your organization

You can add team members to your organization to manage spend and receipts, and have visibility into your organization’s purchasing patterns. You can give everyone their own log in, but have the option to centralize billing with invoicing and share Instacart+ membership perks.

To invite others to join your organization on the website—

  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Select Team members.
  3. Select Invite members.
  4. Select the role and enter the email address of the member(s) you want to invite.
    • If multiple members, separate each email by a comma.
  5. Select Send invites.

To invite others to join your organization in-app—

  1. Tap the account icon at the top right corner.
  2. Tap Team members.
  3. Tap Invite members.
  4. Select the role and enter the email address of the member(s) you want to invite.
    1. If multiple members, separate each email by a comma.
  5. Tap Send invites.

Note, members must join an organization using the same email address provided to the person who created the organization. Members can’t be a part of more than 1 organization at a time.

Invites expire after 1 week. If you need to resend an invite on the website—

  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Select Team members.
  3. Select Pending.
  4. Select Resend next to the member you want to resend the invite to.

Invites expire after 1 week. If you need to resend an invite in-app—

  1. Tap the account icon at the top right corner.
  2. Tap Team members.
  3. Tap Pending.
  4. Tap the member you’d like to resend an invite to.
  5. Tap Resend invite.

At this time, you can’t share payment methods with invited team members.

Bulk invite team members

You can upload a spreadsheet (either a CSV or XLSX file) to invite multiple team members at once. Bulk invite is only available on the website.

To bulk invite team members on the website—

  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Select Team members.
  3. Select Bulk Invite.
  4. Download the template and fill in the required columns:
    • Email (required)
    • Role (required — Admin, Approver, or Buyer)
    • Group (optional)
  5. Upload the completed CSV or XLSX file.
  6. Select Send invites.

If your file has any errors, the entire upload will fail and no invites will be sent. If your upload fails—

  1. Download the error report shown on screen.
  2. Correct the flagged rows.
  3. Re-upload the corrected file and select Send invites.

Bulk invitations are available to all users, but upload limits vary based on your account status:

  • Verified domain: Your organization has confirmed ownership of its business email domain. You can invite up to 100 members in a single upload.
  • Unverified domain: Your business email domain hasn’t been confirmed yet. Bulk invites are still available, but uploads are limited to 25 members.
Switch team member’s role

A Buyer can place orders on behalf of your business. An Approver can approve orders placed by members of your team. An Admin can place orders on behalf of your business, manage members and view analytics.

You can change the role of a team member from Buyer to Approver or Admin or vice versa at any time on the website—

  1. Select the 3 horizontal lines in the upper left corner.
  2. Select Team members.
  3. Under Active, select the team member whose role you want to change.
  4. Select Change under the Role section..
  5. Select the role of Buyer, Approver, or Admin.
  6. Select Save.

You can change the role of a team member from Buyer to Approver or Admin or vice versa at any time in-app—

  1. Tap the account icon at the top right corner.
  2. Tap Team members.
  3. Under Active, tap the team member whose role you want to change.
  4. Tap the member’s current role under the Role section.
  5. Select the role of Buyer, Approver, or Admin.
  6. Tap Save.
Transfer ownership of Business account

If you need to change who owns the Business account on the website—

  1. Open the current owner’s Business account.
  2. Go to the Team Members page.
  3. Select the email of the team member that you want to be the new owner.
  4. Select Change under the Role section.
  5. Scroll down to Transfer Account ownership and select Transfer ownership.
  6. Select Confirm transfer.

If you need to change who owns the Business account in-app—

  1. Open the current owner’s Business account.
  2. Tap the account icon at the top right corner.
  3. Tap Team members.
  4. Tap the email of the team member that you want to be the new owner.
  5. Tap the member’s current role under the Role section.
  6. Scroll down to Transfer Account ownership and tap Transfer ownership.
  7. Tap Confirm transfer.

The previous and new owner will receive an email confirming the transfer of ownership.

Reactivate and Deactivate team members

If you’re an owner or admin on an Instacart Business account, you can deactivate and reactivate team members from the Team members page. Before you start:

  • You must be signed in with an Owner or Admin role on the business account.
  • Deactivated members:
    • Immediately lose access to your business organization.
    • Can’t place orders or manage settings for your business.

To deactivate a team member on the website—

  1. Open the current owner or admin’s Business account.
  2. Go to the Team Members page.
  3. Select the email of the team member that you want to deactivate.
  4. Select Deactivate member.

To deactivate a team member in-app—

  1. Tap the account icon at the top right corner.
  2. Tap Team members.
  3. Under Active, tap the team member that you want to deactivate.
  4. Tap Deactivate member.

To reactivate a team member on the website—

  1. Open the current owner or admin’s Business account.
  2. Go to the Team Members page.
  3. Select the Deactivated tab.
  4. Select the email of the team member that you want to reactivate.
  5. Select Reactivate member.

To reactivate a team member in-app—

  1. Tap the account icon at the top right corner.
  2. Tap Team Members.
  3. Tap the Deactivated tab.
  4. Under Deactivated, tap the team member that you want to reactivate.
  5. Tap Reactivate member.

How to use EBT SNAP

Check out with your EBT card

You can only use EBT as a payment method if you added the card to your account before placing your order. You can’t add a new EBT card to an order after you place it.

Note: If you’re ordering through Grubhub (Groceries Powered by Instacart), you can use your EBT card at checkout.

EBT is not yet available for Seamless or Airbnb Groceries Powered by Instacart orders.

To check out in-app or on the website—

  1. Select the green cart icon in the upper right corner
  2. Select Go to checkout
  3. Select your address, delivery instructions, delivery time, and contact information
  4. Select your debit or credit card and EBT card
    • You can select Change next to your payment method if you want to use a different card
  5. Confirm or edit the Delivery Tip*
  6. Select Change to adjust the amount applied to your EBT card under Your payment split. Then select Confirm.
  7. On the Place Order page, confirm all information is correct
    • You can see expected charges to EBT card and debit or credit card
  8. Select Place Order
  9. After placing your order, enter your EBT card pin to verify your purchase

*Note: Delivery tips charge to your credit or debit card. For more information, check out our Tipping article.

Charges to EBT card or credit/debit card

EBT cards only cover the cost of EBT-eligible items. For more information on EBT-eligible items and how to find them when shopping, check out our Find EBT-eligible items article.

You must add a credit or debit card to your Instacart account to cover the cost of delivery and service fees, bottle deposit fees, taxes, tips, and any non-EBT items.

Adjust charges between payment methods

Look under Your payment split at the bottom of the Place Order page to see the expected charges to each payment method. Select Change to adjust the charges.

You can also adjust charges after checking out, up until delivery. After delivery, you can no longer adjust your payment split. You can’t increase the amount charged to your EBT card if it exceeds the total amount of EBT-eligible items in your order.

In-app—

  1. Tap More at the top of the order confirmation page.
  2. Tap Adjust totals.
  3. Tap Charge more to EBT.
  4. Enter the amount you want to charge to your EBT card.
  5. Tap Charge more to EBT.
  6. Enter your EBT PIN and tap Submit.

On website—

  1. Click Manage order at the top of the order confirmation page.
  2. Click Charge more to EBT.
  3. Enter the amount you want to charge to your EBT card.
  4. Click Charge more to EBT.
  5. Enter your EBT PIN and select Submit
Add items after checkout

When adding items to an order after checkout, you’ll be prompted to select if you want to charge the items to your EBT card or to your back-up payment card.

If you select Add to EBT card, you’ll see your receipt with your current payment split and the balance of your EBT card. Select Charge more to EBT, then enter the amount you want to charge to your EBT card.

Changes to weighted items

You may see a by-weight item message under your EBT card at checkout. Your shopper may need to adjust the weight of items like meat or produce due to in-store availability.

If the new weight is higher than what you ordered, we charge the extra cost to your credit or debit card, or you can remove the item and get a refund.

Note: Items added after checkout or any changes to weighted items automatically charge to your credit or debit card. If the item is EBT-eligible, you can change this charge to your EBT card up until your order is delivered.

Item replacement instructions

After placing your order, you can select replacement items if the item you want is out of stock.

To avoid charges on your credit or debit card, replacements for EBT SNAP items must be—

  1. EBT-eligible
  2. Equal to or less than the value of the original item

If the EBT-eligible replacement costs more, the difference is charged to the credit or debit card you used at checkout. When searching for replacement items, look for SNAP under the price of each item to know if it is EBT-eligible.

Shoppers can replace EBT items with non-EBT items.

  • You can approve or refund non-EBT replacements before the shopper checks out.
  • If you approve, we charge your credit or debit card for the item.

Note: If you’re ordering through Grubhub or Seamless (Groceries Powered by Instacart), you can use your EBT card at checkout. EBT is not yet available for Airbnb Groceries Powered by Instacart orders.

EBT SNAP item restrictions for select states

In 2026, new rules restricting SNAP-eligible foods and beverages are coming into effect in some states. Item restrictions vary by state.

For the most up-to-date details on the specific restrictions and effective date in your state, visit the USDA website.

Retailers have a 90-day grace period to comply with these changes. Note, EBT SNAP item eligibility rules are also expected to change over time.

Want more information on shopping with your EBT card through Instacart?

  • Check out our list of participating EBT SNAP retailers and states available through Instacart.

  • You can also search for EBT-eligible items or update your Instacart account settings to see which items are eligible as you shop. For more information, check out our Find EBT-eligible items Help Center article.

  • If you don’t have EBT SNAP, you can use Instacart’s Supplemental Nutrition Assistance Program (SNAP) eligibility screener to find out if you may be eligible and find the relevant state resources to enroll.

EBT SNAP refunds and returns

To request a refund for EBT-eligible items or if you’d like to return an item purchased with an EBT card, please ask Instacart’s support team instead of the retailer. Only Instacart can process a refund for online EBT SNAP purchases, not the retailer.

EBT items are refunded and processed within the same day to your EBT account. Visit Refunds and returns for non-EBT items.

EBT SNAP update for Alabama customers

Starting January 22, 2026, the Alabama Department of Human Resources will block out-of-state and online EBT transactions.

While blocks are active, EBT cards cannot be used outside of Alabama or online. Note, pickup orders are treated as an online order so these transactions will also be blocked.

To make a purchase using your EBT card online or outside of Alabama, you must unlock your card in the ConnectEBT app, on the ConnectEBT website, or by calling the EBT Customer Service phone number at 1-800-997-8888. For additional information and instructions on how to unlock your card, follow any of these options—

  • ConnectEBT Mobile app
  • Connect EBT website
  • EBT Customer Service
  • ConnectEBT Card Lock Instructions

For up‑to‑date information, please visit the Alabama Department of Human Resources website.

EBT SNAP update for Mississippi customers

Starting June 11, 2026, the Mississippi Department of Human Services (MDHS) will automatically block online and out-of-state SNAP transactions.

While this is in effect, EBT SNAP cards cannot be used online or outside of Mississippi without unlocking your card. Note, pickup orders are treated as an online order so these transactions will also be blocked by default.

To make a purchase using your EBT SNAP card online or outside of Mississippi, you must unlock your card in the ConnectEBT mobile app (Android, Apple), the ConnectEBT online portal , or by calling the EBT Cardholder Assistance number at 866-512-5087. Click here for additional information and instructions on how to unlock your card.

For up‑to‑date information, please visit the Mississippi Department of Human Services website.

Starting May 13, 2026, the Ohio Department of Job and Family Services will automatically block out-of-state and online EBT transactions.

While this is in effect, EBT cards cannot be used outside of Ohio or online without unlocking your card. Note, pickup orders are treated as an online order so these transactions will also be blocked by default.

To make a purchase using your EBT card online or outside of Ohio, you must unlock your card in the ConnectEBT mobile app, on the ConnectEBT website (ConnectEBT.com), or by calling the EBT Customer Service phone number at 1-866-386-3071. Click here for additional information and instructions on how to unlock your card.

For up‑to‑date information, please visit the Ohio Department of Job and Family Services website.
